pūrvatrāgnyādiśruteḥ bhāktatvoktiprasaṅgādatra śraddhāśruteḥ bhāktatvoktiriti saṅgatiḥ | athādipadoktaṃ sādhanaṃ nānuṣṭheyamutānuṣṭheyamiti cintā | tadarthaṃ bhūtānāṃ jīvena saha gatirnāsti utāstīti tadarthaṃ tasminnetasminnagnau devāḥ śraddhāṃ juhvatīti upakrame śrutaśraddhāśabdaḥ kiṃ śraddhāmātrayuktaparaḥ uta śraddhayādbhiśca yuktajīvaparaḥ itisa| tadarthaṃ kiṃ upakramānurodhena "iti pañcamyāmāhutāvāpaḥ puruṣavacasaḥ'  ityupasaṃhāro neya uta viparīta iti | tadarthaṃ kimupakramaḥ prabala uta upasaṃhāra iti | ādye upakramasya yathāśrutārthatayā tadanurodhena upasaṃhārasya neyatvena bhūtagatau praśnottarasyāmānatvāt‌ bhūtānāṃ na sahagatiriti nānuṣṭheyaṃ sādhanamiti pūrvapakṣe phalam‌ | antye tu upasaṃhārasya tyātmakāp‌śabdānurodhena upakramasthaśraddhāśabdasya neyatvena praśnottarabalena bhūtānāṃ sahagatirityanuṣṭheyaṃ sādhanamiti siddhānte phalam‌ | pūrvapakṣastu | na bhūtānāṃ jīvena saha gatiḥ | "tasminnetasmin‌'  iti śrutau upakrāntasya śraddhayeṣyavataḥ śraddhāsahitasyaiva dvayākhye prathamāgnau chāndogye pañcame homaśravaṇena bhūtagateraśravaṇāt‌ anyathā bhūtānītyuktiprasaṅgāt‌ | na copasaṃhārasthāp‌śabdavirodhena śraddhāśabdaḥ tatpara iti yuktam‌ | asaṃjātavirodhitvena prathamopasthitatvena ca upakramasya prābalyāt‌ tadanurodhenaivopasaṃhārasya neyatvāditi | siddhāntastu | upasaṃhārasyāprāthamyepi upakramavyākhyānarūpatayā arthānusandhānataḥ prāthamyena prābalyāttadanurodhenaivopakramasya vyākhyeyatayā tatsthaśraddhāśabdasya ajahallakṣaṇayā śraddhayā tyātmakādbhiśca yuktajīvaparatvopapatteḥ asti bhūtānāṃ saha gatiriti | atra navīnoktaprathamaprābalyakhaṇḍanenopasaṃhāraprābalyavyutpādanaprapañcanaṃ tu gurupādakṛtopasaṃhāravijayādavaseyam‌ || 5 ||
|| oṃ aśrutatvāditi cenneṣyādikāriṇāṃ pratīteḥ oṃ || 6 ||
evamatītanayanirṇītāp‌śrutiprāṇagatirūpamānadvayākṣepeṇa iha śaṅkotthānādantarasaṅgatiḥ | pūrvatraivādyā cintā | tadarthaṃ prakṛtabhūtāni jīvena saha gacchantyuta na gacchantīti | tadarthaṃ prāguktārthikaśravaṇaprāṇagatirūpayuktī bhūtagatisādhake na bhavataḥ uta bhavata iti | tadarthaṃ tābhyāṃ kiṃ niyamena pratyakṣāśravaṇaṃ balavaduta viparītamiti | pūrvapakṣastu | praśnanirūpaṇayoḥ tyātmakāp‌gatyuktyā artādbhūtānāṃ gatiśravaṇādapi "agniṃ vāgapyeti' ityādivata bhūtāni gacchantīti niyamena pratyakṣāśravaṇasyaiva balavattvāt‌
prāṇagatirūpayukterapi viśeṣaśravaṇe satyeva vaidikārthasādhakatvāt‌ pratyakṣagatiśrutyabhāve tayorasādhakatvānna saha gacchanti bhūtānīti nānuṣṭheyaṃ sādhanamiti | siddhāntastu | kadannabhojanasyāpi bhojanatvavadārthikaśravaṇasyāpi śravaṇatvena yukteḥ prāguktadiśā vyāptiliṅgagrāhakapramāṇavattayā balavattayā śravaṇasāmānyābhāvapratibandhakatvena tayoḥ svayamarthasādhakatvāt‌ "atha hainaṃ yajamānaṃ kiṃ na jahāti bhūtānyeva bhūtaireva gacchati bhūtairbhu ‌kte bhūtairutpadyate bhūtaiścarati'  iti pratyakṣaśravaṇācca | atra śrutau yajamānapadaśravaṇe'pi karmakṛto'pi bhūtāviyogena tadanyeṣāṃ kaimutyasiddhatvāt‌ | bhūtāni jīvena saha gacchantyevetyanuṣṭheyaṃ sādhanamiti || 6 ||
|| oṃ bhāktaṃ vā'nātmavittvāttathāhi darśayati oṃ || 7 ||
pūrvatra kaimutyena sarvajīvānāṃ bhūtāviyogoktirayuktā | karmakṛto muktiśravaṇena tasyaiva bhūtāviyogābhāve kaimutyānavatārāditi śaṅkanātsaṅgatiḥ | atrāthādipadenoktaṃ vairāgyādimokṣasādhanaṃ mumukṣuṇā na kāryamuta kāryamiti cintā | tadarthaṃ "apāma somamamṛtā abhūma'  ityādau karmataḥ śrutamamṛtatvaṃ kiṃ mukhyamuta bhāktamiti | tadarthaṃ mukhyatve bādhakaṃ nāstyutāstīti | ādye anyato'pi muktilābhānna vairāgyādi kāryam‌ | antye tu kāryamiti phalaphalibhāvaḥ | pūrvapakṣastu | na vairāgyādisādhanaṃ kāryaṃ tena vināpyapāmetyādi śrutyā suśakena karmaṇaiva muktisiddheḥ | naca śrutāvamṛtatvaṃ gauṇaṃ bādhakābhāvāditi | siddhāntastu | "tamevaṃ vidvānamṛtaḥ'  ityādau ātmavida eva mukhyāmṛtatvaṃ anyasya netyuktyā niravakāśatacchativirodharūpabādhakena apāmetyādiśruteḥ gauṇārthatvāvaśyaṃbhāvena anyato muktyabhāvāt‌ kāryameva mumukṣuṇā vairāgyādi tatsādhanamiti | śrutergauṇārtatvaprakāraśca "amṛto vāva somapo bhavati yāvadindro yāvanmanuḥ' ityādi śrutyā kāmyakarmavataḥ kiñcitkālīnamamṛtatvaṃ akāmyakarmakṛto jijñāso'stu jñānadvārā mokṣākhyāmṛtatvaṃ "aganmajyotiravidāma devān‌'  iti vākyaśeṣe jyotirbrahma vyajijñāsiṣmetyukteḥ "utkramiṣyata evaṃ bhāvāt‌'  ityukteśca "sarvāpekṣā ca yajñādiśruteraśvāt‌'  iti vakṣyamāṇatvācca akāmyakarmiṇo jñāninastu "agnihotradi tu tatkāryāyaiva taddarśanād‌'  ityādai vakṣyamāṇadiśā mokṣaikadeśānandābhivyaktiviśeṣarūpamamṛtatvamityevaṃrūpeṇa jñeyaḥ | phale tu athapadādyuktavairāgyādisādhanānuṣṭhānākṣepasamādhī || 7 ||
|| oṃ kṛtātyaye'nuśayavān‌ dṛṣyasmṛtibhyām‌ oṃ || 8 ||
pūrvatra karmajaphalaṃ amṛtatvaṃ bhāktamityuktam‌ | iha tu karmaṇaḥ svargādau phalajanakatvaṃ bhāktaṃ bhāgenaiva na tu kṛtsnasyetyucyata iti saṅgatiḥ | atrāthādipadoktaṃ vairāgyādikaṃ mumukṣuṇā na kāryamuta kāryamiti cintā | tadarthaṃ bandhakībhūtakarmaṇaḥ svargādau niḥśeṣabhogo'rthaikadeśeneti | tadarthaṃ karmaṇo bhāgaśo bhogakalpakaṃ nāstyutāstīti | pūrvapakṣastu | "karmaṇā badhyate jantuḥ' ityādeḥ bandhakībhūtakāmyakarmaṇo jīvena sahaiva svargādāvagatasya bhāgena bhogakalpane mānābhāvena niḥśeṣabhogasambhavādbhogena kamar bhuktvā taccheṣeṇa svargātpunarāgamanāṅgīkāre'pi evaṃ dvityāvartanena niḥśeṣakarmasamāptisambhavācca | bandhabījābhāve bandhasya svata eva nivṛtteḥ na kāryaṃ mumukṣuṇā vairāgyasādhanamiti | siddhāntastu | "tataḥ śeṣeṇemaṃ lokamāyāti' iti śrutau "bhuktaśeṣānuśayavān‌' iti smṛtau ca svargagatasya yathāyogyaṃ karma sahāgatamapi kiṃcidbhuktavataḥ karmaśeṣayutavata evāgamanasya punaḥ karmakaraṇasya "daśāvarāṇām‌' ityanalpakarmakṛteścoktyā bhogena niḥśeṣakarmakṣayābhāt‌ kāryameva vairāgyādīti || 8 ||
|| oṃ yathetamanevaṃ ca oṃ || 9 ||
pūrvamanuśayavānyātītyukte kiṃ yathāgataṃ tathaivāyāti mārgāntareṇa vetyākāṅkṣātra nivartyata iti saṅgatiḥ | atādipadoktaṃ vairāgyaṃ na kāryamutakāryamiti cintā | tadarthaṃ gatiprakāreṇaivāgamanamanyathā veti | tadarthaṃ mārgaśravaṇamāgamanopayogi utānupayogīti | ādye buddhisthagatamārgeṇaivāgamanāt‌ āgatau kleśābhāvāt‌ taddhetukarmaṇi vairāgyaṃ na | antye tu anyathāpyāgamanena
aparicitamārgakṛtakleśabhāvāt‌ taddhetuvairāgyamāvaśyakamiti phalaphalibhāvaḥ | pūrvapakṣastu | paricitamārgatyāgena aparicitamārgeṇāgatau hetvabhāvāt‌ "yathetamāgacchati' iti śrutivirodhācca gatimārgeṇaivāgamanamiti na kāryaṃ vairāgyādīti | siddhāntastu | gamane jīvasyāsvātanteṇa tatsmaraṇasyāgamanānupayogāt‌ "dhūmādabhram‌' iti bhāṣyokta śrutau ākāśaparyantameva gatimārgeṇāgamanaṃ tato'rvāganyathātvasyoktatvādyathetamiti śruteśca kiṃcidgatiprakāreṇāgateraṅgīkāreṇa tadviṣayatvopapatteḥ | gamanaprakārādanyathāpyāgamanamiti kāryaṃ vairāgyādati || 9 ||
|| oṃ caraṇāditi cenna tadupalīṇārtheti kārṣṇājiniḥ oṃ || 10 ||
|| oṃ ānarthakyamiti cenna tadapekṣatvāt‌ oṃ || oṃ sukṛtaduṣkṛte eveti tu bādariḥ oṃ ||
evaṃ gatyāgatimārgasthitau siddhāyāṃ tatsādhananirṇayo'tra kriyata iti saṅgatiḥ | athādipadoktaṃ kāmyakarmaṇi vairāgyaṃ nāvaśyakamutāvaśyakamiti cintā | tadarthaṃ kiṃ gatāgataṃ caraṇaphalamuta kāmyakarmaphalamiti | tadarthaṃ "tadya iha ramaṇīyacaraṇāḥ ramaṇīyāṃ yonimāpadyante'  iti śrutau caraṇaśabdaḥ kiṃ karmāṅgācāraparaḥ kiṃvā karmaparaḥ iti | pūrvapakṣastu | caraṇaphalameva gatāgataṃ na karmaphalam‌ | caraṇaśrutau gatāgatasya caraṇaphalatvokteḥ | na caraṇameva karma | "sa yathākārī yathācārī' iti śrutau "ācāra iti samproktaḥ karmāṅgatvena śuddhidaḥ' iti smṛtau ca tasyānarthakyaprasaṅgāt‌ | yajñādikāriṇāṃ prakṛtatvena ye yajñādikāriṇo ramaṇīyāṃ ityādirūpeṇokitasambhave vināpi caraṇaśabdaṃ karmopalakṣaṇasambhavāt‌ | naca "ya ime grāma iṣyāpūrte dattamityupāsane te dhūmamabhisambhavanti'  ityādau gatāgatasya yajñādiphalatvoktivirodhaḥ iti vācyam‌ | tatrāpyupāsata ityupaśabdena samīpavṛttivācinā karmasamīpavṛtticaraṇasya gṛhīteriti | siddhāntastu | bhavatyeva puṇyapāpākhyakarmaphalaṃ gatāgatam‌ | "iṣyāpūrtaṃ manyamānāvariṣṭhaṃ nānyacchreyo vedayante pramūḍhāḥ'  iti śrutau "traividyā māṃ somapāḥ pūtapāpāḥ'  ityādismṛtau ca gatyādeḥ karmaphalatvapratītestadbalena caraṇaśruteḥ ajahallakṣaṇayā yajñādyarthatve'pi ramaṇīyādyācāreṇaiva yajñakṛtāṃ ramaṇīyatvādikamiti sūcakatayā caraṇaśabdasya sārthakyasambhavāt‌ | dharmaṃ kurutetyarthe "dharmaṃ carataḥ' iti prayogabalena caraṇaśruteḥ yajñādiparatvādeva "ya ime grāmaḥ'  iti śruterarthāntarakalpanamapramāṇakam‌ | "atha ye yajñena dānena tapasā lokān‌ jayanti te dhūmamabhisaṃviśanti' iti samākhyāviruddhaṃ ceti kāmyakarmaṇi kāryaṃ vairāgyādīti || 10 ||
|| oṃ aniṣyādikāriṇāmapi ca śrutam‌ oṃ || 11 ||
|| oṃ saṃyamenetvanubhūyetareṣāmārohāvarohau tadgatidarśanāt‌ oṃ || oṃ smaranti ca oṃ ||
astyevamuktadiśā gatyādeḥ puṇyākhyakarmaphalatvaṃ na tu pāpākhyakarmaphalatvamiti śaṅkanātsaṅgatiḥ | athapadoktaṃ vairāgyaṃ kiṃ kāmyapuṇyakarmaṇyeva uta pāpādāvapīti cintā | tadatharṃ puṇyapāpākhyakarmaphalatayā prakṛtamārgagamanādiduḥkhaṃ kiṃ puṇyakṛtāmeva uta apuṇyakṛtāmapīti | tadarthaṃ gatāgataṃ kiṃ pratyavāyabhayaphalakāmanādiprayuktamuta karmaprayuktameveti | tadarthaṃ bhayādihīnānāmapi apuṇyakṛtāṃ gatyādau mānaṃ nāstyutāstīti | ādye puṇyakṛtāmeva pratyavāyabhayādimatāṃ gattyādi | antye tu puṇyakṛtāmapuṇyakṛtāmubhayeṣāmapīti phalam‌ | pūrvapakṣastu | puṇyakṛtāmevagamanādiduḥkhaṃ natvapuṇyakṛtāṃ teṣāṃ pratyavāyabhayavattvāt‌ | bibhyatpatati pādapāditi nyāyena bhayavata eva patanavattvādiduḥkhitvopalambhāt‌ phalakāmanāvattvācca | kāmyapuṇyakṛtāmeva gamanādi | "kāmānyaḥ kāmayate manyamānaḥ sa kāmabhirjāyate tatra tatra' iti śrutau kāmina eva punarāgamanokteḥ | aniṣyādikṛtāṃ ca bhayābhāvātkāmanābhāvācca na punarāgatyādi duḥkham‌ | naca bhayādimattvaṃ patanādiduḥkhāprayojakaṃ kintu karmaiveti vācyaṃ kalpakābhāvāt‌ | kiṃca puṇyakṛtāmiva puṇyamakurvatā pāpaṃ ca kuvartāṃ tātkālikakāmoparodhādirūpakleśābhāvena srak‌candanavanitādibhogādhikyena ca bhāvipunarāgamanādimātrakleśasyākiñcitkaratvāt‌ na kāryaṃ tatra vairāgyādīti | siddhāntastu | "tadya iha śubhakṛto ye cāśubhakṛtaste śubhamanubhūyāvartanta'  iti śrutau puṇyakṛtāmivānyeṣāmapyāgateruktatvāt‌ | tathā
"sarve vā ete'śubhakṛtaḥ saṃyamane prapatanti'  iti bhāṣyoktaśrutau "gacchanti pāpinaḥ' ityādi smṛtiṣu ca puṇyakṛdanyeṣāṃ nityanarakādimahānarthasya cokteśca bhagavadajñānināṃ sarveṣāmapyastyevāgamanādiduḥkhaṃ bhavayattvābhāvādihetudvayaṃ cāprayojakam‌ | abibhyatā phalakāmanāśūnyenāpi upabhuktaviṣasya tanmṛtihetutādṛṣyeḥ kāryaṃ tatrātiśayena vairāgyamiti || 11 ||
|| oṃ api sapta oṃ || 12 ||
pūrvatra haridveṣiṇo nityanarakoktirayuktā | "yāvadindraścaturdaśa' iti tadanityatvokterityākṣepātsaṅgatiḥ | athapadoktaṃ bhagavaddveṣādāvatiśayitavairāgyamanāvaśyakaṃ utāvaśyakamiti cintā | tadarthaṃ pūrvaṃ tatphalatvenokto narakaḥ kiṃ punarāvṛttimānuta neti | tadarthaṃ narakaḥ kimeka evotāneka iti | pūrvapakṣastu | narakabhede mānābhāvādekasya yāvadindrāścaturdaśetyādāvanityatvokteḥ punarāvṛttimāneva bhagavaddveṣinarakaḥ | na ca "mahātamasimagnānāṃ na teṣāmutthitiḥ kvacit‌' iti smṛtyā nityatvasyāpyavagamena puruṣabhedenaikasyaiva narakasya nityatvamanityatvaṃ cāstviti vācyam‌ | "cakāra narakaṃ śūnyam‌' ityādevirrodhāt‌ | ato na bhagavaddveṣe'tiśayena vairāgyamāvaśyakamiti | siddhāntastu | "rauravo'tha mahāṃścaiva'  iti bhāṣyoktasmṛtau anityanarakaprapañcasya nityanarakadvayasya ca prādhānyenokteḥ | yāvadindrāścaturdaśetyāderanityanarakaviṣayatvopapatteḥ | bhagavaddveṣinarakaḥ punarāvṛttiśūnya eveti bhāvyaṃ tatrātiśayitavairāgyeṇeti || 12 ||
|| oṃ tatrāpi ca tadvayapārādavirodhaḥ oṃ || 13 ||
astvaniṣyādikāriṇoḥ dvividhādhikāriṇoḥ nityānityanarakabhogaḥ | tāvatā na taddhetau vairāgyamāvaśyakam‌ | tasya duḥkhasvarūpatvābhāvāditi śaṅkanātsaṅgatiḥ | athapadoktaṃ pāpādau vairāgyaṃ na kāryamuta kāryamiti cintā | tadatharṃ tatphalarūpo narakaḥ kimaduḥkharūpo'tha duḥkharūpa iti | tadarthaṃ narakasya saduḥkhatve tatprerakeśvarasya duḥkhabhogaḥ prasajyata uta neti | tadarthaṃ kiṃ bhogo nāma duḥkhasākṣātkāramātramuta tatkṛtanīcatvādireveti | pūrvapakṣastu | duḥkhāparokṣasyaiva tadbhogarūpatvānnārakiṇo duḥkhitve tatprerakasya sarvajñeśvarasya duḥkhasākṣitvarūpaduḥkhabhogaprasaṅgāt‌ narako na duḥkharūpaḥ | na ceśvaro na tatprerakaḥ sarvaṃ prerayatīti bhāṣyoktaśrutivirodhāt‌ | ato na vairāgyaṃ kāryamiti | siddhāntastu | "nīcoccataiva duḥkhāderbhoga ityabhidhīyate' iti  smṛtyā nīcatvādereva bhogaśabdārthatvāttasyaiva ca duḥkhiprerakatve'pi nirvikāre acintyaśaktau harāvabhāvasya "nāsau duḥkhabhuk‌' iti śrutismṛtisiddhatvena nārakiṇāṃ duḥkhitve bādhakābhāvāt‌ saduḥkho naraka iti bhāvyaṃ tatra vairāgyeṇeti || 13 ||
|| oṃ vidyākarmaṇoriti tu prakṛtatvāt‌ oṃ || 14 ||
vairāgyārthaṃ narakādeḥ saduḥkhoktirayuktā | tena vināpi tatphale gatyādau jīvānāṃ svātanteṇecchayā tatphalagatyādityāgopādānasambhavāditi śaṅkanātsaṅgatiḥ | athapadoktaṃ kāmyakarmaṇi vairāgyaṃ nāvaśyakamutāvaśyakamiti cintā | tadarthaṃ jīvānāṃ gatyādau svātantyamasti uta neti | tadarthaṃ "athaitayoḥ pathornaikatareṇa ca gacchanti tānīmāni kṣudramiśrāṇi'  iti śrutau etayorapi kiṃ devayānapitṛyānarūpamārgaparāmarśaḥ uta vidyākarmaṇoriti | pūrvapakṣastu | chāndogye pañcame "te'cirṣamabhisambhavanti'  "te dhūmamabhisambhavanti'  iti pūrvaprakṛtau devayānādimārgau etayorityuravākye parāmṛśya naikatareṇa gacchantītyuktyā bhūtānāṃ gatau bhūtasvātantyamavagamyate | puruṣaprayatnāgocare na gacchantītyuktyayogāt‌ | itaravyapadeśādityādau jīvāsvātantoktāvapīśādhīnasvātantyaṃ sambhavatyeva | jīvānāṃ svātante savareṣāmapi gatiprāptiriti tu sādhanasvātante'pi samam‌ | adṛṣyādinā atiprasaṅgādivāṇamihāpi samam‌ | tathāca gatāveva svātantāt‌ "mūle labdhe naiva śākhāgraṃ gantumicchati' iti nyāyena phala eva svātantāt‌ kiṃ kāmyakarmavairāgyeṇeti | siddhāntastu | "tadya itthaṃ vidurye ceme'raṇye śraddhātapa upāsate te'rciṣamabhisambhavanti' "atha ya ime grāma iṣyāpūrte
dattamityupāsate te dhūmam‌' iti vidyākarmaṇorapi pūrvatraprakṛtatvāddevayānādimārgayoḥ tatsādhanavidyākarmaṇośca prakṛtatvāviśeṣe'pi "vidyāpathaḥ karmapatho dvau panthānau prakīrtitau | tadvarjitastridhā yāti tiryagvā narakaṃ tamaḥ' iti bhāṣyoktasmṛtisamākhyayā vidyākarmaṇorevaitayorityuttaravākye parāmarśena sādhana eva jīvānāṃ svātanteṇa phale tadabhāvāt‌ sādhane vidhisadbhāvena vidhyādiśāstrāvaiyarthyāyaiva tatreśvaraḥ svātantyaṃ dadātītyabhyupagamena phale'pi svātantāpādanāprasakteḥ āvaśyakameva kāmye vairāgyādīti || 14 ||
|| oṃ na tṛtīye tathopalabdheḥ oṃ || 15 ||
|| oṃ smaryate'pi ca loke oṃ || oṃ daśarnācca oṃ || oṃ tṛtīye śabdāvarodhaḥ saṃśokajasya oṃ || oṃ smaraṇācca oṃ ||
atra "tiryagvā narakaṃ tamaḥ' iti pūrvoktatamaḥsvarūpacintātsaṅgatiḥ | athapadoktaṃ bhagavaddveṣiphale tamasyatiśayena vairāgyaṃ na kāryamuta kāryamiti cintā | tadarthaṃ tamaḥ kimitaranarakavat‌ sukhaduḥkhamiśramuta duḥkhaikasvarūpamiti | tadarthaṃ kiṃ duḥkhaṃ sukhāvinābhūtamuta neti | tadarthaṃ yatra duḥkhaṃ sukhaṃ tatreti vyāptigrāhakamānasya tamasyapavādakaṃ pramāṇaṃ nāstyutāstīti | pūrvapakṣastu | yatra duḥkhaṃ sukhaṃ tatreti vyāpteḥ sārvatrikasukhaduḥkhayoratīndriyatvena apratyakṣatve'pi "yatra duḥkhaṃ sukhaṃ tatra' iti bhāṣyoktasmṛtyā grahaṇasambhavena duḥkhayukte tamasi itaranaraka iva sukhasyāpyanumānasiddhatvāt‌ apavādakamānasya cādarśanāt‌ anyanarakāpekṣayā nātiśayena tamasi vairāgyaṃ vidheyamiti | siddhāntastu | śrutismṛtipratyakṣānumānārthāpattibhyaḥ tamaso duḥkhaikayuktvāvagamāt‌ yatra duḥkhamiti smṛteḥ tamonyanarakaviṣayatvāvaśyaṃbhāvādbhāvyaṃ tatrāśayitavairāgyeṇeti | tathāhi | na tamaḥ sukhānuvṛttaṃ "kevalaṃ hyevātra duḥkham‌' iti śrutiḥ nādhetamasimagnānāṃ sukhaleśo'pi kaścana'  iti smṛtiḥ | "nārāyaṇaprasādena'  ityādi bhāṣyoktasmṛtisiddhaṃ caturmukhapratyakṣaṃ "atipriye yathā rājā'  ityādi smṛtisiddhatayā prabalam‌ | vipratipanna na duḥkhavantaḥ tathātveneśvarāniṣyatvāt‌ yadyatheśvarāniṣyaṃ tattathā na bhavati yathā śaśo viṣāṇitvenetyanumānaṃ ca | na cāsiddhiḥ | naite sukhitvena īśvareṣyāḥ apirayatvāt‌ yo yasyāpriyaḥ sa sukhitvena tasyāniṣyaḥ yathā rājavirodhī rājñaḥ ityanumānena tatsiddheḥ | apriyatvaṃ ca dveṣitvādeva siddham‌ | tathā "mahātamastridhā proktam‌' ityādi  smṛtyā tamasaḥ svarūpaśravaṇe śrotṝṇāṃ śravaṇajaduḥkhādhikyajanyamūrchāprāptyavagamena tadanyathānupapattirūpārthāpattiśceti || 15 ||
|| oṃ tatsvābhāvyāpattirupapatteḥ oṃ || 16 ||
astveva duḥkhahetāvaniṣyādau tatphaleca vairāgyaṃ na tu kāmyakarmaṇi | tatphaleca sukhahetutvāt‌ sukharūpatvācceti śaṅkanātsaṅgatiḥ | athapadoktaṃ kāmyakarmādau vairāgyaṃ na yuktamuta yuktamiticintā | tadarthaṃ karma mahāsukhaheturuta neti | tadarthaṃ karmiṇaḥ svargādigatau "dhūmo bhūtvābhraṃ bhavati'  itayādinā śruto dhūmādibhāvaḥ kiṃ dhūmādidevataikyatatpadaprāptyanyatararūpaḥ uta sādṛśyādirūpa iti | tadarthaṃ dhūmo bhavatītyādisāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ kiṃ mukhyamuta sādṛśyādirūpa iti | tadarthaṃ dhūmo bhavatītyādisāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ kiṃ mukhyamuta sādṛśyādinā gauṇamiti | tadarthaṃ bādhakaṃ nāstyutāstīti | pūrvapakṣastu | devataikyapadaprāptyoranyatara eva dhūmādibhāvaḥ | sāmānādhikaraṇyaśrutermukhyārthatyāge bādhakābhāvāt‌ | devatābhiḥ snehādivaśena tābhireva vidyāprāpyasyāpi svapadasya dātumapi śakyatvena karmirastatpadaprāpterasambhāvitatvābhāvācca sukhahetutvānna karmaṇi vairāgyamiti | siddhāntastu | loke'nyasyānyabhāvādṛṣyayāpi atyantabhinnasya karmiṇo detaikyasyāyogāttatpadasya ca niyatādhikāribhiḥ vidyayaiva gamyatayā yugapadanekakarmisamavāye'nekeṣāmekapadaprāptyanupapattyā cāsambhavāt‌ sāmānādhikaraṇyaśrutermukhyārthatyāgāvaśyaṃbhāvena dhūmādibhāvaprāptiśca "tadgatau gatireva tu'  ityādi smṛtyuktadiśā devatāsādṛśyaparā vā tatpraveśena sthānaikyaparā vā neyeti mahāsukhāhetutvādyuktaṃ kāmyakarmaṇi tatphale ca vairāgyamiti || 16 ||
|| oṃ nāticireṇa viśeṣāt‌ oṃ || 17 ||
pūrvatrāgatau karmiṇo dhūmādibhāvaścintitaḥ atra tu āgatireva kiyatkāleneti cintanādvā karmiṇo devatāsvābhāvyāpattāvapi tasyāścirakālīnatvenāpi mahāsukhavattvānna karmaṇi vairāgyamiti śaṅkanātsaṅgatiḥ | atrāthapadoktaṃ kāmyakarmaṇi vairāgyaṃ ca yuktamuta yuktamiti cintā | tadarthaṃ tatkiṃ mahāsukhaheturuta neti | tadarthaṃ āgamane kālaklṛptirnāstyutāstīti | pūrvapakṣastu | āgamane etāvati kāla evāgantavyamiti kālaklṛptyabhāvena "yathetamākāśam‌'  ityādināgatau bahusthānaprāptiśravaṇena ca tattaddevatāsvābhāvyena prāptātimahāsukhatyāgena śīghramāgamane hetvabhāvācca | kalpāntameva madhyermāgamavasthāne prāptasukhahetau na karmaṇi vairāgyaṃ yuktamiti | siddhāntastu | "tadya iha ramaṇīya caraṇā abhyāśo ha yatte ramaṇīyaṃ yonimāpadyante'  ityādiśrutau "svargāllokādavā noti vatsarātpūrvameva tu'  iti smṛtau ca āgatau kamirṇaḥ kālaklṛptyavagamāt‌ kl‌ptyakālādhikāvasthāne kāraṇābhāvāt‌ tasyeṣyatve'pi jīvasyāsvātantāt‌ | anyathā sarveṣāṃ duḥkhābhāvaprasaṅgāt‌ aticireṇaivāgamanamiti bhāvyaṃ kāmye karmaṇi vairāgyeṇeti || 17 ||
|| oṃ anyādhiṣṭhite pūrvavadabhilāpāt‌ oṃ || 18 ||
|| oṃ aśuddhamiti cenna śabdāt‌ oṃ ||
pūvartra śrutyādinā karmiṇo'cireṇāgatirityuktirayuktā tatraivānāśvāsāditi śaṅkanātsaṅgatiḥ | athapadoktaṃ vairāgyaṃ na kāryamuta kāryamiti cintā | tadarthaṃ nirvedamāyādityādivairāgyavidhāyakaśrutau āśvāso na yukta uta yukta iti | tadarthaṃ vedaḥ kvacitphalavyabhicārī uta neti | tadarthaṃ "ta iha vrīhiyavā'  iti śrutyuktakarmirāṃ vrīhyādibhāvaḥ kiṃ mukhyaḥ uta dhūmādibhāvavadgauṇaḥ iti | gauṇatve'pi karmiṇo duḥkhaprāpakamastyuta neti | pūrvapakṣastu | na kāryaṃ vairāgyaṃ tadvidhāyakaśrutāvanāśvāsāt‌ | tadanāśvāsaśca svargakāmo yajetetyādivat‌ vedaikadeśe sukhasādhanatayokte karmaṇi karmaphalamārge vrīhyādibhāvāvagatyā tacchedanakuṭṭanādinā duḥkhaprāpterāvaśyakatā phalavaiparītyenānāśvāsāt‌ | vrīhyādibhāvasya dhūmādhibhāvavat‌ gauṇatve vrīhyādiduḥkhijīvaiḥ sahāvasthānena yāgādikarmaṇaḥ paśuhiṃsārūpatayā pāpatvena karmiṇo duḥkhaprāpakasattvena ca duḥkhāvaśyaṃbhāvācceti | siddhāntastu | karmiṇaḥ śrutavrīhyādibhāvasya jīvāntarādhiṣṭhitavrīhyādidehapraveśarūpatayā pūrvavadgauṇatvāt‌ duḥkhijīvena sahāvasthāne'pi karmaṇo hiṃsārūpatve'pi "vāyavyaṃ śvetamālabheta' ityādi vedavihitatvena apāpatayā duḥkhāprāpakatvena hetvabhāvena karmiṇo duḥkhāprāpterna kvāpi vedānāśvāsa iti bhāvyaṃ vairāgyeṇeti || 18 ||
|| oṃ retassigyogo'tha oṃ || 19 ||
pūrvatrānyādhiṣṭhite vrīhyādidehe iva svargādāgatasya karmiṇaḥ pitṛdehe praveśasādhanātsaṅgatiḥ | athapadoktaṃ karmādāvativairāgyamanapekṣyamutāpekṣyamiti cintā | tadarthaṃ svargādāgatasya karmiṇaḥ pitṛpraveśo nāstyutāstīti | tadarthaṃ "yovā retassiñcati tamevānupratiśati' iti śrutiḥ durbalā "svargādavāggataścāpi māturevodaraṃ vrajed‌' iti vacanaṃ prabalamuta viparītamiti | tadarthaṃ vacanaṃ sayuktikamuta neti | tadarthaṃ karmiṇaḥ pitṛpraveśe prayojanaṃ nāstyutāstīti | pūrvapakṣastu | karmiṇaḥ pitṛpraveśe phalābhāvāt‌ retaso dehapariṇāmāyāpekṣitatve'pi sekānantaraṃ mātṛpraveśe'pi tadupapatteḥ māturevodaraṃ vrajediti vacanāt‌ sayuktikasādhāraṇavacanabāditatayā pitṛpraveśaśruteḥ daurbalyena pitṛpraveśāprāpakatvāt‌ nāsti pitṛpraveśa iti nāpekṣyamativairāgyamiti | siddhāntastu | dahekl‌ptyatharmāvaśyakatayā retasa uktavidhayā anyathopapattāvapi "īśakl‌ptyaiva pitaraṃ praviṣyo yāti mātaram‌' iti īśaklṛptivacanena "retaḥsicamevānupraviśati'  iti śrutyā ca pitṛpraveśasiddheḥ māturevetyevakārasya ayogavyavacchedakatvenopapattyā sayuktikatvābhāvena ca pitṛpraveśāpratibandhakatvādasti pitṛpraveśa iti bhāvyaṃ karmādāvativairāgyeṇeti || 19 ||
|| oṃ yoneśśarīram‌ oṃ || 20 ||
pūrvasūtre athapadoktā pitṛdvārā mātṛprāptirevātra sādhyata iti saṅgatiḥ | athapadoktakarmādāvativairāgyaṃ anāvaśyakamutāvaśyakamiti cintā | tadarthaṃ karmiṇo dehaprāptau āyāso nāstyutāstīti | tadarthaṃ pitṛdehādyonidvārā mātṝdaraṃ prāpya svadehaṃ prā noti uta tathaivā notīti | tadarthamuktakramādanyathāpi janma sambhavatyuta neti | pūrvapakṣastu | uktakramādanyathāpi karmiṇaro janma sambhavati | tathāhi | pitussakāśādeva tasmin‌ bahireva vā māndhātṛdroṇāderiva janmasambhavena māturudaraprāpteranāvaśyakatvāttadāvaśyakatve vā "dehagarbhasthitaṃ kvāpi praviśetsvargato gataḥ' ityukteḥ | āstikāderiva saṃsparśadvārā pitussakāśānmāturudarapraveśasambhavena yoniprāpteranāvaśyakatvāt‌ | "dehaṃ garbhasthitam‌' iti vacanānmātrupabhujyamānānnādinā mātṝdarapraveśasambhavāt‌ mātṛdehastharetasaiva dehapariṇatisambhavācca pitṛpraveśasyāpi vyarthatvāt‌ | ato janmaprāptāvatyāyāsābhāvāt‌ nātivairāgyeṇa kṛtyamiti | siddhāntastu | pitṛpraveśapūrvaṃ yonidvārā mātṝdarapraveśenaiva karmiṇā janma nānyathā | "divaḥ sthāsnuṃ gacchati sthāsnubhyaḥ pitaraṃ pituḥ mātuśśarīram‌ śarīreṇa jāyate' ityādi  śruteḥ | dehaṃ garbhasthitamityādeḥ yogārādhitabhagavatprasādalabdhasāmarthyātiśayavatāṃ mahātmanāṃ janmaviṣayamiti karmiṇo dehaprāptau āyāsasadbhāvāt‌ bhāvyaṃ karmādāvati vairāgyeṇeti || 20 ||
|| iti śrīrāghavendrayatikṛtāyāṃ nyāyamuktāvalyāṃ tṛtīyādhyāyasya prathamaḥ pādaḥ ||
|| atha dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ ||
yaddārḍhyātharṃ vairāgyamuktaṃ pūrvapāde yā ca vakṣyamāṇopāsanāṅgaṃ tādṛśabhaktyarthamatrapāde bhagavanmahimocyata iti pūrvottarapādasaṅgatiḥ | etatpādārthaśca bhāṣyādito vyaktaḥ |
|| oṃ sandhye sṛṣyirāha hi oṃ || 1 ||
|| oṃ nirmātāraṃ caike putrādayaśca oṃ || oṃ māyāmātraṃ tu kātsnaryenānabhivyaktasvarūpatvāt‌ oṃ || oṃ sūcakaśca hi śruterācakṣate ca tadvidaḥ oṃ ||
pūrvatra yoneśśarīramiti jīvānāṃ janmāvagatāvavasthāvarṇanasya avasaraprāptatvāt‌ tatrāvasthānāṃ madhye "yonimanye prapadyante' ityadikāṭhakaśrutau  yoniprāptyanantaraṃ sva noktyā tadanurodhenātrādau sva nārthatatpratītiniyantṛtvarūpo mahimā harernirūpyate | atra jijñāsāsūtrasthāthaśabdoktādhikāriviśeṣaṇībhūtā haribhaktiḥ na kāryota kāryeti cintā | tadarthaṃ sva no'rthaḥ kimīśānadhīna uta tadadhīna iti | tadarthaṃ kimasatya uta satya iti | tadarthaṃ kiṃ satyatve sādhakaṃ nāsti bādhakaṃ cāsti uta viparītamiti | pūrvapakṣastu | neśādhīnaḥ sva nārthaḥ tasyāsattvāt‌ | asato'pi śaśaviṣāṇāderasatyatvena īśādhīnatvasya etena sarve vyākhyātā ityatroktāvapi ghaṭāditaulyena yogakṣemādirūpatadadhīnatvasya asato'nupapatteḥ | na cāsiddho hetuḥ | satyatve sādhakābhāvādbādhakabāhulyācca sva nārthānāṃ satyatve nityatvamanityatvaṃ vā vācyam‌ | ubhayathāpi prāgūrdhvamupalambhaprasaṅgāt | tadaivotpattivināśavattvaṃ tu pramāṇābhāvādayuktam‌ | kartrupādānanimittānāmabhāvācca | bāhyopādānādikāraṇakatve kapālādyupalambhāpātācca | tadajanyatve ca śuktirūpyavadasattvasya durvāratvācca | asantaḥ eva gajādayaḥ sva ne dṛṣyā iti bādhabodhayogācca | evaṃ bādhakāntarāṇi ṭīkoktāni jñeyāni | siddhāntastu | "yadā karmasu kāmyeṣu striyam‌'  ityādi śrutismṛtitaḥ sva nārthānāṃ śubhādisūcakatvapramāṇena śākunavatsatyatvāt‌ | "na tatra rathāḥ'  ityādiśrutyā prāgasatāmevārthānāṃ sva na eva dṛṣyeḥ | sṛṣyeścoktyā tadaivotpattinirodhayoḥ sambhavena prāgūrdhvamupalambhaprasaṅgāt‌ | "ya eṣu supteṣu' iti  śrutau "etasmāddhayeva putro jāyate'  iti śrutau ca sva nārthānāṃ īśakartṛtvasyoktyā kartrabhāvacodyasya tathā bāhyārthaghaṭādivat‌ sphuṭamanabhivyaktatvayuktyā "manogatāṃstu saṃskārān‌' iti  smṛtyā ca bāhyopādānavilakṣaṇamānasavāsanopādānakatvāvagamena nirupādānakatvacodyasya cānavakāśāt‌ | sva nānāṃ
jāgratpadārthatvasyāsattvena bādhopapatteśca sādhakasadbhāvācca sva nārthāḥ satyā eveti sva nārthāḥ taddhiyaḥ tattirodhānaṃ ceti sarvamīśādhīnamiti | phale tu pūrvādhyāyoktasavarniyantṛtvarūpamahimākṣepādidvārā bhaktyākṣepasamādhī | sūtrakramastu | bādhakanirāsa eva sādhakaṃ sukhasthaṃ bhavatīti trisūtyā bādhakāni nirasyānte sādhakoktiḥ | ata eva doṣaparihārāya sūtramiti ṭīkā | tatrāpi sṛṣyau siddhāyāṃ kartrādikārakacintā | tatrāpi kartuḥ prādhānyamityādirūhyaḥ || 1 ||
|| oṃ parābhidhyānāttu tirohitaṃ tato hyasya bandhaviparyayau oṃ || 2 ||
astu sva napadārthānāmuktadiśā īśāyattatvaṃ na tu taddhītattirodhānayoḥ | tayoḥ bāhyārthājñānajñānābhyāmeva siddheḥ iti śaṅkanātsaṅgatiḥ | atrāthapadoktabhaktirna kāryā uta kāryeti cintā | tadarthaṃ prakṛtāvirbhāvatirobhāvau kimīśādhīnau uta tadanadhīnāviti | tadarthaṃ bāhyārthājñānajñāne kiṃ sva nadhītirodhānayoḥ svatantraheturuta nimittamātramiti | pūrvapakṣastu | svā nāvirbhāvatirodhānayoḥ bāhyārthajñānajñānādhīnānubhavena tadāvirbhāvatirodhānayoḥ bāhyārthājñānajñānābhyāmevopapattau īśvareṇa kṛtyābhāvāt‌ | nahi bāhyātharjñānājñānayoḥ svanibandhanasvā nārthadhītannivṛttyoranyāpekṣāstīti | siddhāntastu | yathā bandhamokṣayoḥ īśājñānajñānanibandhanatvaprasiddhāvapi tayorjaḍatvenāsvatantratayā bandhako bhavapāśena bhavapāśācca mocaka ityādi vacaneneśādhīnāveva bandhamokṣau tathā bāhyārthajñānājñānanimittakatayā pratīyamānāvapi svā nāvirbhāvatirobhāvau īśādhīnāveva | jñānājñānayorasvātantāt‌ | te tu nimittamātram‌ | "svā nādibuddhikartā ca tiraskartā sa eva ca' ityāderiti | ṭīkāyāṃ tu sūtre bandhaviparyayāviti dvivacanasya sva nādi buddhikarteti smṛtyukteśca svārasyānurodhena āvirbhāvasya ajñānādhīnatvanirāsakādhikaraṇāntarasyābhāvena tirodhānasya jñānādhīnatvanirāsakādhikaraṇāntarasyābhāvena tirodhānasya jñānādhīnatvanirāsakanyāyasamānaviṣayatvāt‌ ādyasūtrādau tirohitamityasyopalakṣaṇatayā siddhānte svā nāvirbhāvatirobhāvayoḥ sāmyena dvandvena nirdeśādyathāśrutaṃ sūtrānurodhena tirodhānamātraprādhānyena pūrvapakṣoktāvapi dvayossāmyenaiva pūrvapakṣo'bhimata ityavirodhaḥ || 2 ||
|| oṃ dehayogādvāso'pi oṃ || 3 ||
ṣaṣṭhe kāṇvaśrutau jyotirbrahmaṇo "sa vā eṣa etasmin‌  ratvā caritvā'  ityādinā sva noktyanantaraṃ jāgraduktyā sva nāvirbhāvādicintānantaraṃ atra jāgraccintanātsaṅgatiḥ | atra jāgradarthānāṃ svā nārthavanna mithyātvaṃ bahujanavivādābhāvāt‌ bādhakābhāvāt‌ | alpajanavivādasya "nā bhāva upalabdheḥ  "vaidharmyācca na sva nādivat‌'  ityatra nirāsāt‌ | atra bhagavadadhīnatāmātraṃ cintyate | dehābhimānādināvasthitirūpa jāgradavasthā aharādikālādhīneti prāpte pūrvavadeva kālasyāsvātantādīśādhīnaiveti siddhāntaḥ | viśeṣaśaṅkā tu kālāsvātantyabuddheḥ bādhakābhāvena bhramatvāyogāt‌ na nimittamātraparatvamiti | parihārastu "sa eva jāgaritāsthāpayati' ityādiśrutibalānnimittamātraparatvamiti | phale tu pūrvavat‌ || 3 ||
|| oṃ tadabhāvo nāḍīṣu tacchatarātmani hi oṃ || 4 ||
pūrvatra sva najāgaraṇayoḥ kālāderasvātanteṇeśvarādhīnatvoktau avaśiṣyasupterapi pūrvanyāyeneśādhīnatvasiddhau atreśasya suptajīvaprāpyatvalābhāya suptisvarūpameva nirūpyate | atra jīvasya suptiḥ nāḍīparamātmanoranyataratra utobhayatreti cintā | tadarthaṃ "satā saumya tadāsampannaḥ'  iti ca śrutidvayamubhayatra suptivyavasthāpakaṃ na bhavatyatha bhavatīti | tadarthamubhayatra suptiviruddhota neti | tadarthaṃ ādye tacca kiṃ paramātmamuta nāḍyāmiti tadarthaṃ nāḍyāṃ suptau auttarikānusmṛtirna yuktota yukteti | tadarthaṃ sā kiṃ sauṣuptikasukhādyanubhavajanyasmṛtirūpa uta duḥkhābhāvānumitirūpa iti | yadyādyaḥ pakṣaḥ tadā sukhānusmṛtisiddhasauṣuptikasukhaikānubhavasya īśādanyatrasthitau sva nādāvivāyogātparamātmanīti phalam‌ | yadyantyaḥ tadā suptau sukhānubhavasyaivābhāvena nāḍyāmeveti phalam‌ | pūrvapakṣastu | ubhayatra supterviruddhatvena śrutidvayasyobhayatra suptivyavasthāpakatvābhāvāt‌ anyataratretyavaśyaṃ vācyam‌ | suptikālasyājñānena pakṣīkaraṇāyogāt‌ anumānānudayena "etāvantaṃ
kālaṃ sukhamahamasvāpsamityasya smṛtitvena tatsiddhasukhaikānubhavasya bhagavato'nyatrāvasthāne jāgradādāvivāyogāt‌ parāmātmanyeveti | satā saumya tadā sampanno bhavatīti śruteśca | naca nāḍīṣu supto bhavatīti śrutivirodhaḥ prācīnaśrutessayuktikatvena prābalyāditi | siddhāntastu | nityanirdoṣatayā pramāṇabhūtayoruktaśrutyoḥ sākṣādasākṣāttvābhyāṃ nāḍīsthaparamātmanīti vyavasthayā virodhābhāve sambhavati aprāmāṇyāyogāt‌ sukhaikānubhavasya copapatteḥ nāḍīsthaparamātmanyeveti | phale tu śrutyādyavasthādvārā bhaktyādisādhanaśāstrānāsthātannirāsamukhena bhaktyākṣepasamādhī | ṭīkāyāṃ tvanyataratreti pakṣaṃ siddhavatkṛtya paramātmani nāḍīṣu vā ityetatsaṃdihya vādyantarapakṣanirāsāya nāḍyāmeti pakṣaṃ vyudasya paramātmanyeveti pūrvapakṣavyājena sthāpayitvā ubhayatretyeva siddhāntitam‌ || 4 ||
|| oṃ ataḥ prabodho'smāt‌ oṃ || 5 ||
atra prāguktasuptaprāpyatvahenaiva siddhāntakaraṇātsaṅgatiḥ | prakṛtasuptaprabodhaḥ kiṃ kvacit‌ bherītāḍanādi hetvantarādhīnaḥ uta sarvatreśaikādhīna iti cintā | kvacit‌ bherītāḍanādi hetvantaradṛṣyeḥ nimittamātrakalpakaṃ nāstyutāstīti | pūrvapakṣastu | kvacidbherītāḍanādi kāraṇāntarāṇāmapyulambhāttatrāpi īśvarakalpane mānābhāvena kāraṇāntarābhāvasthale prabodhasyeśādhīnatve'pi na kāraṇāntarasadbhāvasthale tatra teṣāmeva svātantopapattau nimittamātratvakalpane hetvabhāvāditi | siddhāntastu | "eṣa eva suptaṃ prabodhayati'  iti sāvadhāraṇaśrutivirodhena hetvantarārāṃ nimittamātratvāt‌ suptaprabodhako'pi īśvara eva na tu kāraṇāntarasvātantyamiti | phale tu atha padoktabhaktyākṣepasamādhī eveti || 5 ||
|| oṃ sa eva ca karmānusmṛtiśabdavidhibhyaḥ oṃ || 6 ||
pūrvatra sāmānyataḥ sva nādyavasthā īśādhīnetyuktam‌ | atra tu sarvajanīnāḥ sarvakālīnā api tadadhīnā iti cintyata iti saṅgatiḥ | sva nādyāvasthāprerakatvena prakṛto viṣṇuḥ kiṃ keṣāñcitkādācitkāvasthāprerakaḥ uta sarvajīvānāṃ sarvakālīnāvasthāprerakaḥ iti cintā | tadarthaṃ lokadṛṣyirniyāmikā bhavati uta neti | tadarthaṃ rājādilokadṛṣyayāpavādakaṃ mānaṃ nāstyutāstīti | pūrvapakṣastu | keṣāñcitkādācitkāvasthāprerako na sarvajanīnasarvakālīnāvasthāprerakaḥ loke rājādau khaṇḍeśatvadarśanāt‌ | naca dṛṣyiraprayojikā | tathātve pratyakṣasiddhā jñānakālabherītāḍanādi kāraṇāpekṣāpi sva nādyavasthāpreraṇena syāt‌ | naca "anyabhāvavyāvṛtteśca'  ityādau lokadṛṣyityāgenoktāṇutvamahatvastrītvapuṃstvādisahabhāvavat‌ "alpaśruteḥ'  ityatroktavyomāṃśavailakṣaṇyavat‌ "śruteḥ'  ityādau niraṃśasyāpyekadeśena pravṛttyādivaccāstu lokadṛṣyityāge sarvaprerakatvamiti vācyam‌ | tatrāṇoraṇīyān‌ asthūlamanaṇvityādi eṣame'ntarhṛdaye jyāyān‌ yo'sau viruddho'viruddhaḥ ityādi pramāṇavat‌ iha sarvāvasthāprerakatve mānābhāvāt‌ yena dṛṣyityāgaḥ syāditi | siddhāntastu | viṣṇureva sarvatra sarvajīvena sarvakālikasarvāvasthāprerakaḥ na tu kvacidanyo'pi prerakaḥ | "eṣa hyeva sādhukarma kārayati' ityādi bhāṣyokta  śrutibalena "ātmana evopāsyatvoktyanyathānupapattyā ca asyaiva sarvaprerakatvāvagamāt‌ | anyathā anyasyāpi kvacitprerakatve śrutāvavadhāraṇāt‌ | nacaivaṃ lokadṛṣyivirodhaḥ | śrutyādipramāṇabalena lokadṛṣyasyāpi laukikadhenvādāvadṛṣyasya kāmadhenvādāvivopapatteriti | phale tūkta eva || 6 ||
|| oṃ mugdhe'rdhasampattiḥ pariśeṣāt‌ oṃ || 7 ||
evaṃ pradhānāvasthānāṃ cintānantaraṃ atrāvāntarāvasthācintanātsaṅgatiḥ | atra sva nādyavasthācintāprasaṅgasannidhāpito mohaḥ kiṃ sva nādyavasthānyatamāntarbhūtaḥ uta tato'vasthāntaramiti | tadarthaṃ sa kiṃ bhagavatprāptitadanyatrasthityoranyatararūpaḥ uta tābhyāmanyādṛśa eveti | tadarthamanyādṛśatve mānaṃ nāstyutāstīti | yadyādyastadā avasthāntaramiti vyaktam‌ | tathānyatararūpa ityatrāpi kimuktabhagavatprāptirūpaḥ kiṃ vā anyatrasthitirūpa iti cintā | tadarthaṃ kimayaṃ suptisadharma sva najāgratsadharmeti | atrāpi vyaktaṃ phalaphalibhāvaḥ | atra ṭīkāyāmādyasandehaḥ siddhāntoktadiśā sujñāna iti pradhānāvasthāntaraṃ neti pradarśanāya sva
nāditritayānyatamatvaṃ siddhavatkṛtya mohe brahmaprāptireva pṛthagavasthātvāditi nyāyavivaraṇe jāgratsva nayoḥ pṛthagavasthādityarthamupetya tadanuguṇatvena saṃdehapūrvapakṣau darśitau | ata eva siddhānte mohasya avasthātrayātiriktatve bhavedetadityādyuktamiti jñeyam‌ | pūrvapakṣastu | mohaḥ sva nādityavasthāntarbhūta eva na tu tato riktaḥ mānābhāvāt‌ | brahmaprāptitadanyatrasthityoḥ suptyādyavasthātvena tato anyādṛśasthiterabhāvena atiriktatvāsambhavācca | bāhyātharājñānasāmyena suptyādyantarbhāvasambhavācca | antarbhāvapakṣe'pi na bhagavadanyatrasthitirūpasva najāgradavasthāntarbhūtaḥ | tayoḥ svā nabāhyagajaturagādyarthajñānavatvarūpatvānmohasya ca tadubhayajñānaśūnyasuptisamānadharmatayā samagrabhagavatprāptirūpasuptyavasthāntarbhūta eveti | siddhāntastu | duḥkhamātrapratismṛteriti  smṛtyā duḥkhātmakālātiriktaviṣayāntarajñānaśūnyarūpatvena sukhādijñānopetasuptyādyantarbhāvāyogāt‌ mohāvasthātrayātiriktāvasthaiva duḥkhaikānubhavatvasyaiva bhānatvāt‌ | na cātiriktatvāsambhavaḥ | paramātmavidūratvasāmīpyapraveśānāṃ tadanyatrasthititatprāptirūpāṇāṃ "hṛdayasthāt‌'  iti smṛtyārdhapraveśasya mohatvenāvaśyaṃ vācyatayā pariśeṣapramāṇasiddhatvāditi | phale tu avasthāntarāvasthitajīvaprāpyatvarūpamāhātmyābhāvatadbhāvābhyāṃ bhaktyākṣepasamādhī | atra mohaḥ kiṃ bāhyakāraṇādhīna uta īśvarādhīna iti sandehe balavadabhighātādibāhyakāraṇādhīnatvānneśādhīna iti prāpte "sa eva ca' ityatrokta  siddhāntanyāyena īśādhīna eva | bāhyakāraṇadṛṣyistu "mūrchāprabodhanaṃ caiva tata eva pravartate' iti viśiṣyakaurmasmṛtyā nimittamātraviṣayetyarthasya pūrvanyāyenaiva siddhatvāt‌ nātra taccintanaṃ kṛtamiti jñeyam‌ || 7 ||
|| oṃ na sthānato'pi parasyobhayaliṅgaṃ sarvatra hi oṃ || 8 ||
|| oṃ na bhedāditi cenna pratyekamatadvacanāt‌ oṃ || oṃ api caivameke oṃ ||
pūrvatra sarvāvasthāpravartakatvasya hareruktau iha tatpravartakaviśvādirūpeṣu bhedaśaṅkānirāsātsaṅgatiḥ | pratiśarīraṃ jāgradādisthāneṣu sthito viśvādirūpī bhagavān‌ kimanyonyaṃ bhedavānutābhinna iti cintā | tadarthaṃ kiṃ tasya bhedasādhakaṃ bhinnānekasthānatvaṃ kāryakāraṇabaddhatvādibhedaśravaṇaṭha ca kiṃ niravakāśamuta gauṇatvādinā sāvakāśamiti | pūrvapakṣastu | anyonyaṃ bhedavāneva akṣikaṇṭhādirūpabhinnasthānakatvāt‌ ghaṭādivat‌ | naca bhinnasthānakatvaṃ bhedarūpasādhyena vinā vyomavadityatroktadiśā vyo na iva vā harerupapannamiti vācyam‌ | vyo nī'śato'nekasthānatvasambhave'pi hareranekasthānasthitaviśvādirūpāṇāṃ "saptāṅga ekonaviṃśatimukhaḥ'  ityādinā pratyekaṃ samagrarūpaśravaṇena vyomavaiṣamyeṇa bhedasādhyena vinā hetoranavakāśāt‌ | kiṃca "kāryakāraṇabaddhau tau'  ityādinā viśvādirūpāṇāṃ jāgratsva nasuṣuptirūpakāryabaddhatvakāraṇabaddhatvābaddhatvasthūlabhuktvasūkṣmabhuktvādirūpeṇa bhedavyapadeśāccānyonyaṃ bhedavāneva | nacāyaṃ vyapadeśo bhedaṃ vināpi hetvantareṇaiva yuktaḥ kalpakābhāvāt‌ hetvantaradṛṣyaśceti | siddhāntastu | anyonyaṃ tatra tatra sthitarūpairabhinna eva bhagavān‌ | "sarveṣu bhūteṣvetameva brahmetyācakṣate'  sarveṣu bhūteṣu cakṣurādisthāneṣu etamekameva pūrṇaguṇamācakṣate iti "eṣa ta ātmāntaryāmyamṛtaḥ  iti "ayameva saḥ yo'yamātmā'  ityādibhāṣyoktaśrutiṣu tatra tatra sthitabhagavadrūpāṇāṃ parasparamūlarūpeṇa cābhedakathanāt‌ | "amātro'nantamātraśca' iti  śrutau ca bhinnāṃśaśūnyaḥ punaranantāṃśaśceti viśvādyanantarūpāṇāṃ abhedokteśca | bhedānumānasya bhedapratinidhiviśeṣeṇa vā uktānekaśrutibādhitatayā aprāmāṇyenaivopapatteḥ | bhedavyapadeśasya ca "bhinno bhinneṣu saṃsthiteḥ'  "aiśvaryādrūpamekaṃ ca sūryavadbahudheyate' ityādismṛtyā goṣṭhe gāvaḥ ekībhavantītyatraikyoktivat‌ vyāpte harau "aṅguṣṭhamātraḥ puruṣaḥ  ityuktivacca sthānabhedenopacāreṇa vā aiśvaryabalena vā upapatteḥ | taduktamanyatra śrutibalātsthānānāṃ bhedaḥ tasyābheda eva' iti  | phale tu bhedaprāptau anugrāhyānugrāhakabhāvādyāpattyā "sa eva ca'  ityatroktaiśvaryanirāsadvārā bhaktyākṣepasamādhī || 8 ||
|| oṃ arūpavadeva hi tatpradhānatvāt‌ oṃ || 9 ||
|| oṃ prakāśavaccāvaiyarthyam‌ oṃ || oṃ āha ca tanmātram‌ oṃ || oṃ darśayati cātho'pi smaryate oṃ ||
pūrvatrobhayaliṅgamiti liṅgapadena prakṛtabhagavadrūpāṇāmatra aprākṛtatvasādhanātsaṅgatiḥ | yadarthaṃ pūrvatra mahimoktaḥ sā bhaktiḥ viṣṇau na yuktāthāyukteti cintā | tadarthaṃ tatra māhātmyaṃ na yuktamuta yuktamiti | tadarthaṃ rūpavattvārūpavattvayordoṣostyuta neti | tathā rūpitvārūpitvaśrutyoḥ kimekaviṣayakatvamuta bhinnaviṣayakatvamiti | pūrvapakṣastu | bhaktirharau na yuktā tasya rūpitve'rūpitve vā aṅgīkṛte doṣāpātena māhātmyāyogāt‌ | rūpitve caitravadanityatvaprasaṅgāt‌ | "nātmāśruteḥ'  ityatroktanityadehatvasyāpi ihākṣepāt‌ | "arūpamavyayam‌'  ityādiśrutyaprāmāṇyāpātācca | arūpitve ca saundaryasulakṣaṇatvādyayogāt‌ | rugmavarṇamādityavarṇamityādiśrutyaprāmāṇyaprasaṅgācca | naca rūpyarūpitvaśrutyoraprākṛtaprākṛtaviṣayatvenāvirodhānna ko'pi doṣa iti yuktaṃ kalpakābhāvāditi | siddhāntastu | "aikātmyapratyayasāram‌'  "ānandamātramajaram‌'  "ānandarūpamamṛtam‌'  ityādibhāṣyoktaśrutismṛtibalena jñānānandādyātmakadivyarūpaśravaṇena rūpaśrutestadviṣayatvasya ca "bhautikānīharūpāṇi bhūtebhyo'sau paro yataḥ | arūpavānataḥ'  ityādismṛtyā prakṛtyādyatītatvahetunā prākṛtarūpahīne gṛhāntaścākṣuṣaprakāśe satyapi bāhyaprakāśābhāvena prakāśo netyuktivat‌ arūpaśruteḥ aprākṛtavigrahe'pi harau prākṛtarūpābhāvaviṣayatayopapatteḥ | aprākṛtadivyavigrahasya ca anityatvādyanāpādakatvāt‌ saundaryādisambhavācca māhātmyavattvāt‌ yuktā tatra bhaktiriti | phale tu bhaktyākṣepasamādhī || 9 ||
|| oṃ ata eva copamā sūryakādivat‌ oṃ || 10 ||
yatpūrvanayadvaye bhagavadaṃśānāṃ "amātro'nantamātraḥ' itayantaṃ aikyakathanaṃ yaccārūpavaditi prākṛtarūparāhityakathanaṃ tadubhayamayuktam‌ | jīvasya bhagavadaṃśānāṃ bhedābhedāt‌ prākṛtarūpavattvācceti śaṅkanātsaṅgatiḥ | athapadoktabhaktirayuktota yukteti cintā | tadarthaṃ kiṃ jīvaḥ paramātmanā bhinnābhinno bhinna eveti | tadarthaṃ kiṃ paramātmanā jīvānāṃ samudāyasamudāyibhāvo'tha bimbapratibimbabhāva iti | tadarthaṃ pratibimbatvaṃ kimupādhikṛtatvādirūpamuta tadadhīnatvādirūpamiti | ādye tvanādinityajīvānāmaupādhikapratibimbatvāyogena samudāyitvarūpatayā samudāyarūpeśvareṇa bhedābhedau aupādhikabhede satyapi svābhāvikasvābhedavati svasminniva bhaktirayuktetyādye phalam‌ | ante tu tadadhīnatvādirūpapratibimbatvasya jīvānāṃ yuktatvenātyantabhinne harau bhaktiryukteti phalam‌ | pūrvapakṣastu | pṛthagupadeśādityatra jīveśabhedoktāvapi sarvacetanasamudāyasyeśatvena ekaikajīvasya paramātmanā bhedābhede'pi bhedāvirodhāt‌ | aupādhikabhedasya ca svābhāvikabhedakāryāpratibandhakatvena svābhinne tasminbhaktirayukteti | na ca bhedo'siddhaḥ cetanatvādaṃśatvānmatsyādivaditi tatsiddheḥ | bimbapratibimbabhāvastu aupādhiko'nādinityayorjīveśayorna yukta iti | siddhāntastu | jīvo neśābhinnaḥ tatpratibimbatvāt‌ | yo yatpratibimbaḥ sa tenābhinno na bhavati yathā sūryakādiritayanumānavirodhena cetanatvāderabhedāsādhakatvāt‌ | nacāsiddho hetuḥ | jīvasyāpīśvarasvarūpāṃśatve matsyādisāmyāpattyā jñānaiśvaryādyaviśeṣaprasaṅgarūpayuktyā "rūpaṃrūpaṃ pratirūpo babhūva'  iti śrutyā "bahavassūryakā yadvatsūryasya sadṛśā jale' iti smṛtyā ca tatsiddheḥ | na cānādinityasyaupādhikapratibimbatvāyogaḥ | "pṛthagupadeśāt‌'  "sandhye sṛṣyirāha hi'  "tadguṇasāratvāt‌'  ityuktaiḥ bhinnatvatadadhīnatvatatsadṛśaireva dharmai pratibiṃbatvopagamāditi || 10 ||
|| oṃ ambuvadagrahaṇāttu na tathātvam‌ oṃ || 11 ||
pūrvatra bimbasādṛśyādinā jīvasyeśapratibimbatvoktau tarhi jñānānandādirūpasādṛśyarūpamuktenirtyasiddhatvānna tadarthā bhaktiḥ kāryeti śaṅkodayātsaṅgatiḥ | atra athapadokteśvarabhaktiḥ na kāryota kāryeti cintā | tadarthaṃ īśajñānatatprasādadvārā jñānānandādibrahmasādṛśyābhivyakteḥ śrutaṃ phalatvaṃ na sambhavatyuta sambhavatīti | pūrvapakṣastu | jñānānandādibrahmasādṛśyasya "puṃstvādivattvasya'  ityatroktadiśā jīvasvarūpatayā nityasiddhatvāttadabhivyaktestu "anubandhādibhyaḥ  iti vakṣyamāṇadiśā jñānahetubhaktiphalatvāyogāt‌ | suptāviva kadācit‌ svayameva sambhavena ca
īśvarāparokṣajñānatatprasādadvārāpi bhaktiphalatvaṃ tasya na yuktamiti phalābhāvānna kāryā bhaktiriti | siddhāntastu | sādṛśyasya nityasiddhatve'pi īṣadabhivyakteḥ bhaktiṃ vinā suptau bhāve'pi samyagabhivyaktirūpamukteḥ "yamevaiṣa'  itayādibhāṣyoktaśrutismṛtibalena bhaktisādhyatvāttadarthaṃ bhaktiravaśyaṃ kāyareti || 11 ||
|| oṃ vṛddhihrāsabhāktvamantarbhāvādubhayasāmañjasyādevam‌ oṃ || 12 ||
|| oṃ darśanācca oṃ ||
atra prāk‌ phalahetutvena uktabhakteḥ tadupalakṣitatvena sādhanajātasya taratamabhāvanirūpaṇātsaṅgatiḥ | pūrvasūtroktaṃ bhaktayupalakṣitabhaktyādisādhanajātaṃ kiṃ bhakteṣvekaprakāramutānekaprakāramiti cintā | tadarthaṃ tatphalatayoktānandādibrahmasādṛśyābhivyaktiḥ sarvatraikaprakārā utānekaprakārā iti | saiṣānandasyetyādeḥ vairāgyapraśaṃsāparatvena ānandatāratamye mānābhāvena phalatāratamyāsiddhayā bhaktayāditatsādhanamapi bhakteṣvekaprakārameva phalavaiṣamye'pi sādhanāvaiṣamye bādhakābhāvācceti pūrvapakṣaḥ | siddhāntastu | saiṣetiśruteḥ vairāgyapraśaṃsādiparatvasya tadvākyavaiyarthyādinā'nyatra bṛhadbhāṣyādisthaleṣu bhāṣyakāraireva nirāsānnyāyāmṛte'pi  khaṇḍitatvāt‌ saiṣetyādibhāṣyoktaśrutyādisiddhānandādirūpaphalatāratamyānyathānupapattyā tattsādhaneṣu tāratamyamavaśyamabhyupeyam‌ | anyathā viṣamaphaladāturīśvarasya vaiṣamyādidoṣāpātāt‌ | sa ca netyuktaṃ vaiṣamyanaye  | sādhanasyottamatvenetibhāṣyoktasmṛtivirodhācceti | ānandādiśrutermuktaparatvaṃ ca "akṣaradhiyāṃ tu'  ityatrāgre vyaktam‌ || 12 ||
|| oṃ prakṛtaitāvatvaṃ hi pratiṣedhati tato bravīti ca bhūyaḥ oṃ || 13 ||
pūrvatra śrautānandatāratamyānyathānupapattyā taddhetutāratamyanirṇaya ivehāpi śrutāvadhikavyāpāroktyanyathānupapattyā pālanākhyāpāranirṇayātsaṅgatiḥ | athapadoktabhaktirayuktota yukta iti cintā | tadarthaṃ pālakākhyaguṇo harerna yukta uta yukta iti | tadarthaṃ pālanaṃ nāma kaścidvayāpāro nāstyutāstīti | pūrvapakṣastu | sṛṣyasya vastuno yāvatsaṃhāraṃ sthiteḥ svata eva siddheḥ saṃhārādikartuḥ asaṃhārādyatirikto rakṣārūpo vyāpāra eva neti na pālakatvaṃ hareryuktamiti | siddhāntastu | yuktaiva harau bhaktiḥ pālakatvākhyaguṇavattvāt‌ | na cāsaṃhārātiriktaḥ sa neti vācyam‌ | "naitāvadenāparonyadastyukṣā sa dyāvā pṛthivī' iti  śrutau pūrvatra "asyedeṣā sumatiḥ paprathānā'  ityādinoktasṛṣyisaṃhārakartṛtvasya "naitāvad‌' ityanena tāvanmātratvaṃ niṣidhya "paro'nyadastītyādinā pālanākhyavyāpārāntarasyāpyuktyā annādidānena poṣaṇādirūpavyāpārāntarasya pālanasya sambhavāt‌ sṛṣyiṃ ca pālanaṃ caiveti smṛteścaiveti | phale tu vyakte || 13 ||
|| oṃ tadavyaktamāhahi oṃ || 14 ||
|| oṃ api saṃrādhane pratyakṣānumānābhyām‌ oṃ || oṃ prakāśavaccāvaiśeṣyam‌ oṃ || oṃ prakāśaśca karmaṇyabhyāsāt‌ oṃ || oṃ ato'nantena tathāhi liṅgam‌ oṃ ||
pūrvatra janmādisūtroktasṛṣyayādyaṣyake pālakatvaṃ prasādhya atra svajñānadatvamiha sādhyata iti saṅgatiḥ | atrāthapadoktabhagavadbhaktiḥ na kāryota kāryeti cintā | tadatharṃ kimaphalota phalavatīti | sā kiṃ īśasya vyaktatve'vyaktatve cobhayathāpi paramapumarthahetvīśāparokṣaheturuta avyaktatve'pi īśvaraprasādadvārā taddheturiti | pūrvapakṣastu | hareḥ stambhādivatpratyakṣayogyatve stambhādipratyakṣasyeva bhaktiṃ vināpi pratyakṣatvasambhavāt‌ avyaktaikasvabhāvatve dharmādiriva bhaktyādisādhanenāpi pratyakṣatvāsambhavāt‌ anyathā tādṛśasyāpi pratyakṣatve samārādhanenāpi yakṣasākṣātkāravadīśvarasākṣātkārasambhavāt‌
agnyādibhūtānāṃ sthūlasūkṣmaviśeṣeṇa pratyakṣāpratyakṣatvavanmūlarūpeṇa avyaktasyāpi gṛhītarūpeṇa pratyakṣatvasambhavāt‌ na bhaktiḥ aparokṣaheturiti vyarthā satī na kāryeti | siddhāntastu "arūpamakṣaraṃ brahma sadā'vyaktaḥ' iti  śrutyā avyaktaikasvabhāvasyāpi "ātmā vā are draṣyavyaḥ śrotavyaḥ'  ityādiśrutibalena bhaktipūrvakaśravaṇādyābhyāsātprasannena acintyaśaktikeśvareṇaiva tadāparokṣasya "nityāvyakto'pi bhagavānīkṣyate nijaśaktitaḥ' iti  śrutyuktena mokṣasya jñānajaprasādasādhyatvaliṅgena avyaktatvapratyakṣatvaśrutidvayānyathānupapattyā ca siddhatvāt‌ | "na tamarādhayitvāpi kaścidvayaktīkariṣyati'  iti smṛtisiddhamahatpratyakṣāt‌ avyaktatvaliṅgena sūkṣmatvānumānāt‌ bhaktihīnārādhanena "nāsau sūkṣmo na sthūlaḥ' ityādi  śrutyādito gṛhītarūpeṇa ca īśapratyakṣatvasya abhāvāvagamāt‌ bhaktireva śravaṇādiyuktā bhagavadaparokṣaheturiti arthavatī bhaktiravaśyaṃ kāryeti || 14 ||
|| oṃ ubhayavyapadeśāttvahikuṇḍalavat‌ oṃ || 15 ||
|| oṃ prakāśāśrayavadvā tejastvāt‌ oṃ || oṃ pūrvavadvā oṃ || oṃ pratiṣedhācca oṃ ||
pūrvatra hareravyaktatve pratyakṣatve ca mānābhāvena avyaktasyāpi vyaktatvoktivadihāpi guṇātmatve'pi guṇitve cobhayatra mānābhāvāt‌ guṇātmano'pi guṇitvamiti kathanātsaṅgatiḥ | athapadoktā haribhaktirna kāyarota kāryeti cintā | tadarthaṃ tasya guṇatvaṃ nāstyutāstīti | tadarthaṃ guṇātmano hareḥ guṇitvaṃ sambhāvanāpramāṇābhyāṃ hīnaṃ yuktiviruddhaṃ cātha viparītamiti | pūrvapakṣastu | tadātmake tadvattvasya kvāpyadarśanāt‌ guṇarūpasya hareḥ guṇavattvamasambhāvitaṃ ata eva pramāṇaśūnyaṃ ca | harirna guṇavān‌ guṇātmakatvāt‌ yathā na svaḥ svavān‌ yo yadvān‌ sa tadātmako na yathā daṇḍavān‌ caitraḥ iti vā ityādi yuktiviruddhaṃ ca | naca guṇātmakatvamasiddhaṃ samavāyābhyupagamāccetyatra  samavāyāpākaraṇena "ekamevādvitīyam‌'  iti guṇaguṇyādibhedaniṣedhena tatsiddheḥ | ato guṇitvābhāvāt‌ na harau bhaktiḥ kāryeti | siddhāntastu | kuṇḍalātmakasyāpyaheḥ viśeṣabalātkuṇḍalitvavat‌ prakāśātmakasyāpyādityasya prakāśitvavat‌ kālasya pūrveṇābhede'pi pūrvaḥ kālaḥ iti viśeṣaṇaviśeṣabhāvavacca guṇātmano'pi hareḥ guṇitvaṃ pratinidhiviśeṣabalānnāsambhāvitam‌ | "ānandaṃ brahmaṇo vidvān‌'  "athaiṣa paramaḥ ānandaḥ' iti guṇatvaguṇitvobhayavyapadeśarūpapramāṇācca "śāstrayoni'  nayanyāyena yuktyagamyatayā "śruteḥ' tu ityatroktanyāyena  yuktyaviruddhaṃ ceti harau bhaktiḥ kāryeti || 15 ||
|| oṃ paramataḥ setūnmānasambandhabhedavyapadeśebhyaḥ oṃ || 16 ||
|| oṃ darśanāt‌ oṃ || oṃ buddhayarthaḥ pādavat‌ oṃ ||
pūrvatreśaguṇānāṃ tadātmakatvaṃ taddharmavattvaṃ coktam‌ | iha tu alaukikatvaṃ laukikaśabdavācyatvaṃ cocyata iti saṅgatiḥ | athapadoktā harāvatiśayitabhaktiḥ na kāryota kāryeti cintā | tadarthaṃ tadīyaguṇajātaṃ laukikamutālaukikamiti | tadarthaṃ tasyālaukikatve jñānānandāditattatpadavācyatvaṃ ayuktamutayuktamiti | pūrvapakṣastu | ghaṭavilakṣaṇo ghaṭapadavācyatvādṛṣyayā bhagavadānandādau ca "ānandaṃ brahmaṇaḥ' ityādau ānandādipadaprayogeṇa tadanyathānupapattyā'laukikatve mānābhāvena ca tadguṇājātaṃ laukikameva | anyathā tatra tatpadaprayogāyoga iti | siddhāntastu | "eṣaseturvidhṛtiḥ'  "ya eṣa ānandaḥ parasya' iti "yato vāco nivartante'  iti "etasyaivānandasyānyāni anyajjñānaṃ parasya ca' iti "adṛṣyamavyavahāryamavyapadeśyam‌'  ityādau brahmānandādiguṇeṣu laukikānandādāvasambhāvyamānasetutvonmānatvaśabditapūrṇatvabimbatvalokavilakṣaṇādṛṣyatvāvyapadeśyatvādidharmāṇāṃ uktyā alaukikatvāvaśyambhāvini tattacchabdaprayogasya jīveśvarayoraṃśāśitvajñāpanārthatvena prapdhipādavilakṣaṇe'pi jīve pādo'sya viśvabhūtānītyādau pādaśabdaprayogasya jīveśaguṇānāṃ arthaprakāśakatvānukūlavedyatvādijñāpanārthatvenopapatteḥ | "alaukiko'pi jñānādiḥ tacchabdenaiva bhaṇyate | jñāpanārthāya lokasya' iti smṛteśca alaukikameveśaguṇajātamiti yuktā tatrātiśayabhaktiriti
| vyutpattireva padānāṃ tatrāyukteti tu "kāryākhyānād‌' ityatra  nirasiṣyate || 16 ||
|| oṃ sthānaviśeṣātprakāśādivat‌ oṃ || 17 ||
|| oṃ upapatteśca oṃ ||
prāguktabimbatvahetukālaukikatvasyeha ākṣepeṇa śaṅkodayātsaṅgatiḥ | athapadoktātiśayitabhaktisiddhayarthaṃ prāguktamīśaguṇānāṃ bimbatvahetukamalaukikatvaṃ na yuktamuta yuktamiti cintā | tadarthaṃ bhagavadānandādiguṇasya vicitrabrahmādyānandādibimbatvamayuktamuta yuktamiti | tadarthaṃ pratibimbavaicityameva kāraṇamutānyadapīti | pūrvapakṣastu | "vṛddhihrāsabhāktvam‌' ityatroktadi  brahmādyānandādīnāṃ mitho vicitratvātteṣāṃ ceśaguṇapratibimbatve tāṃśca pratīśaguṇānāṃ bimbatve ca tadvadeva vaicityaṃ syāt‌ | "adṛṣyāniyamād‌' ityatra  adṛṣyādivaicityeṇa pratibimbavaicityoktāvapi svābhāvikānandāderādimatkāraṇakatvāyoga iti tasyaivehākṣepeṇa bimbavaicityanibandhanatvātpratibimbavaicityasya tathāca bhagavadrūpeṣu guṇatāratamyaṃ ca syāt‌ | tacca netyuktam‌ "na sthānataḥ'  ityatra | ato bimbatvābhāvānneśaguṇānāmalaukikatvamiti na tatrātiśayitabhaktiḥ kāryeti | siddhāntastu | bimbavaicityābhāve'pi sūryakāntādisthānaguṇena sūryādipratibimbatve'gnijanakatvādivaicityavat‌ brahmādyanādibhaktyādisthānaguṇavaicityeṇa bimbabhūtabhagavadaiśvaryavaśena ca avicitrabhagavadānandādipratibimbavaicityasyopapatteḥ | "brahmādiguṇavaiśeṣyād‌'  iti "aiśvaryātparamādviṣṇoḥ' iti ca  smṛteśca īśaguṇānāmavaicitye'pi bimbatvaṃ yuktamityalaukikatvādatiśayitā bhaktirāvaśyakīti || 17 ||
|| oṃ tathānyatpratiṣedhāt‌ oṃ || 18 ||
sthānaviśeṣādityasya bimbapratibimbabhāvarūpahetusādhakatayā "paramate'  ityanenaikārthenāvyavadhāyakatvāt‌ paramata ityatroktanyāyātideśakatvātsaṅgatiḥ | athapadoktā bhaktiḥ na kāryota kāryeti cintā | tadarthaṃ brahma vyaktamutāvyaktamiti | tadatharṃ dhyānapratītameve brahmota tadanyaditi | pūrvapakṣastu | "phalamate' iti  vakṣyamāṇadiśā brahmaṇa eva phalapradatvāt‌ "taṃ yathāyathā' ityāderupāsitasyaiva phaladātṛtvāt‌ dhyātasyābrahmatve tasyāḥ phaladātṛtvena dhyānavaiyarthyāt‌ dhyātṛpuruṣabahutvaprayuktadhyeyabahutvasya "na sthānataḥ' ityuktanyāyena bhedahīne'pi harau tadrūpāṇāṃ nānānvenopapatteśca dhyānakālapratītameva brahmeti bhaktiṃ vināpi puruṣaprayatnena vyaktatvasambhavānna tatra bhaktirāvaśyakīti | siddhāntastu | "setūnmānatvādinā' īśaguṇajātasya lokato'nyatvavat‌ "arūpavadeva' ityādāvuktadiśā aprākṛtanirdoṣasarvasulakṣaṇadivyasvarūpavigrahasya hareḥ nānādurlakṣaṇayuktadhyānapratītervastutvāyogena tadanyatvāvaśyaṃbhāvāt‌ "tadeve brahma tvaṃ viddhi nedaṃ yadidamupāsate' iti  śrutau dhyānapratītasya brahmatvaṃ pratiṣidhya tadanyadeva brahmetyuktatvācca dhyānapratītādanyadevāvyaktaṃ brahmeti | naca phalābhāvāpātaḥ | "paśyanti paramaṃ brahma' iti  smṛtyā dhyānakāle pratītavāsanāmayapratimāsthasyaiva phalapradatvopapatteḥ bhaktirāvaśyakīti || 18 ||
|| oṃ anena sarvagatamāyāmayaśabdādibhyaḥ oṃ || 19 ||
evamavyaktatve'pi hareḥ prasannasya aparokṣadānamiva svasvātantyamapi anyasya kvaciddadyāditi śaṅkanātsaṅgatiḥ | athapadoktaharāvevātiśayitabhaktiḥ na yuktota yukteti cintā | tadarthaṃ kiṃ kvacit‌ kadācidanyo'pi kartā uta sarvatra sarvadā sarvavastunaḥ harireva sṛṣyayādikarteti | tadarthaṃ svasvātantyaṃ kadācidanyasyāpi dadātyuta neti | pūrvapakṣastu | hareḥ kṛṣṇādyavatāreṣu parapreṣyatvādyasambhāvitavividhalīlādarśanena kvacitkadācit‌ svātantyasyāpyanyasya dānasambhavāt‌ harereva sarvasṛṣyayādau mānābhāvālloke rājādīnāṃ khaṇḍeśatvadarśanāt‌ deśāntarādāvanyo'pi sṛṣyayādikataretyanumānasambhavācca na harāvevātiśayitā bhaktiryukteti | siddhāntastu | "svātantātkrīḍate viṣṇuḥ na hi svātantyamanyagam‌ | karoti kvāpi'
 ityāderatyaktasvātantyasyaiva krīḍāvagamādanyatra svātantyadānasyāsambhāvitatvāt‌ harireva sarvakartetyasya "eṣa sarvagataḥ' ityādibhāṣyoktaśrutisiddhatvādanumānasya etacchatibalena kartrantare mānābhāvācca harireva sṛṣyayādikarteti yuktā tatraivātiśayitā bhaktiriti || 19 ||
|| oṃ phalamata upapatteḥ oṃ || 20 ||
|| oṃ śrutatvācca oṃ || dharmaṃ jaiminirata eva oṃ || oṃ pūrvaṃ tu bādarāyaṇo hetuvyapadeśāt‌ oṃ ||
evaṃ harereva sarvakartṛtve tasya vaiṣamyādidoṣanirāsāya pūrvoktadiśā karmasāpekṣatve tarhi karmaṇa evāstu phalapradatvamiti vā karmaṇa eva phalodayāt‌ prāguktaṃ hareḥ sarvasraṣyṛtvamayuktamiti vā śaṅkanātsaṅgatiḥ | athapadoktā bhaktiḥ na kāyarota kāryeti cintā | tadarthaṃ kiṃ karmaiva phaladātṛharistatra śeṣabhūta uta viparītamiti | tadarthaṃ "rātirdātuḥ parāyaṇam‌' iti  śrutaṃ hareḥ phaladātṛtvaṃ gauṇamuta mukhyamiti | tadarthaṃ karmaṇaḥ phalānvayavyatirekau śeṣitvakalpakau uta śeṣatayāpi yuktāviti | tadarthaṃ karmaṇaḥ śeṣitvakalpakaṃ nāstyutāstīti | pūrvapakṣastu | īśvaraḥ phaladātetipakṣe'pi karmaṇo'vaiyarthyāya vedaprāmāṇyāya hareḥ vaiṣamyādyabhāvāya kṛtakamarṇaḥ phaladṛṣyayā ca karmāpekṣatave'vaśyaṃbhāvini phalasya karmānvayavyatirekitvāt‌ tasya ca anyathāsiddhikalpakābhāvāt‌ "rātirdātuḥ' iti śruteḥ "eṣahyeva sādhukarma kārayati'  ityādinā hareḥ phalapradakarmahetutayā gauṇatvenāpyupapatteḥ āptatamagautamajaiminimatatvācca karmaiva prādhānyena phalapradaṃ haristu karmaśeṣa iti na tatra bhaktiḥ kāryeti | siddhāntastu | cetanatvayuktyā "puṇyena puṇyaṃ lokaṃ nayati'  ityādiviśeṣaśrutyā ca "rātirdātuḥ' iti śruterbalavattayā gauṇārthatvāyogāt‌ brahmaṇaḥ prādhānyena phaladātṛtve'pi karmaṇaḥ kāraṇatvenānvayavyatirekayoḥ jaiminimatasya copapatteḥ brahmaiva phalapradaṃ na karma kintu karaṇameveti yuktā tatra bhaktirita || 20 ||
|| iti śrīmadrāghavendrayatikṛtāyāṃ nyāyamuktāvalyāṃ tṛtīyādhyāyasya dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ samāptaḥ ||
|| atha tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ ||
prāguktāthapadārthavairāgyabhaktisādhyajñānahetujijñāsāśabditopāsanāsminpāde ucyate | sā ca dvedhā sarvasacchāstraśravaṇamananarūpā dhyānarūpā ceti | tatrādyā dhyānāṅgatvādādāvucyate |
|| oṃ sarvavedāntapratyayaṃ codanādyaviśeṣāt‌ oṃ || 1 ||
|| oṃ bhedānneti cedekasyāmapi oṃ || oṃ svādhyāyasya tathātvena hi samācāre'dhikārācca oṃ || oṃ salilavacca tanniyamaḥ oṃ || oṃ darśayati ca oṃ ||
atra jijñāsāśabdokte sarvasacchāstraśravaṇamanane na kārye uta kārye iti cintā | tadarthaṃ brahma svasvaśākhoktarītyā jñeyamuta sarvavedoktadiśeti | tadarthaṃ harejñareyatve sādhakaṃ nāstyuta bādhakaṃ cāsti atha viparītamiti | tadarthaṃ śākhābhedaḥ pratiśākhaṃ arthabhedoktiś‌ca niravakāśā uta sāvakāśeta | pūrvapakṣastu | brahma tathā jñeyamityatra sādhakābhāvāt‌ pratyuta vedarāśerekaikādhikārikatve kāṇvādiśākhābhedāyogasya vijñānamānandaṃ brahmetyādirūpeṇa pratiśākhamarthabhedoktyayogasya ekaikasya sarvaśākhādhyayanābhāvena tadarthaṃ jñānāyogasya mandānāṃ brahmajñānavaimukhyaprasaṅgarūpasya ca bādhakasyābhāvāt‌ tattacchākhoktameva tena tena jñeyamiti noktarūpaśravaṇamanane kārye iti | siddhāntastu | bhagavajjñānasampādanārthaṃ pravṛttānāṃ "ātmetyevopāsīta'  ityevamādikāṇvāditattacchākhāgatavidhīnāṃ jñānārthaṃ pravṛttavidhyarthamuktānāṃ "atra hyete sarva ekaṃ bhavantīti'  "na hyāsya karma kṣīyate'  ityādi tatra vidhisamīpā nātārthavādānāṃ ca tathā "svādhyāyo'dhyetavyaḥ'
 "vedaḥ kṛtsno'dhigantavyaḥ sarahasyo dvijñanmanā' ityevamādeḥ arthāvabodhaphalakasvādhyāyādhyayanavidheśca sarvādhikārisādhāraṇyena "sarvavedoktamārgeṇa karma kurvīta' iti  smṛtyā sarvavedoktamārgānuṣṭhāne sarveṣāmadhikārāvagamena ca tadanyathānupapattirūpayuktyā ca "sarvavedānadhītya vicāyar ca jñātumicchenmumukṣuḥ' iti  śrutyā "sarvān‌ vedān‌ setihāsān‌' iti  smṛtyā caikaikasya sarvaśākhādhyayanadvārā taduktārthajñānādhikārāvagamena sādhakasadbhāvāt‌ "śākhābhedo hyaśaktijaḥ' iti  smṛtyā kāṇvādiśākhābhedasya aśaktihetukatvena pratiśākhamarthabhedokteḥ ekaśākhasthārthabhedoktivat‌ prasaṅgabhedādanuvādādirūpatayā sarvavedoktarītyā brahma jñeyamiti niyamasya sarvāsāmapāṃ samudra ekāyanamityuktaniyamasyeva "yathā nadīnāṃ salilaṃ śakye sāgaragaṃ bhavet‌' iti  smṛtyā tattacchaktyanusāritayopapattyā prāguktabādhakānāmabhāvācca sarvavedoktadiśā yathāśakti sarvasacchāstraśravaṇamanane sarvairapi kārye eveti | phale tu jijñāsāśabdārthaikadeśākṣepasamādhī || 1 || || oṃ upasaṃhāro'rthābhedādviśeṣavatsamāne ca oṃ || 2 ||
|| oṃ anyathātvaṃ ca śabdāditi cennāviśeṣāt‌ oṃ || oṃ na vā prakaraṇabhedātparovarīyastvādivat‌ oṃ || oṃ saṃjñātaścettaduktamasti tu tadapi oṃ ||
vaiyarthyahetunā prāguktayathāśaktisarvavedārthaparijñānasyākṣepeṇeha śaṅkanātsaṅgatiḥ | atra pūrvoktasarvavedoktaparijñānaṃ nāvaśyakamutāvaśyakamiti cintā | tadarthaṃ sārvatrikaguṇopasaṃhārānupasaṃhārābhyāṃ vedāthūbhūtabrahmadhyānaṃ na yuktamutamayuktamiti | pūrvapakṣastu nobhayathāpi dhyānaṃ sambhavatīti | tathāhi | na tāvaduṣasaṃhṛtya dhyānaṃ kāryamiti pakṣo yuktaḥ | sarvaparijñānasya kramabhāvitvena śakyatve'pi sārvatrikasarvaguṇopasaṃhāreṇa dhyānasya vikṣipyoktaguṇānāmekabuddhayupārūḍhatvarūpasyāśakyatvāt‌ | sarvāśakyatve'pi kāṃścitprati tathā dhyānaṃ śakyamiti kalpanasya kalpakaśūnyatayā'yogāt‌ | ātmetyevopāsīteti sāvadhāraṇātmatvamātropāsanavidhivākyaviruddhaṃ ca parovarīyaḥprabhṛtiprakaraṇabhedāyogāt‌ | nāpyanupasaṃhṛtya dhyānaṃ kāryamiti pakṣaḥ spaṣyapramāṇābhāvāt‌ | ātmetyevetyatra evakārasya ayogavyavacchedakayāpi sāvakāśatopapatteḥ | naca parevarīyovaiśvānarādiprakaraṇabheda evānupasaṃhāre mānamanyathā sa na syāditi yuktam‌ | ekasminnapi prakaraṇe sarvaguṇokterapi sambhavāt‌ | ata ubhayathāpi dhyānasyāyuktatvāt‌ yaddhayānāṅgatayā prāguktaṃ śaktānāṃ sarvavedārthaparijñānaṃ sampādyamiti tatrānyataradapi vyarthatvānnāvaśyasampadyatāmarhatīti | siddhāntastu | ubhayathāpi dhyānaṃ sambhavatīti prāguktaṃ dvirūpamapi vedārthaparijñānaṃ yuktameveti | tathāhi | sarvavedoktaguṇānāṃ doṣābhāvānāṃ copasaṃhāreṇa dhyānasya "upāsya ekaḥ' iti bhāṣyokta  śrutau "sarve guṇāḥ sarvadaiva hyupāsyāḥ'  iti śrutau ca vihitatvāt‌ anyathā vihitakriyālopenānatharprasaṅgāt‌ | tathā chāndogye saptame "ṛgvedaṃ bhagavo'dhyemi'  ityādinā nāradenātmanaḥ sarvavidyāveditvamuktvā "so'haṃ bhagavo mantravidena nātmavid‌' iti  śrutyantare ca "so'haṃ nāmavideva nātmavid‌' ityuktyā ca sarvavidyānāṃ bhagavannāmatvena tadguṇābhidhāyakatvānyathānupapattiyuktyā ca tathā dhyānasya siddhatvāt‌ guṇābhidhānasya "adhyāyanāya prayojanābhāvād‌' iti  nyāyena dhyānārthatvāt‌ "nāma vā etāḥ brahmaṇaḥ sarvavidyāstasmādekaḥ sarvaguṇaivircintya' iti  śrutisiddhatvācca | ātmetyevetyevakārasyāyogavyavacchedakatvena avirodhakatvāt‌ prakaraṇabhedagateśca "puruṣavidyāyām‌'  ityatra vakṣyamāṇatvāt‌ upasaṃhṛtya dhyānaṃ kāryamiti pakṣo yuktaḥ | tathānupasaṃhṛtya dhyānaṃ kāryamiti pakṣo'pi yukta eva upasaṃhṛtya dhyānasya aśakyatvātprakaraṇabhedāt‌ | "sa eṣa parovarīyānudgīthaḥ sa eṣo'nantaḥ'  ityādi prakaraṇeṣu parovarīyastvādikatipayaguṇasyaivoktyā ekatra sarvaguṇokterabhāvāt‌ | tasmādubhayathā dhyānasya prāmāṇikatvena yuktatvāduktarūpavedārthaparijñānamarthavedeveti | phale tu pūrvākṣepasamādhī || 2 ||
|| oṃ prāpteśca samañjasam‌ oṃ || 3 ||
atra prāguktaguṇopasaṃhārānupasaṃhārapūrvakadhyānayoradhikāribhedena yuktatvasamarthanātsaṅgatiḥ | jijñāsāśabdābhimataṃ prāguktadvirūpadhyānaṃ ayuktamuta yuktamiti cintā | tadarthaṃ adhikāribhedena vyavasthā na sambhavatyuta sambhavatīti | tadarthaṃ vyavasthāpakaṃ nāstyutāstīti | pūrvapakṣastu | dvirūpadhyānasya virodhenaikasyāyogāt‌ adhikāribhedena vyavasthitatvasya cātrāsyādhikāro'tra
tasyeti vyavasthāpakapramāṇābhāvenāyogādayuktaṃ dvirūpadhyānamiti | siddhāntastu | "yasya yāvadguṇāḥ spaṣyaṃ pratibhāsantyupāsane yugapatsvabhujau yadvaddhayāyettāvata eva saḥ' iti  śrutyuktadiśā "guṇaissarvairupāsyosau brahmaṇaḥ' ityādi  smṛtyuktadiśā ca dhyānakāle ca yugapatspaṣyasarvaguṇapratītyā sarvaguṇapratītirūpayogyatāviśeṣayuktabrahmatadanyādhikāribhedena vyavasthopapatteḥ yuktaṃ dvirūpadhyānamiti || 3 ||
|| oṃ sarvābhedādanyatreme oṃ || 4 ||
atra pūrvatra sarvaguṇadhyānādhyānāyoravirodhoktau atrānyataravaiyarthyaṃ nirasyata iti saṅgatiḥ | atra jijñāsāpadoktaṃ pūrvatra prakṛtaṃ sarvaguṇadhyānaṃ na kāryamuta kāryamiti cintā | tadarthaṃ tasya phalātiśayavattvanirṇayo na sambhavatyuta sambhavatīti | tadarthaṃ tannirṇāyakaṃ nāstyutāstīti | pūrvapakṣastu | mokṣarūpaphalasyāsarvadhyānenāpi siddheḥ sarvaguṇadhyānasya asarvaguṇadhyānasādhyaphalātiśayavattvanirṇayasya ca nirṇāyakābhāvenāyogāt‌ vyarthaṃ tanna kāryamiti | siddhāntastu | sarvaguṇadhyānamasarvaguṇadhyānādadhikaphalavat‌ tato mahattvāt‌ | yatsādhanaṃ yato mahat‌ tattato'dhikaphalavat‌ yathā agniṣyomādito vājapeyādikamityanumānena "sampūrṇopāsanādbrahmā sampūṇarānandabhāgbhavet‌' ityādismṛtyā ca phalātiśayavattvaniṇaryopapatteḥ kāryameva sarvaguṇadhyānamiti || 4 ||
|| oṃ ānandādayaḥ pradhānasya oṃ || 5 ||
pūrvoktādhikāribhedena phalabhedena ca dvirūpopāstirayuktā sarvamumukṣusādhāraṇopāstimātrānirṇaye tadviśeṣānavasarāditi śaṅkanātsaṅgatiḥ | jijñāsāpadoktaṃ bhagavadvayākhyānaṃ na yuktamuta yuktamiti cintā | tadarthaṃ sarvamumukṣudhyeyaguṇanirṇayo na sambhavatyuta sambhavatīti | tadarthaṃ sarvadhyeyaguṇāḥ saṅkhayānirdeśābhyāṃ kl‌ptāḥ pṛthaksanti guṇāḥ | yadi bhaveyuḥ tarhi kiyantaḥ | yadi catuḥpañcaṣāḥ ānandādaya ityucyeta tarhi keṣāñcitsamuccaye'nyeṣāmapi samuccayaprasaṅgena niyamāsambhavāt‌ | ānandādibhyo'nya evetyapi vaktuṃ śakyatvena eta evetinirdeśakl‌ptyabhāvācceti sarvamumukṣudhyeyaguṇanirṇayābhāvānna bhagavaddhayānaṃ kāryamiti | siddhāntastu | sarveṣāṃ bahuguṇopāsane'śakyatvāt‌ sarvāpekṣitasya nirdoṣānandānubhavarūpamokṣasya "taṃ yathā yathopāsate' iti śrutyā tadanuguṇopāsanasādhyatvācca nirdoṣatvajñānānandānāṃ trayāṇāmāvaśyakatvāt‌ ātmatvaśabditasvāmitvasya svasambandhānusmaraṇasya bhagavatprītihetutvena "ātmeti tūpagacchanti'  iti vakṣyamāṇadiśāvaśyakatvāt‌ "saccidānanda ātmā'  ityādismṛteśca sadādayaścatvāra eveti saṅkhayānirdeśābhyāṃ kl‌ptisambhavena dhyeyaguṇanirṇayāt‌ yuktameva bhagavaddhayānamiti || 5 ||
|| oṃ priyaśirastvādyaprāptirupacayāpacayau hi bhede oṃ || 6 ||
catvāra eveti prāguktaniyamākṣepeṇa śaṅkanātsaṅgatiḥ | catvāra eveti prāguktaniyamo yukta utāyukta iti cintā | tadarthaṃ "priyameva śiraḥ' ityādinā śrutapriyaśirastvādayaḥ sarvopāsyā uta neti | tadarthaṃ teṣāṃ sarvānupāsyatve prāguktaphalaprāptirnāstyutāstīti | pūrvapakṣastu | priyaśirastvādayo'pi sarvāpāsyāsteṣāṃ ānandaviśeṣatvāt‌ | ānandādīnāṃ ca svābhimataphalāvāptyarthaṃ sarvopāsyatvokteḥ anyathā phalābhāvāpātāt‌ | ataḥ prāguktaniyamo na yukta iti | siddhāntastu | ānandaviśeṣamātreṇa priyaśirastvādeḥ sarvopāsyatve savarguṇānāmapi guṇacatuṣyayaviśeṣatvasya vaktuṃ śakyatayā sarveṣāṃ sarvaguṇopāstiprasaṅgāttasya ca "darśanācca' ityatrokta  ānandaśrutisiddhaphalabhedaparilopāpattyā aniṣyatvena phalabhedasiddhayarthaṃ guṇopāsaneti tāratamyasya ca vācyatvāt‌ "naiva sarvaguṇāssarvairupāsyā muktibhedataḥ' iti  smṛteśca na sarvopāsyāḥ priyaśirastvādayaḥ | nacaivaṃ phalābhāvaḥ | "yathāyogyān‌ guṇapūrṇānupāsya phalaṃ bhavet‌' ityādiśrutyā viśeṣānupāsane'pi yogyaguṇopāsanenaiva muktiphalaprāpteravagamāditi noktaniyamabhaṅga iti || 6 ||
|| oṃ itaretvarthasāmānyāt‌ oṃ || 7 ||
pūrvatra yathāyogyānitiśrutyā yogyaguṇopāsanenaiva phalaprāptyuktau devānāṃ caturguṇādhikaguṇopāsane yogyatāniścayāsambhavāt‌ teṣāṃ adhikaguṇopāstirayukteti śaṅkanātsaṅgatiḥ | jijñāsāpadoktaṃ adhikaguṇopāsanaṃ na kāryamuta kāryamiti cintā | tadarthaṃ tadadhikāriṇāṃ devānāṃ yogyatāniyamanirṇayo'śakya uta śakya iti | tadarthaṃ prāpteścetyatroktaṃ dhyānakālīnaguṇabhānasya yogyatānirṇāyakatvamayuktamuta yuktamiti | pūrvapakṣastu | dhyānātpūrvameva yogyatāniyamājñāne'yogyopāsanaprasaṅgāttasya ca "acetanāsatyāyogyānyanupāsyānyaphalatvaviparyayābhyām‌' iti sāṅkarṣaṇasūtroktadiśā anarthaphalatvena dhyākālīnaguṇabhānasya yogyatānirṇāyakatvābhāvānnādhikopāsanaṃ kāyarmiti | siddhāntastu | tattatprāpyaphalāpekṣayopāsanayogyatānirṇayopapatteḥ bhāvyutkarṣajñasarvaguruṇā tattatprāpyabhaviṣyatphalānusāriguṇopadeśopapatteśca caturbhyodhikopāsanaṃ kāryameveti || 7 ||
|| oṃ ādhyayanāya prayojanābhāvāt‌ oṃ || 8 ||
|| oṃ ātmaśabdācca oṃ ||
evaṃ pūrvatra mandamadhyottamādhikāribhedena anupasaṃhāropasaṃhārau uktvā tayoḥ "saṃjñātaścet‌' iti  "prakaraṇabhedāt‌' iti  ca yanmānamuktaṃ tadevehākṣipya samādhīyata iti saṅgatiḥ | atra jijñāsāpadābhimatau prāguktaguṇopasaṃhārānupasaṃhārau na kāryāvuta kāryau iti cintā | tadarthaṃ tayormānaṃ nāstyutāstīti | tadarthaṃ prāk‌ "saṃjñātaḥ' iti "prakaraṇabhedāt‌' iti coktamubhayatra mānaṃ na yuktamuta yuktamiti | pūrvapakṣastu | saṃjñātaścetyatroktadiśā sarvavidyānāṃ bhagavannāmatvena tatra guṇābhidhānasya upasaṃhārārthatve sarvaguṇānāṃ ekatraivoktiprasaṅgena vikṣipya nānāsthāneṣūkteḥ vyarthatvāt‌ mokṣasyānupasaṃhāreṇāpi siddhayā upasaṃhārasya vyarthatvācca | ata eva vyarthavidheḥ aśraddheyatvena "vidhiśeṣavat‌'  ityatroktavihitatvayukterapyaprayojakatvāt‌ tattadvidyāprakaraṇeṣu etāvadevopāsanamityuktyabhāvena prakaraṇabhedasyānupasaṃhāre'mānatvena mānābhāvānna kāryāvetau dvāviti | siddhāntastu | guṇokterupasaṃhārānyaprayojanābhāvāt‌ nānāsthāneṣūktānāmapi upasaṃhāre muktau phalātiśayabhāvena tathokterarthavattvāt‌ "ātmatvopāsanavidhānāt‌ prāgevakārasyāyogavyavacchedakatvoktāvapi iha "prāpteśca' ityatrokta  nyāyena adhikāribhedenāvyavacchedakatvāvirodhācca dvāvapi kāryāviti || 8 ||
|| oṃ ātmagṛhītiritaravattaduttarāt‌ oṃ || 9 ||
""ātmetyevopāsīta' iti pūrvoktaśrutisthaivakārasya svāmitvānyajñānādiguṇavyavacchedakatvaprāptāvatra sa netyucyata iti saṅgatiḥ | atra jijñāsāpadābhimataṃ "ānandādayaḥ'  ityatroktaṃ caturguṇopāsanaṃ na yuktamuta yuktamiti cintā | tadartha tatkiṃ prāguktaśrutiviruddhaṃ naveti | tadarthaṃ ātmetyeveti prāguktātmaśabdaḥ kiṃ suśakatvādiyuktyā svāmitvamātravacanaḥ uta vākyaśeṣabalāt‌ jñānādicaturguṇavacana iti | pūrvapakṣastu | ātmatvamātropāsteḥ suśakatvādāśvāparokṣyahetutvācca sāvadhāraṇaśrautātmapadasya svāmimātravacanatvena tanmātropāsanasyaiva kāryatvādayuktaṃ caturguṇopāsanam‌ | vākyaśeṣastu stutitvenāpyupapadyata iti | siddhāntastu atyalaukikaparamamuparthahetvāparokṣye'tisuśakatvasyāyuktatvāt‌ guṇatve'pi vā sarveṣāṃ mumukṣūṇāṃ "phalasāmyādapekṣitaḥ' ityānandanayoktanyāyena  svāpekṣitajñānānandādiphalaprāptyarthatvena anyopāsanasyāpyāvaśyakatvāt‌ vākyaśeṣasyārthavādatve'pi aviruddhe svārthe tātparyābhāvāt‌ | "prāṇanneva prāṇanāmā bhavati manvāno manaḥ' ityādipūrvavākye prāṇapadādinā prakṛtānandādiguṇānāṃ parāmarśena "atra hyete sarva ekaṃ bhavanti'  ityātmaguṇe ānandādiguṇānāmantarbhāvoktiparavākyaśeṣānurodhena "ānandānubhavatvācca' ityādi  smṛtyanurodhena ca ātmaśabdasya svāmitvādicaturgaṇavacanatvena tadavirodhādyuktaṃ sarvādhikārikacaturguṇopāsanamiti | na caivamanupasaṃhāramānatvoktivirodhaḥ asyānupasaṃhārarūpatvāt‌ || 9 ||
|| oṃ anvayādicetsyādavadhāraṇāt‌ oṃ || 10 ||
atrātmapadasya prāguktānupasaṃhāramānatvamākṣipya sādhyata iti saṅgatiḥ | atra prāguktamātmapadasyānupasaṃhāramānatvamayuktamuta yuktamiti cintā | tadarthamātmapadoktaguṇāḥ upasaṃhartṛmātropāsyāḥ utānupasaṃhartrūpāsyā aṣīti | tadarthaṃ ātmaśabdo'khilaguṇānāmapi pratyāyakatvenānupasaṃhartṝn‌ prati vibhramakaraḥ atha vyavasthayā tattadyogyaguṇapratyāyakatayā nirṇayakāra iti | pūrvapakṣastu | ātmetyevopāsītetyasya "codanādyaviśeṣāt‌'  iti nyāyena akhilaguṇadhyātṛbrahmādīn‌ pratyapi pravṛtteḥ tatra ātmaśabdasya anupasaṃhartṛmātrayogyaguṇabodhakatve ātmetyevetyavadhāraṇāyogena tadanyathānupapattyā "āptavyāpterātmaśabdaḥ paramasya prayujyate' iti smṛtyā ca ātmaśabdasya sarvaguṇavācitvenānupasaṃhartṝn‌ caturguṇādyupāsakān‌ prati tattadyogyamātraguṇāpratyāyakatayā tasya vibhramakaratvāt‌ taduktaguṇānupasaṃhartṛbhiranupāsyā ityayuktamanupasaṃhāramānatvam‌ | naca tasya sarvaguṇapratyāyakatve'pi svasvayogyaguṇamātraṃ sarvairupāsyamiti śaṅkayam‌ | ātmetyevetyavadhāraṇenātmaśabdasya yāvatpratītaṃ tāvatopyupāsyatvapratīteḥ | anyathāvadāraṇavirodhāditi | siddhāntastu | ātmaśabdasya sarvaguṇavācitve'pi tattadguṇopāsanayogyān‌ sarvaguṇān‌ vaktītyasya tattadanubhavasiddhatvena tasya avibhramakaratayā taduktaguṇānāṃ sarvopāsyatvādyuktamanupasaṃhāramānatvamiti || 10 ||
|| oṃ kāryākhyānādapūrvam‌ oṃ || 11 ||
pūrvatrādhikāriṇāṃ svasvayogyaguṇānyaguṇānāmātmaśabdenāpratītyā tattanmātraguṇopāsanaṃ yuktamityuktamayuktam‌ | yogyānāmapi "paramataḥ'  ityuktadiśā'laukikatvena ātmaśabdenāpratīteriti śaṅkanātsaṅgatiḥ | īśvare guṇā dhyeyā ityuktam‌ | te hi laukikā eva dhyeyā utālaukikā iti cintā | tadatharṃ laukikānāmadhyāne guṇadhyānasyaivābhāvaḥ prasajyata uta neti | tadarthaṃ alaukikaguṇānāmanubhavo na sambhavatyuta sambhavatīti | pūrvapakṣastu | alaukikaguṇaviṣaye pratyakṣānumānayoraprasakteḥ prasaktasya ca śabdasya saṅgatigrahaṇasāpekṣatvāt‌ tasya ca sambandhijñānādhīnatvāt‌ śabdarūpasambandhinaḥ pratyakṣatve'pi artharūpasambandhinaḥ śabdādanyenāpratīteḥ śabdāntareṇa tatpratītau tatrāpyevamityanavasthāpattyā saṅgatigrahaṇāyogena śabdenāpyanubhavayogena dhyānakāle tatpratīterabhāvāt‌ tādṛśasya ca dhyānāyogāt‌ laukikānāmadhyāne guṇadhyānasyaivābhāvaprasaktyā dhyānasādhyāparokṣajñānajanmano'bhāvāpātena guṇadhyānasyāvaśyakatvāt‌ laukikā eva dhyeyāḥ | tathā cāvidyamānadhyānasya "na pratīke na hi saḥ'  iti caturthādyapāde nirasiṣyamāṇatvena īśvare dhyeyānāṃ guṇānāṃ laukikatvasya vāstavatvāvaśyaṃbhāvāt‌ "paramatassetūnmāna' iti sūtre bhagavadguṇānāmuktālaukikatvamayuktamiti | siddhāntastu | alaukikāstasya guṇāhyupāsyāḥ' iti śrutau mokṣasyālaukikatvakathanāt‌ tādṛśamokṣasya cālaukikaguṇopāsanena vinā'yogāt‌ "arthasāmānyāt‌'  ityuktanyāyena phalānusāridhyānasya kartavyatayā alaukikā eva guṇā dhyeyāḥ | naca tādṛśaguṇāpratītirdoṣaḥ | "śāstrovastunaścaiva vyutpattiḥ śāstraliṅgataḥ' ityabhimāninayānuvyākhyānasudhayoruktarītyā "yaḥ sahasrākṣaḥ sa indraḥ yaḥ pāśahastaḥ sa varuṇa ityādau vākyārthamaryādayopasthite vastuni indrādiśabdasya vyutpattivadanukūlavedanīyo bhagavadguṇo yaḥ saḥ ānanda iti sākṣātkṛteśuṇānāṃ viduṣāmupadeśenopasthitau tatra tatpadaśaktigrahopapattyā śabdenaitatpratītisambhāt‌ prāguktaguṇānāmalaukikatvā yuktamiti || 11 ||
|| oṃ samāna evaṃ cābhedāt‌ oṃ || 12 ||
|| oṃ sambandhādevamanyatrāpi oṃ ||
arthasāmānyādityukta  nyāyena pūrvatrāpūrvakāryākhyānādapūvarguṇajātadhyānaṃ kāryamityuktamayuktam‌ | dhyānasya svānusāriphaladatvāniyamāditi śaṅkanātsaṅgatiḥ | atra "arthasāmānyāt‌' iti prāguktadhyānasya phalānusāritvaṃ nāstyutāstīti | tadarthaṃ tayoḥ samaviṣamaphalavatoḥ upāsanamapi samaviṣamarūpaṃ na sambhavatyuta sambhavatīti | tadarthaṃ
trivikramatvādikriyāviśeṣāṇāṃ brahmabrahmāṇībhyāṃ yathākramamupāsyānupāsyatve na yukte uta yukta iti | pūrvapakṣastu | trivikramatvādikriyāviśeṣāṇāmanityatvena dhyānakāle'bhāvādavidyamānasya ca ninditatvena brahmāṇībrahmaṇorapi kriyāviśeṣānupāsanasya vācyatvena guṇaviṣaye sāmyasya kriyāviśeṣaviṣaye vaiṣamyasya durvacanatvena prakārāntareṇa brahmāṇībrahmabhyāṃ kriyamāṇopāsanayoḥ samatvasya viṣamatvasya cābhāvena samaviṣamatvāyogāt‌ tadubhayaprāpyaphalasya sthānaikyaguṇatāratamyarūpatayā samaviṣamatvena tābhyāṃ kriyamāṇopāsanasya svasadṛśaphalānusāritvābhāvādarthasāmānyādityuktamayuktamiti | siddhāntastu | "guṇakriyādayo viṣṇoḥ svarūpaṃ nānyadiṣyate' iti tattvanirṇayodāhṛta śrutyā guṇakriyādīnāṃ viṣṇunā tādātmyarūpasambandhena nityatayā dhyānakāle asattvābhāvena tadupāsane brahmaṇo'sadupāsanāprasakteḥ | kriyādiviśeṣaṇāmalaukikatve'pi "guṇāstraivikramādyāśca saṃhartavyā na saṃśayaḥ | viriñcasyaiva nānyeṣām‌' iti  smṛtyā viriñcasyaiva yogatvena teṣāṃ brahmopāsyatvavat‌ brahmāṇyā nityavikrāntyādikriyāntarbhāvenaivopāsyatayā pṛthagupāsyatvābhāvena kriyāviṣaye viṣamatvasya guṇādiviṣaye samatvasya ca sambhavena brahmāṇībrahmabhyāṃ kriyamāṇopāsanasyāpi phalasyaiva samaviṣamatvena kvāpyupāsane phalānusāritvasya vyabhicārābhāvāt‌ arthasāmānyādityuktaṃ yuktamiti || 12 ||
|| oṃ navā viśeṣāt‌ oṃ || 13 ||
|| oṃ darśayati ca oṃ ||
"ātmetyevopāsīta' ityātmapadoktaguṇopāsanasyāvaśyakatvādātmapadena ca brahmāṇyabhimataguṇebhyo nyūnaguṇokterapi sambhavena sarvaguṇānāṃ kriyāsamanvayasya ca uktaniyamābhāvāt‌ brahmāṇyāḥ prāguktasarvaguṇopasaṃhāraṃ kriyāsāmānyopasaṃhāraścāyukta iti śaṅkanātsaṅgatiḥ | atrātmaśabdasya "ātmaśabdācca' ityatroktaṃ  anupasaṃhāramānatvaṃ na yuktamuta yuktamiti cintā | tadarthaṃ ātmaśabdoktāḥ guṇāḥ na sarvopāsyā iti | tadarthaṃ tasya guṇābhidhāyakatve'pi adhikāriṇaṃ prati tattadyogyaguṇābhidhāyakatvamapi prāguktaṃ na yuktamuta yuktamiti | tadarthaṃ tattadupāsanāyogyaguṇamātrābhidhāyakatvaniyamo na sambhavatyuta sambhavatīti | pūvarpakṣastu | ātmaśabdo brahmāṇaṃ prati sarvānīśvaradharmān‌ brahmāṇīṃ prati aśeṣaguṇān‌ kriyāsāmānyāni anyāṃśca prati tadupāsanāyogyaguṇāneva tannyūnādhikān‌ vaktīti pratisvikaniyamābhāvena prāguktatattadadhikāriyogyaguṇābhidhāyakatvasya "anvayāt‌' ityatroktasyāyogāt‌  na sarvopāsyā ātmaśabdoktā guṇā ityanupasaṃhāramānatvamātmaśabdāccetyatroktamayuktamiti | siddhāntastu | ātmaśabdasya sarvaguṇoktiśaktasyāpi tattadyogyaguṇapratyāyakatvānnyūnādhikasambhave'pi tattadadhikārisāmarthyavaśena ātmaśabdena yasya yāvanto guṇāḥ pratīyante tatra tāvanto dhyeyā iti vyavasthāsambhavāt‌ "sarvān‌ guṇān‌' itibhāṣyokta śrutibalāccātmaśabdoktaguṇāḥ sarvopāsyā eveti yuktamanupasaṃhāramānatvamiti || 13 ||
|| oṃ sambhṛtidyuvyāptyapi cātaḥ oṃ || 14 ||
atra "ukṣā sa dyāvā pṛthivī'  "eṣahi sarveṣu lokeṣu bhāti' iti  śrutasambhṛtyādiguṇasya sarvopāsyatvaśaṅkāyāṃ prāguktādhikāriviśeṣeṇa siddhāntakaraṇātsaṅgatiḥ | atra "ānandādayaḥ'  ityatroktaṃ caturguṇopāsanaṃ na yuktamuta yuktamiti cintā tadarthaṃ sambhṛtidyuvyāptī sarvopāsye uta neti | tadarthaṃ guṇopāstau tajjanyaphalāpekṣaiva heturuta yogyatāviśeṣo'pīti | pūrvapakṣastu | sarveṣāṃ mumukṣūṇāṃ mokṣe jñānānandāderiva svāvarasambharaṇasya prakāśavyāpterapekṣitatvāt‌ phalāpekṣayā eva ca tatsādhanānuṣṭhāne niyāmakatvāt‌ yogyatāviśeṣasya hetutve vā savareṣāmapi yogyatāviśeṣasya sattve'pi virodhābhāvāt‌ tadabhāve vā sādhanānuṣṭhonana tadanuguṇaphalaprāptivirodhācca apekṣitasambharaṇādiphalasya "yādṛśo bhāvitaḥ śrīśastādṛśo jīva ābhavet‌' iti  smṛtyā arthasāmānyādityuktanyāyena sambhṛtyādiguṇaviśiṣyatayā bhagavadupāsanenaiva prāpyatvācca sarvopāsye eva sambhṛtidyuvyāptī | nacaivaṃ "sarvaguṇā api' iti śaṅkayaṃ sarveṣāṃ sarvaguṇāpekṣābhāvāt‌ | ato na yuktaṃ sarveṣāṃ catvāra eva guṇā upāsyā iti | siddhāntastu | phalāpekṣāyā eva
tatsādhanānuṣṭhāne niyāmakatve traivarṇiketareṣāmapi svargahetuyāgādyanuṣṭhānātphalaprāptiprasaṅgena "anyathā anarthakṛdbhavet‌' itibhāṣyoktasmṛtāvayogyānuṣṭhānasyānarthaphalalatvoktyā ca yogyatāviśeṣasyaiva niyāmakatvāttasya ca "devādīnāmupāsyāstu bhṛtivyāptyādayo guṇāḥ' iti  smṛtyā devādīnāmeva bhāvena sarveṣāmabhāvāt‌ na sarvopāsye sambhṛtidyuvyāptī kintu dovopāsye iti yuktaṃ prāguktaṃ caturguṇopāsanam‌ | apekṣitaṃ prakāśādimātraṃ tu bhagavadrūpapratītyavinābhūtaprakāśādinaiva labhyamiti || 14 ||
|| oṃ puruṣavidyāyāmapi cetareṣāmanā nānāt‌ oṃ || 15 ||
evaṃ iyatā granthena suranaraviriñcirūpamadhyamādhamottamādhikāriṇāṃ bahvalpasarvaguṇadhyānāya trividhairapi svasvayogyaguṇā itastataḥ upasaṃhāryā iti yatsiddhaṃ tadayuktam‌ | ekaikatraiva vidyāyāṃ tattadyogyaguṇoktisambhavāditi śaṅkanātsaṅgatiḥ | atra "sarvavedānta' naye  trividhādhikāriṇāṃ yathāśaktiyatsarvaparijñānamuktaṃ tadayuktamuta yuktamiti cintā | tadatharṃ teṣāṃ svasvayogyaguṇānāmupāsanasya itastato nopasaṃhāryā utopasaṃhāryā iti | tadarthaṃ svasvayogyaguṇā ekaikatra vidyāyāmā nāyante atha neti | tadarthamekaikatra tattadyogyaguṇānāmanuktijñāpakaṃ nāstyutāstīti | pūrvapakṣastu | ekaikatra tattadyogyaguṇā nocyante ityatra jñāpakābhāvena ekaikatraiva tattadapekṣitaguṇoktisambhavena tanmātraparijñānenāpi svasvayogyadhyānaviṣayaguṇānāṃ buddhayārūḍhasambhavena kṛtyābhāvāt‌ na itastato dhyeyapuṃguṇā upasaṃhāryā iti trayāṇāṃ yathāśaktisarvaparijñānaṃ yuktamiti | siddhāntastu | "sarvataḥ pauruṣe sūkte' iti bhāṣyokta  smṛtyā sarvavidyottamapuruṣasūktavidyāyāmapi kaimutyasiddhatvena uttamādhikāriṇāṃ sarvato guṇopasaṃhārasyāvaśyakatvāt‌ madhyamādhikāriṇāmapi mahadalpaguṇābhidhāyakavidyāsu saṅkhayāsāmyasambhave'pi svasvābhimataguṇānāmekaikatraivoktiniyamābhāvāt‌ itastataḥ kāyar evopasaṃhāraḥ iti yuktaṃ sarveṣāṃ yathāśaktisarvaparijñānamiti || 15 ||
|| oṃ vedhādyarthabhedāt‌ oṃ || 16 ||
pūrvatra itastato'pekṣitaguṇopasaṃhārasya sarvaiḥ kartavyatvaprasaktau satyāṃ vedhādiguṇopasaṃhāro'pyapekṣitaphalahetutvāt‌ savaraiḥ kāryeti śaṅkanātsaṅgatiḥ | atra "ānandādayaḥ' ityatroktaḥ pūrvanaye prasaktaḥ sarveṣāṃ caturguṇopāstiniyamo na yukta uta yukta iti cintā | tadarthaṃ "taṃ pratyaṃcamarciṣā''vidhya marman‌'  ityādiśrutyukto vedhādiguṇaḥ kiṃ sarvopāsyo'tha neti | tadarthaṃ tatphale'pekṣāyogyatve staḥ uta neti | pūrvapakṣastu | vedhādibhagavadguṇopāsanāphalasya duṣyajananigrahasya sarvasajjanānāmapekṣitatvāt‌ "prāṇastvā vaṃśo hāsyatītyenaṃ brūyāt‌'  | "ātatāyinamāyāntaṃ hanyādevāvicārayan‌' iti śrutismṛtyoḥ vihitatvena yogyatāvattvasyāpyavagamācca vedhādeḥ sarvopāsyatvātprāguktaniyamo na yukta iti | siddhāntastu | "bhindhi viddhi śruṇīhi' iti  smṛtau vedhādiguṇopāstiphalasya yatiprāpyaphalādvilakṣaṇaphalatvoktipūrvaṃ yatīnāṃ tatrāyogyatvasyoktatvāt‌ | tata eva tatrāpekṣāyā apyayogāt‌ | na śa noṣīti māmāttha hāsyatastvāmato hi tau | jñānasāmarthyāvānitthaṃ brūyāddevādiruttamaḥ'  iti smṛtyā devaviṣayatvena smṛteśca kṣatriyaparatvena ca sarveṣāṃ yogyatvānirṇāyakatvāt‌ yogyatāpekṣayorabhāve tannibandhanavedhādiguṇopāsteḥ abhāvasiddheryuktaḥ prāguktaniyama iti || 16 ||
|| oṃ hānautūpāyanaśabdaśeṣatvātkuśācchaṃdastutyupagānavattaduktam‌ oṃ || 17 ||
|| oṃ sāmparāye tartavyābhāvāttathāhyanye oṃ ||
evamatītairnayaiḥ trividhādhikāriṇāmupāsanopayuktatattadyogyaguṇopasaṃhāro nirṇītaḥ | itaḥ paraṃ upāsanāyāḥ phalanaiyatyayogyatātāratamyādhikāreyattāgurupradānādītikartavyatādikaṃ nirūpayituṃ atropāsanāyāḥ nirupādhikatvarūpasvabhāva
ucyate iti saṅgatiḥ | yadarthaṃ pūrvaṃ guṇopasaṃhāro nirṇītaḥ yasmācca atharsāmānyādityuktasvasadṛśaphalatvaṃ "samāna evaṃ ca' ityatra  samarthitaṃ sopāsanā muktena na kriyata uta kriyata iti cintā | tadarthaṃ muktena kriyata ityatra pramāṇaṃ nāstyutāstīti | tadarthaṃ "etatsāmagāyannāste' iti  śrutiḥ gauṇamuktiparā uta paramamuktipareti | tathā kriyate ityatrāpi kiṃ vidhyādyupādhīnā kriyate atha kuśāccandastutyupagānavadīśvarakriyāvat‌ svecchayā līlarūpatayeti cintā | tadatharṃ upāsanāvidhīnāṃ mukte'pi pravṛttirastyuta neti | tadatharṃ muktau prāptavyaphalamastyuta neti | muktasya niṣkriyatvena sāmagāyanādikriyāyogena "etatsāmagāyannityādeḥ gauṇamuktiparatvena muktāvupāsanāstitve mānābhāvāt‌ | na cāphalasya karaṇāyogena saphalatvopagame'pūrṇatāprasaṅgena ca muktena upāsanā na kriyate iti prāpte "sa ya evaṃ vidasmāllokātpretya' iti pūrvavākye brahmajñāninaśvaramadehatyāgamuktvā paścādānandamayanyāyena ānandamayādibhagavadrūpapañcakaprāptiṃ "etamannamayamātmānamupasaṅkramya' ityādinoktvā ā nātasya "etatsāma' iti vākyasya gauṇamuktiparatvāyogena paramamuktiparatvena pramāṇasadbhāvāt‌ "kṛṣṇo muktai rijyate' iti  muktasya yajanādikriyāsattvena kaimutyanyāyācca muktenopāsanaṃ kriyata eveti siddhānta ityādyaḥ pakṣaḥ | dvitīyastu vidhīnāṃ iṣyasādhanatvam‌ | tadbodhakāśca "upāsīta' ityādi vidhipratyayārthāḥ | te "brahmavidā noti param‌' iti  brahmajñānena mokṣo bhavatītyukte tatkathamityapekṣāyāṃ tādarthe pravṛttā api yathā baddhān‌ prati upāsanaṃ mokṣarūpeṣyasādhanamiti pratyāyayanti evaṃ muktaṃ pratyapi ānandātiśayasya prācīnakarmaphalatvasya "agnihotrādi tu tatkāryāyaiva' iti  vakṣyamāṇatvena tatsādhanatvabodhanāsambhave'pi muktau tadā tadā prāptāniṣyaparihārarūpaphalasyeṣyasya sambhavena tatsādhanatābodhakatvasambhavena mukte'pi pravṛttirasti | anyathā tatkṛterevāyogāt‌ | ato vidhiphalakāmanādyupādhinaiva upāsanā kriyata iti prāpte "tīrṇo hi tadā sarvān‌ śokān‌ hṛdayasya bhavati'  ityādeḥ muktau sarvāriṣyaparihārasya siddhatvena prāptavyāniṣyaparihārasyāpyabhāvenaiva tatsādhanatvamādāyāpi iṣyasādhanatvamupāsanasya muktaṃ prati bodhayitumaśakyatvena baddhena muktasya samānadharmatāpattyā ca mukte upāsanāvidhīnāmapravṛtteḥ | vidhiphalakāmanayorabhāve'pi niyatabrahmayajñānantaraṃ kuśaiḥ svecchayā dvijānāṃ mantrasāmādhyayanavat‌ īśvarakriyāvacca svecchayā līlārūpatayaivopapatteḥ kriyate muktenopāsaneti | tathā ca smṛtiḥ "tīrṇatartavyabhāgāśca svecchayopāsate param‌'  iti siddhānta iti | atra prayojanaṃ pūrvapādoktabhaktyākṣepasamādhī | atrādyamadhikaraṇaśarīraṃ bhāṣye "etatsāmagāyannāste' iti śrutyuktyā bhavediyaṃ cintā yadi muktasya upāsanameva syāttadeva kutaḥ iti ṭīkayā muktau upāsanabhāve phalādikamapi syāditi nyāyavivaraṇena ca sūcitam‌ | dvitīyaṃ tu muktasyopāsanā kartavyā navetyādi bhāṣyādineti jñeyam‌ | ṭīkāyāṃ tu bhāṣyavivaraṇayorekavākyatāsampādanāya vyāmiśreṇa nirūpaṇaṃ kṛtamityavirodhaḥ | yadvā muktenopāstiḥ na kriyate kriyate veti cintā | tadarthaṃ muktāvupāstibhāve sādhakaṃ nāstyatha viparītamiti | tadarthaṃ muktasya vidhiviṣayatvādikaṃ bādhakaṃ bhavatyuta neti | pūrvapakṣastu | muktasyopāsanāvattve mānābhāvena vidhibaddhatvena vā tadabaddhatvena vā ubhayathāpyupāsanābhāve phalaprāptyā apūrṇatāprasaṅgena ādye saṃsārisamānadharmāpattyā antye avihitakaraṇasyaivāyogena bādhakena muktenopāsanā na kriyata iti | siddhāntastu | etatsāmagāyannityādi sādhakabhāvena upāsanāvidhīnāṃ mokṣavākyaśeṣatayā muktyarthatvena muktasya tadaviṣayatve'pi kuśācchandastutyupagānavatsvecchayaivānandavṛddheḥ prācīnanivṛttakarmaphalatvena sarvāniṣyanivṛtteḥ prāptatvena prāpyaphalābhāve'pi īśvarakriyāvanmuktasya līlātvenopāsanākriyāyāḥ svabhāvopapatteḥ bādhakābhāvena ca muktenopāsanā kriyata ityekarūpeṇaiva pūrvottarapakṣau dhyeyau || 17 ||
|| oṃ chandata ubhayavirodhāt‌ oṃ || 18 ||
|| oṃ gaterarthavattvamubhayathā'nyathā hi virodhaḥ oṃ || oṃ upapannastallakṣaṇārthopalabdherlokavat‌ oṃ ||
upāsanāprasaṅgasannidhāpitaṃ karmāpyupāsanāvanmuktau niyatameveti śaṅkanātsaṅgatiḥ | pūrvatra prakṛtā muktāḥ kimupāsanavanniyamena karma kurvantyuta neti cintā | tadarthaṃ niyamena tadanuṣṭhāpakamastyuta neti | tadarthaṃ śaktatvādyevānuṣṭhāpakamuta vidhyādivattvamaṣīti | pūrvapakṣastu | sādhanānuṣṭhāne hi tajjanyaphalārthitve sati śaktatvaṃ prayojakam‌ | muktānāṃ ca prāptaphalatvenārthitvābhāve'pi siddhavaiduṣyāṇāṃ svecchayopāsanānuṣṭhānavat‌ karma karaṇe'pi śaktisadbhāvātkarmaṇo bhagavatpūjārūpatvācca anuṣṭhāpakasadbhāvātkarmāpi
