niyamena kuvarntyeva | anyeṣāṃ tasyānanuṣṭhāpakatve upāsanamapi niyamena na kuryuḥ aviśeṣāditi | siddhāntastu | karmaṇo bhagavatpūjātmakatve'pi muktānāṃ tadanuṣṭhānaśaktatve'pi na tāvanmātraṃ niyamenānuṣṭhāne hetuḥ | kiṃ tu vihitatvaṃ akaraṇe pratyavāyavatvaṃ aprāptaphalatvaṃ ca | na caitatsarvaṃ muktānāmapi mokṣasyāpumarthatvaprasaṅgena vidhyāderabhāvātprāptaphalatvācca teṣām‌ | ato niyamena na karma kurvanti kintu bhagavatpūjātvāt‌ pūrvānuṣṭhānavāsanābalācca kadācideva kurvanti | upāsanaṃ tu pūrvaniyamenānāratamanuṣṭhānena tadvāsanābalānnityajñānasvarūpācca niyamenānutiṣṭhanti | "kadācitkarma kuvarnti kadācinnaiva kurvate | nityajñānasvarūpatvānnityaṃ dhyāyanti keśavam‌'  iti smṛteriti || 18 ||
|| oṃ aniyamaḥ sarveṣāmavirodhācchabdānumānābhyām‌ oṃ || 19 ||
pūvarṃ jñānadvārā karmaphalasya mokṣasya prāptatvānna karmānuṣṭhānanaiyatyamityuktau tarhi jñānināṃ sarveṣāṃ phalaprāptiruta keṣāñcideveti cintanātsaṅgatiḥ | yadvā upāsanāvidhīnāṃ jñānadvārā mokṣavākyaśeṣatvamupāyanaśabdaśeṣatvādityatra uktamayuktam‌ | satyapi jñāne keṣāñcinmokṣābhāvādityāśayenātraśaṅkanātsaṅgatiḥ | pūrvanayasya tu prāsaṅgikatvāt‌ na vyavadhāyakatvam‌ | tallakṣaṇārthopalabdheḥ'  ityādau pūrvatrārthapadena prakṛto mokṣaḥ kiṃ keṣāñcideva jñānināṃ jñānināṃ prāpya uta sarveṣāṃ jñāninamiti cintā | tadarthaṃ jñāniṣvapi upāsane yogyāyogyavibhāgo'styuta neti | tadarthaṃ jñāniṣvapi yogyāyogyavibhāgabhāve jñāpakaṃ nāstyutāstīti | pūrvapakṣastu | keṣāñcideva jñānināṃ mokṣo na sarveṣām‌ | jñāniṣvapi keṣucidayogyatvasambhavāt‌ | "kāmaṃ bhavema vṛjinairnirayeṣu naṣyāśceto'livadyarhi te padayo rameta' iti  tṛtīyaskandhe kṛmārokteḥ | ayogyānāṃ ca mokṣasyopāsakeṣvayogyānāṃ sarvaguṇopasaṃhārasyevāprāpyatvāt‌ | na copāsakeṣu sarvaguṇopasaṃhārayogyajanavat‌ jñāniṣu muktayogyajano nāstyeva | prāguktakumoroktistu bhagavatpādaratistutipareti vācyam‌ | sarvaguṇopasaṃhārayogyāyogyavibhāgo'sti jñāniṣu nāstītyatra jñāpakābhāvāditi | siddhāntastu | sarveṣāmeva jñānināṃ mokṣo na keṣāñcideveti niyamaḥ "sarvairguṇairbrahmaṇaiva hyupāsyo nānyaiḥ' iti niṣedhabalenopasaṃhāre yogyāyogyavibhāgasattve'pīha jñāniṣu keṣāñcinmuktiniṣedhābhāvena pratyuta "na kaścidbrahmavitsṛtimanubhavati mukto hyeṣa bhavati' iti  sarvajñānināṃ muktinaiyatyavacanena vimatā jñānino niyatamokṣāḥ jñānitvātsammatajñānivaditi nirbādhaśrutimūlānumānena ca jñāniṣvayogyābhāvaniścayātsarvajñāniprāpyo mokṣa iti tatsādhanopāsanaṃ niḥśaṅkaṃ kāryameva | yuktaṃ copāyanaśeṣatvaṃ taddhidhīnāmiti || 19 ||
|| oṃ yāvadadhikāramavasthitirādhikārikāṇām‌ oṃ || 20 ||
|| oṃ akṣaradhiyāṃ tvavirodhaḥ sāmānyataddhāvābhyāmaupasadavattaduktam‌ oṃ ||
yadyevamaniyamena jñānino mokṣaḥ tarhi akiñcitkaro'sau yogyatāviśeṣaḥ tatprayojakaphalatāratamyasyānyenaivopapatteriti pūrvoktatarkapratihataśaṅkanātsaṅgatiḥ| yeṣāmupāsakānāṃ jñānaprāptau satyāṃ mokṣanaiyatyaṃ prāguktaṃ tadupāsakāḥ kiṃ yogyatātāratamyavanto uta na tadvanta iti cintā | tadatharṃ tatra pramāṇaṃ nāstyutāstīti | tadarthaṃ muktāvānandāditāratamyaṃ sādhakaṃ nāsti bādhakaṃ cāsti atha viparītamiti | pūvarpakṣastu | "vṛddhihrāsabhāktvam‌' iti naye  daśarnāccetiguṇasūtropāttayā "athāta ānandasya mīmāṃsā bhavati' ityādiśruteḥ saṃsāriparatvena muktāvānandatāratamye mānābhāvāt‌ dveṣerṣyādiprasaṅgarūpabādhakācca muktau tāratamyamevāyuktam‌ | yuktaṃ cedapi kṛṣyādiprayatnatāratamyena dhānyādiphalatāratamyavadupāsanādiprayatnatāratamyenaivopapādyate iti tasya yogyatātāratamyāprayojakatvena pamāṇābhāvānnopāsakāḥ yogyatātāratamyavantaḥ iti | siddhāntastu | "nānāvidhā devasaṅghā vimuktau' ityādi viśeṣa śrutyā "narādibrahmaparyantaṃ vimuktānāṃ śatocchrayaḥ | apyānande mithopyuktastvadhvaryūṇāṃ śrutau pṛthak‌' iti smṛtyā ca ānandaśruteramuktaviṣayatvāyogena muktau tāratamye pramāṇābhāvāt‌ viṣayalampaṭatvamatsarādidoṣitvānupakāritvānāmeva leke dveṣerṣyādiprayojakatvasya dṛṣyatvena dveṣerṣyādinimittaviṣayalampaṭatvādeḥ brahmajñānitayā mukteṣvabhāvāt‌ uttamebhyo'dhamānāṃ jñānādilakṣaṇopakārabhāvena anupakāritvābhāvāt‌ bādhakābhāvācca yuktameva muktau tāratamyam‌ | tattu prācīnayogyatātāratamyena vinā'nupapadyamānaṃ
yogyatātāratamyaṃ gamayet‌ | nacopāsanādiprayatnatāratamyenānyathopapattiḥ | "svādhikārādhiko yatnaḥ prāyaśo nopapadyate | kathaṃcidadhike yatne doṣaḥ kaścitsamāpatet‌' iti vacanena prayatnatāratamyasyāpi yogyatātāratamyanibandhanāt‌ | ato yogyatātāratamyavanta evopāsakā iti | phalaṃ tu prāpteścetyatroktayogyatābhedasamarthanam‌ || 20 ||
|| oṃ jñayadāmananāt‌ oṃ || 21 ||
|| oṃ antarā bhūtagrāmavaditi cettaduktam‌ oṃ || oṃ anyathā bhedānupapattiriti cennopadeśavat‌ oṃ ||
prāguktayogyatāniyamasya niravadhikatvamāśaṅkaya sāvadhikatvarūpaviśeṣacintanātsaṅgatiḥ | chāndogye saptame nāmāvāṅ‌manassaṃkalpacittadhyānavijñānabalānnāptejaākāśasmarāśāprāṇaśabditāḥ uṣāsvāhā parjanyamitrāgnivaruṇavidyutpravāhāniruddhāhaṃkārikaprāṇendromārudravāṇīmārutāṃtadevatāḥ uttarottaramādhikyenoktāḥ | pūrvatrādhikāripadena prakṛtāste upāsanādhikāravantaḥ kiṃ mukhyaprāṇāvadhikā na bhavanti uta tadavadhikā eveti cintā | tadarthaṃ prāṇādadhikādhikārisādhakamanumānaṃ "prāṇo vā sarvebhyo bhūyānna prāṇādbhūyāniti śrutibādhitaṃ na bhavatyuta bhavatīti | tadarthaṃ śrutau sarvaśabdaḥ kiṃ paramātmānyapara iva adhikāryantarānyaparatayāpi saṃkucadvṛttiḥ sambhavatyuta neti | pūrvapakṣastu | "prāṇo vāva sarvebhyaḥ' ityatra sarvaśabdasyāsaṃkucadvṛttitve mukhyaprāṇya nirupacaritasarvottamatvaprāptyāsarvottamavastudvayāyogena śrutyādisiddhasarvottamatākaviṣṇvabhede vācye "cakṣurādivat‌' ityādau  bhāṣyoktānekamānasiddhaprāṇaparamātmabhedābhāvaprasaṅgabādhakena tadanyaparatvena saṅkucadvṛttitve'vaśyaṃ vācye adhikāriviśeṣavyatiriktaparatvenāpi saṃkoce bādhakābhāvāt‌ | tathā ca tasyāḥ śruteḥ prāṇaḥ svottamādhikāriyuktaḥ adhikāritvāt‌ nāmādivadityanumānabādhakatvena tadabādhitādasmādanumānāt‌ prāṇādapyuttamādhikārisiddheḥ upāsanādhikāriṇo na mukhyaprāṇāvadhikā iti | siddhāntastu | sarvaśabdasyānekamānabalena bhagavadanyatra saṃkucadvṛttitve'pi cakṣurādivattvityādau tatratatra bhāṣyoktavacanaiḥ "uttamatvaṃ hi devānām‌' ityādi  vakṣyamāṇabhāṣyavacanaiśca prāṇāddharerevottamatvapratipādanaparaiḥ tadanusāriṇyāḥ bhagavanmātravyatiriktaparatvena saṃkucadvṛttikasarvaśabdopetaprāguktaśrutyā ca bādhitatvena anumānasyāpramāṇatvāt‌ prāṇāvadhikā evopāsakā iti | phalaṃ tu sarvaguṇopasaṃhārasya brahmādhikārikatvoktisamarthanam‌ || 21 ||
|| oṃ vyatihāro viśiṃṣati hītaravat‌ oṃ || 22 ||
pūrvatra prāṇasyaiva hiraṇyagarbhatvāt‌ tasya prāguktassarvaguṇopasaṃhāro yukta ityaktamayuktam‌ | tasyaiva savarottamatvenānyasya tadupāsyasyābhāvāditi śaṅkānādvā upāsanādhikāriṇo nirūpyopāsyamiha nirūpyata iti vā saṅgatiḥ | pūrvatra prāṇāduttamatvena prakṛto viṣṇuḥ kiṃ prāṇānnottamaḥ athottama iti cintā | tadarthaṃ chāndogye "prāṇo vā āśāyā bhūyān‌' ityā nānāt‌  prāṇādupari "satyaṃ tveva vijijñāsitavyam‌'  ityā nātaḥ sarvottamaḥ satyanāmakaḥ kiṃ prāṇādananya utānyo viṣṇuriti cintā | tadarthaṃ prāṇaprakaraṇāt‌ satyaprakaraṇasya bhedakaṃ nāstyutāstīti | pūrvapakṣastu | nāmādiprāṇaparyantānāṃ pañcadaśaprakaraṇānāṃ bhedakasya praśnaprativacanabhāvasyeveha "asti prāṇādbhūyaḥ iti praśnasya "satyaṃ vāva prāṇādbhūyaḥ' iti prativacanasya prāṇasatyaprakaraṇabhedakasyābhāvena prāṇasatyayoranyatvānna satyaśabdārthabhūtaḥ san‌ viṣṇuḥ prāṇāduttamaḥ | na ca prakaraṇabhedakayoḥ praśnaprativacanayoradhyāhāraḥ jñāpakābhāvāditi | siddhāntastu | "sa vā eṣa evaṃ paśyannevaṃ manvāna evaṃ vijānannativādī bhavati' iti  prāṇavādino'tiśayitavastuvāditvenātivāditvamuktvā paścāt‌ "eṣa tu vā ativadati yaḥ satyenātivadati'  iti satyasya vastu vadatopyativāditvoktyā prāṇātivādito'pi satyativādinaḥ tuśabdena vyāvartanāt‌ jñāpakāt‌ praśnaprativacanayoradhyāhāreṇa prakaraṇavicchedāt‌ "uttamatvaṃ hi devānām‌' iti  smṛteśca satyanāmā viṣṇuḥ prāṇāduttama iti tuśabdo nādhyāhārakalpakaḥ "vijñānaṃ tveva'  "matistveva'  ityādau vakṣyamāṇadiśā bhedahīne'pi vastuni śravaṇena vyabhicārāditi codyantu "bhūmaḥ' naye  nirastam‌ | nirāsakaprakāraśca candrikāyāṃ vyaktaḥ | phalaṃ tvānandādaya ityatrokta ātmatvopāsanasamarthanam‌ ||
22 ||
|| oṃ saiva hi satyādaya oṃ || 23 ||
atra prāgukte viṣṇoḥ prāṇāduttamatve viśeṣacintanātsaṅgatiḥ | prakṛtātprāṇāt‌ satyapadokto viṣṇurevottamaḥ atha bahavo'pyuttamāḥ santīti cintā | tadarthaṃ satyasyopari paṭhitānāṃ vijñānamatiśraddhāniṣṭhakṛtisukhabhūmāhaṃkārātmanāṃ navānāṃ kiṃ satyāt‌ parasparaṃ ca bheda utābheda iti | tadarthaṃ abhede jñāpakaṃ nāstyutāstīti | pūrvapakṣastu | satyādīnāmātmāntānāṃ abhede jñāpakābhāvāt‌ śabdāntaranyāyena bhedasyaiva nyāyyatvāt‌ prāṇātsatyādayo bahava uttamā iti | siddhāntastu | vijñānādeḥ satyāditonyatve "satyaṃ bhagavo vijijñāsya'  ityādi praśne vijñānāderukterasāṃgatyāpatteḥ tadanyathānupapattibalena "satyaṃ bhagavo vijijñāsya' ityasya satyaguṇajijñāsāparatve sati satyāvinābhūtavijñānoktiḥ | evaṃ "vijñānaṃ bhagavo vijijñāsya'  ityatrāpi vijñānaguṇajijñāsāyāṃ tadavinābhūtaguṇoktiparatvena sarveṣāmabhedasya nyāyaprāptatvāt‌ "satyādyā ahamantā yadguṇāḥ' ityādi smṛteśca teṣāmabhedāt‌ viṣṇureva prāṇāduttama iti || 23 ||
|| oṃ kāmāditaratra tatra cāyatanādibhyaḥ oṃ || 24 ||
|| oṃ ādarādalopaḥ oṃ || oṃ upasthitestadvacanāt‌ oṃ ||
evaṃ satyādeḥ sarvasya viṣṇutvoktau śriyopyadhikāritvāt‌ prāṇādūrdhvaṃ śriyo'pyadhyāhāraḥ syāt‌ | tathāca prāṇāvadhitvamadhikārivargasyoktamayuktamiti śaṅkanātsaṅgatiḥ | atra prāṇaviṣṇusannidhāpitā śrīḥ kimupāsanāyāmadhikārirī atha neti cintā | tadarthaṃ sā baddhā uta neti | tadarthaṃ sītādirūpeṇa bhūmau bhavanādikaṃ jīvavaddehayogādirūpajanmādyeva uta prādurbhāvādirūpamiti | tadarthaṃ śriyo bhagavadarcanaṃ janmādihetukarmādibandhamocananimittaṃ uta muktavannirupādhikabhaktyatiśayanimittamiti | tadarthaṃ bhaktinimittatvakalpakaṃ nāstyutāstīti | pūrvapakṣastu | "yadarcitaṃ brahmabhavādibhissuraiḥ śriyā ca devyā munibhiśca sātvataiḥ'  ityādau śriyo'pi bhagavadupāsanā śrūyate | sā cetareṣāmiva bhagavatprītidvārā bandhamocanārthā | tasyāḥ kṛtakṛtyatve tadayogāt‌ | bhaktyatiśayanimittatve kalpakābhāvāt‌ | anyathā sāttvataprabhṛtīnāmapi sā tadarthā na syāt‌ | evaṃ ca tadbalātsītārukmiṇyādirūpeṇa bhavanatirodhānādeḥ karmādhīnadehopādānatyāgarūpatvena itarajīvavat‌ baddhatvāvagamāt‌ baddhatve sati sādhutvācca adhikāriṇī evopāsanāyāṃ śrīriti | siddhāntastu | sarvāyatanetyādibhāṣyoktaśrutyādibalena nityaṃ bhagavatsambandhitvena atiśayitatatprasādāt‌ nityamuktatvāvagamāt‌ tadbalena bhūmau bhavanādeḥ īśecchādhīnasvecchāmātrahetukatayā prādurbhāvarūpatvena kamarādhīnadehayogādirūpatvābhāvāvagamena "yathāśrīrnityamuktāpi' iti smṛtyā cārcanasya bhaktyatiśayanimittatvakalpanānnādhikāriṇī śrīriti | phalaṃ tu upāsakānāṃ prāṇāvadhitvasamarthanam‌ || 24 ||
|| oṃ tannirdhāraṇārthaniyamastaddṛṣyeḥ pṛthagghyapratibandhaḥ phalam‌ oṃ || 25 ||
pūrvatra śriyaḥ kṛtārthatā phalābhāve'pi ādarādupāsanā'lopoktau tarhyakṛtārthānāṃ phalābhāvāt‌ śravaṇādisamastopāsanasya lopa eva syāditi śaṅkanātsaṅgatiḥ | atra śravaṇamananadhyānarūpatrividhamupāsanaṃ vyastaṃ kāryamuta samastamiti cintā | tadarthaṃ trividhasyāpi phalaṃ nāstyutāstīti | tadarthaṃ tritayasyāpi tatsādhanatve manopayogau na sta uta sta iti | pūrvapakṣastu | upāsanaṃ hi bhagavaddarśanārthaṃ "draṣyavyaḥ' śruteḥ | tasya ca śravaṇamananābhyāṃ vā dhyānenaiva vopapattau na tritayasādhyatve pramāṇamasti | "draṣyavyaḥ śrotavyo mantavyo nididhyāsitavyaḥ' iti śrutistu vaikalpikasādhanavidhānenāpyupapannā | kiṃ ca na tritayasyopayogo'sti | na cājñātajñāpanena śravaṇasya, tanniyamajananena manasya, dṛśijananena dhyānasyopayoga iti yuktam‌ | tathāca tritayasyāpi pṛthaktvaphalatvena darśanoddeśena tritayavidhānānupapatteḥ | evaṃ ca pramāṇopayegayorabhāve darśanasya
tritayasādhyatvābhāvena phalābhāvānna samastopāsanaṃ kāryamiti | siddhāntastu | "draṣyavya' iti śrutau darśanoddeśena tritayasyāpi vidhānādvikalpadyotakaśabdābhāvena samuccayasādhanatāvagamāt‌ samuccitānāmeva dṛśirūpaphalahetutritayaṃ kāryameva | nacopayogābhāvaḥ śaṅkayaḥ | darśanaṃ prati dhyānasyaiva hetutve'pi dhyānasya dṛśijanane'pekṣitāyāḥ pratibandhakājñānasaṃśayaviparyayanivṛtteḥ sampādanena śravaṇamananayorupayogitvāditi | phalaṃ tu jijñāsāsūtroktatritayasamarthanam‌ || 25 ||
|| oṃ pradānavadeva hi taduktam‌ oṃ || 26 ||
pradhānakartavyatvasiddhau tadaṅgamihanirūpyata iti vā pūrvaṃ śravaṇāditrayasyāpi phalamuktaṃ ihāṃgamucyata iti vā dṛśi hetordhyānasya śravaṇamananākhyamaṅgamuktaṃ iha tritayasyāpyaṃgamucyata iti vā saṅgatiḥ | atra prakṛtaśravaṇāditrayaṃ yathākathaṃcidanuṣṭhitaṃ jñānaheturuta prītipūrvakagurūpadeśayuktamiti cintā | tadarthaṃ tasya tādṛśagurūpadeśayuktatvena phalahetutve sādhakaṃ nāsti śrutivirodhādibādhakaṃ cāstyuta viparītamiti | pūrvapakṣastu | gurūpadeśayuktameva śravaṇādikaṃ dṛśiheturityatra pramāṇābhāvāt‌ pratyuta draṣyavya iti śrutau dṛśyuddeśena śravaṇāditrayasyaiva vidhānena tadvirodharūpasya jñānasya guruprasādādhīnatve jñānino'pi mokṣasya tadadhīnatvaprasaṃgena kadācidgurukopena mokṣābhāvasyāpyāpattyā "aniyamassarveṣām‌' ityukta  bhaṃgarūpasya bādhakasya ca sattvācca na gurupradānayuktaṃ jñānahetuḥ kiṃtu kevalameva śravaṇādikam‌ | naca gurukopo na mokṣapratibandhakaḥ | tathātve gurupradānamapi jñāne'kiṃcitkaramiti | siddhāntastu | prītipūrvakagurūpadeśarūpāṃgayuktameva śravaṇādikaṃ jñānaheturna kevalam‌ | "ācāryavān‌ puruṣo veda' iti  śrutau jñānasya guruprasādādhīnatvāvagamena tena vinā śravaṇādimātreṇa jñānāsiddheḥ | "samyaglakṣaṇasampanno yaddadyātsuprasannadhīḥ | śiṣyāya satyaṃ bhavati tatsarvaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ' iti "nātiprasannahṛdayo yaddadyādgururapyasau | na tatsatyaṃ bhavettasmādarcanīyo gurussadā' iti smṛteśca | naca draṣyavya iti śrutivirodho bādhakaḥ | śravaṇādeḥ pradhānasādhanatvādgurudānasya tadaṅgatvāt‌ somarasādhyāyagasya dṛḍhamuṣyinipīḍanādhīnatve'pi tasya tadaṅgatvavat‌ jñānasya gurupradānādhīnatve'pi tasya jñānahetuśravaṇādyaṅgatvopapatteḥ | aṅgasya ca pradhānasādhanena saha tulyakakṣyatayā vidhānābhāve'pi dṛḍhamuṣyinipīḍanāderiva atharādvacanāntarādvā prāptatvena pradānasya draṣyavyaśrutyanumatatvāt‌ | naca kadācitkopena jñānasya vā jñānino mokṣasya vā'bhāvāpāto bādhaka iti vācyam‌ | jñānamokṣayorīśvaraklaptatvena tadabhāvāyogāt‌ | guruśāpādeśca "jñānino guruśāpepi' ityādi  smṛtyā jñānādihrāsahetutvena sāphalyopapatteriti | phalaṃ tu itaretvarthasāmānyāt‌' ityatrokta gurusāpekṣatvasya samarthanam‌ || 26 ||
|| oṃ liṅgabhūyastvāttaddhi balīyastadapi oṃ || 27 ||
pūrvaprakṛtāṅgāṅginomardhye'trāṅgasya balavatvasādhanātsaṅgatiḥ | atra śravaṇādisādhanaṃ tadaṃgagurupradānaṃ ceti dvayaṃ samabalaṃ utānyataradbalavaditi cintā | tadarthaṃ puruṣatvasāmyaṃ guruśiṣyaprayatnayoḥ sāmyāpādakaṃ bhavatyuta neti | tathānyataradbalavadityatra śiṣyaprayatno balavānatha gurudānaṃ balavaditi cintā | tadarthaṃ pradānasya balavattve sādhakaṃ nāsti bādhakaṃ cāstyathaviparītamiti | pūrvapakṣastu | pradānasya balavattve kvāpītikartavyatāyāḥ sādhanādapi prābalyādaśarnena itikartavyatvabhaṅgaprasaṅgāt‌ śravaṇādivaiyarthyaprasaṅgācca bādhakābhāvāt‌ sādhakābhāvācca na pradānaṃ balavat‌ kintu śravaṇādyeva pradhānasādhanatvāt‌ | māstu vā balavat‌ guruśiṣyayoḥ puruṣatvāviśeṣāt‌ gurupradānaṃ śiṣyaprayatnaśca dvayaṃ samameva syāt‌ | nahi kaścinnyūnādhikabhāve'pi viśeṣo'stīti | siddhāntastu | na tāvatsāmyaṃ yuktaṃ puruṣatve gurutvaśiṣyatvavat‌ tatprayatnayorapi nyūnādhikabhāvasambhavena puruṣatvamātrasyāprayojakatvāt‌ nyūnādhikabhāve pradānasyaivādhikyamuktam‌ liṅgabāhulyarūpasādhakabhāvāt‌ | hiṃsāyāḥ pāpajananaśaktāyā api yāgīyahiṃsāyāḥ prokṣaṇāṃjanaparyagnikaraṇādītikartavyatābalena puṇyajanakatvadarśanena tatretikartavyatāyāḥ prābalyavat‌ atra pradānasya prābalye aṅgatvabhaṅgaprasaṅgābhāvāt‌ | śrotavya ityādi śrutyā "guruprasādo balavān‌' ityādi  smṛtyā ca śravaṇāderapyāvaśyakatvena tadvaiyarthyābhāvācca bādhakābhāvācceti | liṅgabāhulyaṃ ca ṭīkādau vyaktam‌ | phalaṃ tu setikartavyatvasyaiva śravaṇāderjñānahetutvarūpaprāguktaprameyasamarthanam‌ | anyathā kvacidguroranapekṣitatvaprasaṅgena prāguktamayuktaṃ syāditi || 27 ||
|| oṃ pūrvavikalpaḥ prakaraṇātsyātkriyāmānasavat‌ oṃ || 28 ||
|| oṃ atideśācca oṃ ||
yatprasādo balavānityuktaṃ sa gururatra cintyata iti saṅgatiḥ | atra prakṛto guruḥ kiṃ pūrvaprāpta eveti niyamo'thānyo'pi gṛhītuṃ śakya iti cintā | tadarthaṃ pūrvatyāge kāraṇaṃ nāsti bādhakaṃ cāsti uta viparītamiti | tathā anyo'pi grāhya iti pakṣe sa kiṃ yaḥ kaściduta pūrvasmādanapakṛṣyaḥ samagrānugrahakartaiveti | pūrvapakṣastu | śubhalābhārthaṃ hi gurusvīkāro bhavati | sa ca pūrvaprāptaguruṇaiva bhaviṣyatīti tattyāge kāraṇābhāvāt‌ | " gurumantraparityāgī rauravaṃ narakaṃ vrajet‌' iti doṣaśravaṇarūpabādhakācca pūvarprāpta eva guruḥ natvanyo'pi grāhya iti | anyo'pi grāhya iti pakṣe ca niyāmakābhāvādyaḥ kaścideveti | siddhāntastu | guruparigraho hi na kevalaṃ kiṃcicchubhalābhārthaṃ kintu yāvadapekṣitasvayogyaśubhalābhārtham‌ | tasya yadi pūrvagṛhīto na samarthaḥ tarhi svāpekṣitālābhaprasaṅgena samagrānugrahārthatvarūpakāṇasattvādanyo'pi guruḥ svīkāryaḥ | so'pi na yādṛśatādṛśaḥ kintu svasmādanapakṛṣyaḥ samagrānugrahakartaiva | sa ca samaścet‌ pūrvānujñayā svīkāryo na veti vikalpaḥ samaphalayordhyānayoriva na tu svīkārya eveti niyamaḥ | uttamaścetpūrvānujñāṃ vinaiva svīkāryaḥ syāt‌ | sarvasyoktārthasya "pūrvasmāduttamo labdhaḥ' iti  smṛtyā "samagrānugrahaṃ kaścit‌' iti  smṛtyā ca siddhatvāt‌ "brahmopāsva' iti  śrutau pūrvaguruṇaiva svasamottamasvīkārāyātidiśyamānatvācca | naca doṣavacanavirodhaḥ | tasyādhamasvīkāraviṣayakatvena vyarthasvīkāraviṣayakatvena pūrvānujñābhāvaviṣayakatvena sāvakāśatvāditi | phalaṃ tu itaretvityatroktārthasya sarvajñagurūktaprakāreṇa guṇādidhyānaṃ kāryamityasya samarthanam‌ || 28 ||
|| oṃ vidyaiva tu nirdhāraṇāt‌ oṃ || 29 ||
|| oṃ darśanācca oṃ ||
yasyopāsanasya gurupradānarūpetikartavyatāphalabhedādhikāridhyeyādikaṃ tasya laghūpāyakarmahetutāvākyena muktihetutvānirṇayāt‌ sarvaṃ prāguktaṃ abhitticitrāyitamiti śaṅkanātsaṅgatiḥ | atra prakṛtaṃ śravaṇādyupāsanaṃ na kāryamuta kāryamiti cintā | tadarthaṃ tasya jñānadvārā muktihetunirṇayo na sambhavatyatha sambhavatīti | tadarthaṃ karmahetutvavākyānāṃ "bhāktaṃ vā' ityatra  paramparayā muktihetutvādirūpabhāktatvavarṇanaṃ ayuktamuta yuktamiti cintā | tadarthaṃ karmaṇaiveti tṛtīyayā tasya sākṣānmuktihetutvabodhanasyāpavādakaṃ nāstyutāstīti | tathā jñānadvārā muktihetutvanirṇayapakṣe kiṃ parokṣajñānadvāramātreṇa uta aparokṣajñānadvāramātreṇāpīti | pūvarpakṣastu | "karmaṇaiva hi saṃsiddhimāsthitā janakādayaḥ' ityādau  avadhāraṇasya vakṣyamāṇadiśā sāvakāśatve'pi tṛtīyāvibhaktyā karmaṇaḥ saṃsiddhiśabditamuktiṃ prati sākṣāddhetutvāvagamasyāpavādakasya pramāṇasya kasyacidabhāvāt‌ "tarati śokamātmavit‌' ityādeḥ jñānasya muktihetutāmātraprāpakatayā karmaṇo muktihetutvānapavādakatvāt‌ | evaṃ ca nirapavādakasākṣānmuktihetubodhakavākyabalena karmaṇo'pi sākṣānmuktihetutvāvagamena "bhāktaṃ vā'nātmavittvāt‌'  iti tṛtīyādyapāde karmaṇo muktihetutvaṃ gauṇamiti varṇanasyāyuktatvāt‌ jñānadvārā śravaṇādyupāsanameva muktiheturiti nirṇayāsambhavāt‌ laghūpāye sati gurūpāyasya niśśaṅkamanuṣṭhānāyogācca na kāryamupāsanamiti | māstu vā jñānadvāropāsanaṃ muktihetuḥ tathā'pi "tamevaṃ vidvān‌' iti śrutyā  aparokṣasyaiva muktihetutvāvagamāt‌ tasya ca śravaṇādinaiva siddhe dhyānaṃ na sarvathā kāryameveti | siddhāntastu | "nānyaḥ panthāḥ' iti sāvadhāraṇahetutvavacanasya karmavacanādapi balavattvena tena sākṣātkarmahetutvasyāpoditatayā karmaṇaivetyāderapi paramparayā kathaṃcinmuktihetutvasya prāguktarītyā varṇanīyatvāt‌ jñānadvārā muktihetutvasyopāsane nirṇayasambhavāt‌ kāryamevopāsanam‌ | dvārabhūtaṃ jñānamapi na parokṣamātraṃ kintu "dṛṣṭvaiva taṃ mucyate' ityādi śrutibalena vidvāniti śruterapi aparokṣaparatvādaparokṣajñānamapīti śravaṇāditritayamapi kāryameveti | smṛtirapi sāvadhāraṇetyetadagre nirasiṣyate | phalaṃ tu prāguktasamarthanam‌ || 29 ||
|| oṃ śrutyādibalīyastvācca na bādhaḥ oṃ || 30 ||
yadi śruteḥ sāvadhāraṇatvena balavattvaṃ tarhi smṛterapi tathātvāt‌ āptavākyatvācca balavattvamiti śaṅkanātsaṅgatiḥ | atra jñānaphalatvena pūrvatraprakṛto mokṣaḥ kiṃ karmasādhyo'tha jñānasādhya iti cintā | tadarthaṃ "karmaṇaiva' iti smṛtiḥ "nānyaḥ panthāḥ' iti śrutito'pi prabalā uta neti | tadarthaṃ karmaṇaivetyavadhāraṇaṃ niravakāśamuta sāvakāśamiti | tadarthaṃ sāvakāśakalpakaṃ nāstyutāstīti | pūrvapakṣastu | karmasādhyo mokṣaḥ karmaṇaiti smṛteḥ | naca sāvadhāraṇaśrutibādhitā satī smṛtiḥ durbaleti vācyam‌ | smṛterapi sāvadhāraṇatvāt‌ | naca tadayogavyavacchedakatayā sāvakāśaṃ kalpakābhāvāt‌ | naca śrutivirodhe daurbalyaṃ smṛteḥ śrutereva ca prābalyamiti "virodhetvanapekṣaṃ syāt‌' iti pūrvatantre siddhamiti vācyam‌ | smṛteḥ śrutimūlatvena prāmāṇyapakṣa eva hi tat‌ | karmaṇaiveti smṛteśca bhagavadvākyatvenaiva prāmāṇyopagamenāptavākyatayā sāvadhāraṇatayā ca prābalyena vidyāśruterbādhāditi | siddhāntastu | jñānasādhya eva mokṣaḥ na karmasādhyaḥ | jñānasādhyatve balavacchatiliṅgopapattismṛtīnāṃ bhāvāt‌ niravakāśāvadhāraṇayuktatvena śrutebarlavattvāt‌ | tathā bahukarmakṛtopīndrasya duḥkhāsambhinnasukharūpamokṣārthaṃ tattvajñānaprārthanaliṅgasya "indro'śvamedhān‌' iti śrutiprasiddhasya balavattvāt‌ | tathā'pi utpattināśavatphalatvenāvadhṛtakarmaṇā nityapumartho na bhavatītyupapatteśca | "nāstyakṛtaḥ kṛtena' iti  śrutisiddhatayā nirdoṣatayā ca prabalatvāt‌ | "karmaṇā badhyate' iti smṛteśca bhagavadvākyatayā yuktimattayā ca balavattvāt‌ | evaṃ balavatāṃ mokṣasya jñānasādhyatvanirṇāyakānāṃ anekasādhakānāṃ bhāvādeva karmaṇaivetyavadhāraṇasya karmaṇā saṃsiddhimāsthitā evetyayogavyavacchedakatayā sāvakāśatvāt‌ smṛteḥ na karmasādhyatvāpādane prāmāṇyamiti | phalaṃ tūpāsanākartavyatvasamarthanam‌ || 30 ||
|| oṃ anubandhādibhyaḥ oṃ || 31 ||
"darśanācca' iti  pūvartra darśanasya muktihetutayā pūrvanaye nirṇītatvena prakṛtatvāt‌ tasya bhaktyādisādhyatvamatrocyata iti saṅgatiḥ | prakṛtaṃ darśanaṃ kiṃ bhaktyādinā vinā pradānopetaśravaṇādimātrāt‌ bhavati uta neti cintā | tadarthaṃ darśanahetūpāsanaṃ bhaktyādinā vināpi bhavadapi phalaparyavasāyi uta neti | pūrvapakṣastu | bhaktyādinā vinā pradānayuktaśravaṇādimātrādeva darśanaṃ bhaviṣyati | "samyaglakṣaṇasampannaḥ' ityādi  smṛtyā guruprasādāsādhyasya kasyāpyabhāvāt‌ | na copāsanameva bhaktyādihīnasya neti yuktam‌ | dveṣeṇāpi tatsambhavāt‌ | naca dveṣayutopāsanaṃ na phalaparyavasāyīti yuktam‌ | "dveṣāccaidyādayo nṛpāḥ' iti saptamaskandhe dveṣeṇa dhyāyatāmapi muktismaraṇāditi | siddhāntastu | darśanaṃ na bhaktyādihīnātpradānayutopāsanādbhavati | kintu bhaktyādyupetādeva | bhaktyādinā vinā bhagavato'pyupāsanāya pumarthāhetutvasya pratyutānarthahetutvena "dveṣādyanmuktikathanaṃ śrutivākyavirodhi tat‌' ityupakramyānuvyākhyānoktānekamānasiddhatvena phalaparyavasāyyupāsanasya tu guruprasādasya ca bhaktyādinaiva siddherityasya "tathāpyanādisaṃsiddhabhaktyādiguṇapūrṇataḥ | labhedguruprasādam‌' ityādi  siddhatvāt‌ | "dveṣāccaidyādayaḥ' ityādestu svābhāvikabhaktaviṣayatayopapatteriti | phalaṃ tu bhaktyarthakapūrvapādīyamāhātmyoktisāphalyasamarthanam‌ || 31 ||
|| oṃ prajñāntarapṛthaktvavaddṛṣyiśca taduktam‌ oṃ || 32 ||
yaddarśane bhaktyādikamāvaśyakamityuktaṃ tasmindarśane tāratamyamatra nirūpyate iti saṅgatiḥ | prakṛtaṃ muktihetubhagavaddarśanaṃ kimekaprakāramutānekaprakāramiti cintā | tadarthaṃ "dṛṣṭvaiva taṃ mucyate'  "bhaktyā māmabhijānāti'  ityādivacanaṃ sarveṣāmekarūpajñānasya hetutāparamuta neti | tadarthaṃ sarveṣāmekarūpajñānasya hetutāparaṃ netyatra kalpakaṃ nāstyutāstīti | pūrvapakṣastu | sarveṣāṃ muktihetujñānaṃ ekarūpameva "dṛṣṭvaiva tam‌' iti śrutau sarveṣāmaviśeṣitajñānena mokṣaśravaṇāt‌ | "bhaktyā māmabhijānāti yāvānyaścāsmi tattvataḥ | tato māṃ tattvato jñātvā viśate tadanantaram‌' iti sarveṣāṃ bhaktyādinā pūrṇajñānotpatteḥ | sampūrṇajñānenaiva mokṣasya gītāyāṃ smaraṇāt‌ | naca śrutyādikaṃ viśeṣaparaṃ kalpakābhāvāt‌ | naca jñānahetūpāsanatāratamyameva kalpakam‌ | anekavidhasādhanavatāmapi kadācitkarṣakāṇāmiva sādhyasāmyopapattyā tasyāprayojakatvāt‌ | jñānasya nyūnādhikarūpaviśeṣavattve "bhaktyā' iti smṛtau sampūrṇajñānenaiva muktyukteḥ taddhīnānāṃ muktyabhāvāpātena "aniyamassarveṣām‌' ityuktabhaṅgaprasaṅgācceti | siddhāntastu | anekarūpamevādhikāriṇāṃ bhagavaddarśanam‌ | tatsādhanopāsanasya vicitratayā tajjanyajñāne'pi vaicityasyāvaśyakatvāt‌ sādhanānusāritvāt‌ phalasya | naca tadanyathopapattyā'prayojakam‌ | "antardṛṣyayo bahirdṛṣyayaḥ' ityādi  śrutau jñānavaicityasya pramitatvena sarveṣāṃ ekarūpasya bādhitatayā tenopāsanavaicityasyānyathopapatteḥ | kādācitkakarṣakadṛṣyāntamātreṇa kalpayitumanucitatvāt‌ | naca sarveṣāṃ muktyabhāvāpātadoṣaḥ | "dṛṣṭvaiva hyavatārāṇām‌' iti  smṛtyā taratamabhāvāpannajñānenaiva adhikāriṇāṃ mokṣāvagamena "bhaktyā' iti smṛteḥ sāmānyataḥ sarvaviṣayakajñānasya muktihetuparatvena vā hiraṇyagarbharūpādhikāriviśeṣaparatvenavā upapatteriti | phalaṃ tu yāvadityatroktamokṣatāratamyasamarthanam‌ || 32 ||
|| oṃ na sāmānyādapyupalabdhermṛtyuvannahi lokāpattiḥ oṃ || 33 ||
yadbhagavaddarśane vaicityaṃ taddarśanaṃ bimbaviṣayameva muktiheturiti nirūpaṇātsaṅgatiḥ | atra prakṛtaṃ bhagavaddarśanaṃ kiṃ yatkiñcidrūpaviṣayaṃ muktiheturuta bimbarūpaviṣayamiti cintā | tadarthaṃ rūpaviṣayakasyaiva muktihetutve na sthānato'pītyatra nirṇītabhagavadrūpāṇāṃ sāmyabhaṅgaḥ prasajyate'tha neti | tathā yatkiṃcidrūpadṛṣyeḥ muktihetutve vaiphalyaṃ tasyāḥ prasajyate na veti | pūrvapakṣastu | kasyacidrūpasya mokṣadānaśaktiranyasya netyaṅgīkāre "na sthānato'pi parasyobhayaliṅgaṃ sarvatra hi' ityukta  sarvarūpasāmyasya bhaṃgāpattyā yatkiṃcidrūpajñānamapi muktihetureva | kiṃ ca sāmānyadṛśermuktihetutve taddarśanasya vaiphalyaprasaṅgānna ca tadyuktam‌ | ato muktireva tatphalamiti sāmānyadṛṣyirapi muktihetureva | api ca yadi kiṃcidrūpajñānaṃ muktiheturanyanneti vikalpaḥ tarhi biṃbajñānamapi kiṃcinmuktihetuḥ kiṃcinnetyaniyama ityuktabhaṅgaprasaṅga iti | siddhāntastu | yathā
mṛtīnāṃ mṛtitvāviśeṣe'pi mṛtiviśeṣānantarameva muktiḥ evaṃ bhagavadrūpāṇāṃ samatve'pi bhagavatsaṅkalpānusāreṇa bimbarūpaviśeṣajñānameva muktihetuḥ na yatkiñcidrūpajñānam‌ | samo'pi bhagavān‌ svabimbadarśane evainaṃ mocayatīti śruteḥ | nacaivaṃ sāmānyadṛśervaiphalyam‌ | "sāmānyadarśanāllokāḥ' iti  smṛtyā darśane vikalpavat‌ bimbadarśane tadanāpatteriti | phalaṃ tu bimbopāsananaiyatyasamarthanam‌ || 33 ||
|| oṃ pareṇa ca śabdasya tādvidhyaṃ bhūyastvāttvanubandhaḥ oṃ || 34 ||
dṛśihetutvena prāguktabhakteratra svātantyanirāsātsaṅgatiḥ | prakṛtadarśanaṃ kiṃ bhaktisāmarthyena bhavati uta īśvaraśaktyeti cintā | tadarthaṃ "bhaktirevainaṃ darśayati' iti  śrutiḥ kiṃ bhakteḥ svātantyaparā atha karturīśvarasya dṛśidāne karaṇatvapareti | tadarthaṃ bhakteḥ karaṇatvaparatve kalpakaṃ nāsti bhaktireveti viśiṣyoktiśca yuktota viparītamiti | pūrvapakṣastu | "bhaktirevainaṃ darśayati' iti bhakteḥ kartṛtvaśravaṇasya karaṇatvaparatvakalpane kalpakābhāvāt‌ sādhanāntareṣu satsu viśiṣya bhakterevātrokteḥ bījābhāvenāyuktatvācca bhaktireveśvaraprasādamanapekṣya svaśaktyaiva bhagavadāparokṣyaṃ dadātīti | siddhāntastu | bhaktereva dṛśikartṛtve "etairupāyairyatate yastu vidvāṃstasyaiṣa ātmā viśate brahmadhāma  ityathavarṇaśrutau etaiḥ pravacanādyupāyaiḥ yo darśanārthaṃ yatate tasyārthe prasannaḥ paramātmā brahmadhāma vāyuṃ viśati tasyāvibharvati iti bhaktyādyupāyaiḥ prasanneśvarasyaiva svāparokṣadātṛtvagrahaṇena virodhāpattyā "bhaktirevainam‌' iti śruteḥ bhakteḥ kartṛparatvāyogena karaṇaparatvāt‌ sādhaneṣu bhakteḥ prādhānyena bhaktireveti viśiṣya bhakteruktyupapatteḥ | bhaktyādisarvaśabdānāṃ prathame'dhyāye bhagavati samanvayasyoktatvena prakṛtinayanyāyena strīliṅgabhaktiśabdasyāpi viṣṇau mukhyatayā śruterviṣṇuparatayopapatteḥ | "bhaktisthaḥ paramo viṣṇuḥ' ityādi  smṛtibalāt‌, anādibhaktiprasanneśvarasāmathyarena tadaparokṣajñānamiti | phalaṃ tu "tadavyaktamāhahi' ityukta  avyaktatvasamarthanam‌ || 34 ||
|| oṃ eka ātmanaḥ śarīre bhāvāt‌ oṃ || 35 ||
anādibhakterdṛṣyikāraṇatvoktirayuktā | "sthānaviśeṣāt‌' ityatra  bhāṣyādyuktidiśā anādibhaktyādisampattirūpayogyatāsāpekṣatvasyaivāyogāditi bhāvena śaṅkanātsaṅgatiḥ | atra prakṛtopāsanādau anādiyogyatāpekṣā nāstyutāstīti cintā | tadarthaṃ yogyatāyāḥ anāditvaṃ na sambhavatyuta sambhavatīti | tadarthaṃ ajurnadyaṃśānāṃ kiṃ svarūpeṇotpattirastyuta neti | tadarthaṃ aṃśāṃśinobhareda utābheda iti | tadarthamabhedasādhakaṃ nāsti bādhakaṃ cāsti atha viparītamiti | pūrvapakṣastu | aṃśāṃśinorabhede sādhakapramāṇābhāvāt‌ pratyutāṃśāṃśinoścaitramaitrayoriva jñānasukhaduḥkhādibhedopalambhasya abhāvaprasaṅgarūpabādhakabhāvācca bhedāvaśyaṃbhāvena aṃśina indrāderanāditve'pi arjunādyaṃśasya svarūpeṇotpattisambhavena indrādyaṃśānāmarjunādīnāṃ sāditvena taddhamaryogyatayā api tathātvena yogyāyā anāditvāyogāt‌ nā'nādiyogyatāpekṣāstyupāsanādāviti | siddhāntastu | aṃśasya svarūpotpattipakṣe taccharīrārjakapūrvatanatadanuṣṭhitakarmābhāvāt‌ mokṣābhāvaprasaṅgena karmanimittaśarīrāyogādananyagatyā "aṃnistu pṛthagjātā aṃśāstasyaiva karmaṇā' iti  smṛtyā ca aṃśikarmārjitaśarīre arjunādyaṃśānāmavasthānamityavaśyaṃ vācyatvāttasya cāṃśāṃśinorbhede'yogāt‌ tatkamarārjitaśarīre'vasthānānyathānupapattirūpeṇa aṃśigatapradhānasādhanopāsanādiphalasyāṃśenānusandhīyamānatvena tadanyathānupapattirūpeṇāṃśāṃśinorabhedasādhakamānena alpajñānasukhādyananusandhānasya mānuṣasaṃparkaprākṛtānnabhojanādinā bhagavadicchayopapattyā ca bādhakābhāvena cāṃśāṃśinorabhedāt‌ anādyabhinnāṃśasya svarūpotpattyayogena tasyāpyanāditvena taddharmabhūtayogyatāyā apyanāditvasambhavāt‌ astyanādiyogyatāpekṣopāsanādau iti | phalaṃ tu yogyatāhīnānāmapi upāsanaprasaṅgenāvyavasthāpattyabhāvaḥ || 35 ||
|| oṃ aṅgāvabaddhāstu na śākhāsu hi prativedam‌ oṃ || 36 ||
|| oṃ mantrādivadvā'virodhaḥ oṃ ||
pūrvatra prakṛtendrādidevatānāṃ tadyogyatānusāryupāsanamatrocyata iti saṅgatiḥ | atra prakṛta bhagavadupāsanaṃ kevalaṃ kāryamuta brahmāṃgadevatopāsanayuktamiti cintā | tadarthaṃ "tamevekaṃ jānatha'  "pūjayennānyadevatā' ityādau anyopāsti niṣedhaḥ kiṃ sarvātmanā uta prādhānyenetyatra jñāpakaṃ nāstyutāstīti | tathānyadevatopāsano'pi kimekatra pratītā guṇāḥ sarveṣāṃ dhyeyāḥ uta tattadyogyā eveti | pūrvapakṣastu | "tamevaikaṃ jānathetyādiśrutismṛtiṣu anyadevatopāsanapūjādeḥ niṣiddhatvātsvātanteṇaiva pūjāderniṣedha ityatra jñāpakābhāvāt‌ kevalameva bhagavadupāsanaṃ kāryam‌ | devatopāsteḥ karaṇe'pi ekatrapratītaguṇayuktatvenaiva sarvadevatopāstiḥ kāryā | devatvādeḥ sarvatropāsyatvadarśanādbādhakābhāvācceti | siddhāntastu | devānāṃ vedeṣu pratipādanasya "ādhyayanāya prayojanābhāvāt‌' iti  nyāyenopāsanārthatvena tadupāsanābhāve vedeṣu taduktervaiyarthyena taduktyanyathānupapattirūpasādhakasadbhāvāt‌ tata eva "tameva' ityādeḥ prādhānyenopāstiniṣedhaparatvāvaśyaṃbhāvena tadvirodhābhāvādanyadevatopāstiyuktatvenaiva bhagavadupāsanaṃ kāryam‌ | anyadevatopāsane'pi "samatvādvottamatvādvā' itayādi  smṛtyā devānāṃ nīcoccabhāvāpannatayā uttamaguṇā nādhameṣu dhyeyāḥ | kintu tattadyogyā eva | yathā bhagavadvācakavākyānāṃ na sarveṣāṃ sarvatrānvayaḥ kintu yogyānāmeva tadvat‌ adhamaguṇāstūttameṣu dhyeyāḥ | yathā'dhamadevatāpratipādakamantrāṇāmuttamapratipādakatvena dhyānaṃ tadvat‌ | tathopāsanamapi phalaviśeṣārthaṃ uttamādhikāribhireva kāryaṃ na sarvaiḥ | "upāsanāṃgadevānām‌' ityādi  smṛteriti phalaṃ tu devatāpratipādakavedabhāgasārthakyasamarthanam‌ || 36 ||
|| oṃ bhū naḥ kratuvajjyāyastvaṃ tathā ca darśayati oṃ || 37 ||
pūrvatra devatopāsane guṇopasaṃhṛtiphalasya sarvāprāpyatvāt‌ sā na sarvaiḥ kāryetyuktau tarhi bhūmaguṇopāstirapi na sarvaiḥ kāryā | niratiśayabhūmatvasya kenāpyaprāpyatvāditi śaṅkodayātsaṅgatiḥ | atra "yo vai bhūmā' ityādau  śrutau bhūmaguṇaḥ kiṃ na sarvopāsyaḥ atha sarvopāsyaḥ iti cintā | tadarthaṃ tadupāsanamanarthakamutārthavaditi | pūrvapakṣastu | yathā sambhṛtyādiguṇopāstiphalasya sarvāprāpyatvānna sambhṛtyādikaṃ sarvopāsyam‌ tathā niratiśayapūrṇatvarūpabhūmatvaṃ kasyāpyayogyatayā prāptumiṣyaṃ neti vyarthatvādbhūmatvaṃ na sarvopāsyam‌ | tasya sarvopāsyajñānānandādiviśeṣaṇatve'pi priyaśirastvādikamiva phalānupayogenānarthatvānna sarvopāsyaṃ bhūmatvamiti | siddhāntastu | "bhūmaiva devaḥ paramo hyupāsyaḥ' iti  śrutyā jñānānandādiguṇeṣu sarvatra bhūmatvasya sahabhāvenopāsane satyeva iṣyaphalasiddhiḥ anyathā tu netyavagamena jñānaṃ bhūmaṃ ānandaśca bhūmetyevaṃrūpeṇa bhūmaguṇopāsterabhimataphalaprāptihetutvenārthavattvātsarvairupāsyaṃ bhūmatvaṃ yathā dīkṣāprāyaṇīyodayanīyasavanatrayāvabhṛtātmakaḥ kratuḥ sarvayāgeṣvanuvartana eva sāphalyāt‌ anyathā vaiphalyājjyāyastvena sarvaiḥ kartavyastadvaditi | phalaṃ tu kāryākhyānādityatrālaukikaguṇopāsanasamarthanam‌ || 37 ||
|| oṃ nānā śabdādibhedāt‌ oṃ || 38 ||
pūrvaṃ sāmānyenoktasyātra viśeṣacintanādvā bhūmatvasya sarvopāsyatve "yāvat‌' ityatrokta  tāratamyabhaṃgāpattiriti śaṃkanādvā saṃgatiḥ | atra prakṛtaṃ bhūmatvaṃ adhikāriṇāmekarūpeṇa pratīyate'tha nānārūpeṇeti cintā | tadarthaṃ nānārūpeṇa pratītau tasyāḥ pratīteḥ bhrāntitvamāpadyate uta neti | tadarthaṃ bhūmatvamekarūpameva na nānārūpamapi sambhavatīti | pūrvapakṣastu | niratiśayitapūrṇatvarūpabhūmatve tāratamyasyāsambhāvitatayānupapannatvena ekarūpatvāt‌ | ekarūpasyāpi śravaṇamananāparokṣajñāneṣu tāratamyena bhāne śravaṇādermithyājñānatvaprasaṃgādekarūpeṇa pratīyate ityeva yuktamiti | siddhāntastu | atītavatsaramāsapakṣadinaghaṭikādyānantye tāratamyavadihāpi bhūmatve tāratamyasambhavena bhagavadguṇeṣu bhagavati ca tāvadānantyānāṃ sattvena tattadadhikāyarnusāreṇa tāvattāvadbhūmatvasya śravaṇamananāparokṣajñāneṣu pratītau tāvanmātramevetyapratītyā bhrāntitvānāpatteḥ nānārūpameva pratīyate iti nānārūpamevādhikāribhiḥ svasvaprāpyaphalānurodhenopāsyamiti | phalaṃ tu prāguktatāratamyasamatharnam‌ || 38 ||
|| oṃ vikalpo viśiṣyaphalatvāt‌ oṃ || 39 ||
pūrvatra guṇopāstau viśeṣaḥ ukto'tra tu rūpopāstau sa ucyate iti saṃgatiḥ | atra bhagavadupāsanaṃ kiṃ biṃbātiriktarūpaviṣayaṃ na kāryameveti niyama uta kāryaṃ na veti vikalpa iti cintā | tadarthaṃ tadupāsanasya phalaṃ nāstyutāstīti | tadarthaṃ tadupāsanasya mokṣaḥ phalamanyadapīti | pūrvapakṣastu | mumukṣubhiḥ kriyamāṇopāsanasya anyaphalakatvāyogena mokṣaphalasya "na sāmānyādapyupalabdheḥ'  ityatroktadiśā biṃbopāstisādhyatve mokṣaphalakatvasyāpyayogena ca niṣphalatvāt‌ pratyuta mokṣopayogibiṃbopāstivi nakaratvācca biṃbātiriktanṛsiṃhādyupāsanaṃ na kāryameveti | siddhāntastu | upāsakānāṃ mokṣakāmitve'pi mokṣahetubimbopāsanopayogitayā apekṣitaduritanivṛttiphalakatvena saphalatvāttādṛśaphalakāminā kāryaṃ nṛsiṃhādyupāsanam‌ | tīvrayā svabiṃbopāsanayaiva duritaṃ kṣapayituṃ samarthena tu na kāryamiti ṣoḍaśigrahaṇavat‌ vyavasthitavikalpaḥ | "muktyarthaṃ ātmayogyaṃ hi' ityādeḥ  smṛteriti | phalaṃ tu nṛsiṃhādipratipādakagranthasāphalyasamarthanam‌ || 39 ||
|| oṃ kāmyāstu yathākāmaṃ samuccīyeranna vā pūrvahetvabhāvāt‌ oṃ || 40 ||
mokṣopayogiphalatvena nṛsiṃhādyupāsanasya kāryatve tadupayogitvāt‌ kāmyarūpādyupāsanamapi kāryaṃ syāditi śaṃkanādvā nṛsiṃhādyupāsteriva kāmyarūpādyupāsterapi vaikalpikatvasādhanādvā saṃgatiḥ | atra kāmasādhanabhagavadrūpaguṇaviṣayakamapyupāsanamāvaśyakamuta vikalpa iti cintā | tadarthaṃ tadāvaśyakatve mokṣopayogādiheturastyuta neti | pūrvapakṣastu | arthādinā vinā jñānādisiddhayā arthādyārjakaṃ yadbhagavadrūpaṃ tadguṇādikaṃ tadupāsanasya jīvanādidvārā jñānopayogitvāt‌ arthakāmanāvaśyaṃbhāvāt‌ āvaśyakameva kāmyabhagavadrūpādyupāsanamiti | siddhāntastu | arthagṛhārāmakṣetravidhurāṇāmapi jñānasaṃpādanadarśanenāvaśyaṃ arthādinaiva jñānamiti niyamābhāvena tasya mokṣahetūpāsanānupayogāt‌ adhikāriṇāṃ mumukṣutvenārthādikāmanābhāvācca kāmyopāsanaṃ mumukṣubhirna kāryaṃ anyaiḥ kāryamityadhikāribhedena vā mumukṣurapyarthādyarthaṃ na kāryaṃ bhagavatprītyarthaṃ kāryamiti phalabhedena vā vyavasthitavikalpa eva natvāvaśyakamiti niyamaḥ "yasya yasya hi' iti  smṛteriti | phalaṃ tu vairāgyapādoktavairāgyaphalakagarbhayātanādinirūpaṇasamarthanam‌ || 40 ||
|| oṃ aṃgeṣu yathāśrayabhāvaḥ oṃ || 41 ||
|| oṃ śiṣyeśca oṃ || oṃ samāhārāt‌ oṃ || oṃ guṇasādhāraṇyaśruteśca oṃ ||
pūrvatra kāmyopāsteradhikārisaṃbhavena kāryatve'pīhāṃgadevatopāstestadabhāvenākāryatvamiti śaṃkanātsaṃgatiḥ | atra bhagavadaṃgāśritatvena brahmādidevatāviṣayakamupāsanaṃ na kāyarmuta kāryamiti cintā | tadarthaṃ śrutau devatānāmaṃgāśritatvakathanaṃ tathopasanavidhānaṃ guṇasādhāraṇaśravaṇaṃ ca tathopāsanakāryatvāpādanakṣamaṃ na bhavatyuta bhavatīti | tadarthaṃ tatkimupāsanaṃ niradhikārikamuta sādhikārikamiti | tadarthamadhikārikalpakaṃ nāstyutāstīti | pūrvapakṣastu | tathopāsanaṃ na kāyarṃ hetvabhāvāt‌ | naca "brahmā śirasi lalāṭe rudraḥ' ityādi śrutau devānāmaṃgāśritatvenokteḥ "ādhyāyanāya prayojanābhāvāt‌' iti  nyāyenopāsanārthatvāttaduktyanyathānupapattiḥ | "yasminyasminyo hi cāṃge niviṣyaḥ parasya cintyaḥ sa tathā tathaiva' iti  śrutau vidhānaṃ "sādhāraṇyātsarvaguṇaḥ parasya samāhāryāstattvadṛ mumukṣoḥ' iti  jñānānandādiguṇataulyena sūryādyāśraya cakṣustvādīśaguṇānāmupāsyatvaśravaṇaṃ ca tathopāsanākāryatve heturiti vācyam‌ | tathopāsanasya sarvādhikārikatve ānandādaya ityuktacaturguṇopāstiniyamabhaṃgāt‌ nahi brahmāśritaśiraskatvādikaṃ bhūmatvamivānandādiviśeṣaṇaṃ yena prāguktaniyativyāghātakaṃ syāt‌ | naca devādhikārikaṃ tadupāsanaṃ kalpakābhāvāt‌ | nahi rājā rājasūryena yajetetyādau rājādipadamiva brahmā śirasītyādau devā ete upāsīranniti vākyamasti | tādṛśaviśeṣavidhyabhāve'pi adhikāriviśeṣakalpane "codanādyaviśeṣāt‌'  iti sarvavedāntanayoktanyāyavirodhaḥ | ataḥ tathopāsanasya
niradhikārikatvāt‌ śrutau taduktyādikaṃ svayaṃ vyarthaṃ sat‌ na tathopāsanakāryatāpādanakṣamamiti | siddhāntasta | kāryameva tathopāsanam‌ | naca hetvabhāvaḥ śrutau taduktyanyathānupapattyāderanuṣṭhāpakatvāt‌ | naca niradhikārikatvadoṣaḥ | aṃgaiḥ parādye hi devā visṛṣyāḥ'  iti śrutau brahmāśritaśiraskatvarudrāśritalalāṭatvasūryāśritacakṣuṣṭvādiguṇopāsanasya paramasthānaprāptirūpaphalaśravaṇāt‌ | tasya ca devaprāpyatvena phalaparyālocanayā madhuvidyāyāmiva devānāmevādhikāritvakalpanopapatteriti | phalaṃ tu tadvidyāsārthakyasamarthanam‌ || 41 ||
|| oṃ na vā tattsahabhāvaśruteḥ oṃ || 42 ||
|| oṃ darśanācca oṃ ||
atra prāguktameva viśeṣahetunākṣipya samarthyata iti saṃgatiḥ | atra brahmāśritariskatajavādibhagavadguṇopāsanaṃ na kāryamuta kāryamiti cintā | tadarthaṃ tadupāsanasya sarvaśākhāsvanuktirakāryatvena vinānupapannotānyathopapanneti | tadarthamanyathopapattiphakalpakaṃ nāstyutāstīti | pūrvapakṣastu | na kāryameva tathopāsanaṃ sarvaśākhāsvanuktatvāt‌ | avaśyakāryatve sarvaśākhāgatopāsanena sahabhāvaprasaṃgāt‌ | atra sarvatrānuktiḥ akāryatvena vinānupapannā tadeva gamayatīti | siddhāntastu | sarvathā kāryatvābhāve kvacittaduktervaiyarthyaprasaṃgāt‌ "satyo jñānaḥ paramānandarūpaḥ' ityādi  śrutyā jñānādiguṇacatuṣyayā adhikaguṇānāṃ devopāsyatvakathanena tathopāsanasya devamātrādhikārikatvena sarvatrānukteḥ | sarvairakāryatvenānyathopapatteḥ kāryameva tathopāsanamiti | phalaṃ tu prāguktaprameyasamarthanam‌ || 42 ||
|| iti śrīmadrāghavendrayatikṛtāyāṃ nyāyamuktāvalyāṃ tṛtīyādhyāyasya tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ samāptaḥ ||
|| atha caturthaḥ pādaḥ |
|| oṃ puruṣārtho'taḥśabdāditi bādarāyaṇaḥ oṃ || 1 ||
|| oṃ śeṣatvātpuruṣārthavādo yathā'nyeṣviti jaiminiḥ oṃ || oṃ ācāradaśarnāt‌ oṃ || oṃ tacchateḥ oṃ || oṃ samanvārambhaṇāt‌ oṃ || oṃ tadvato vidhānāt‌ oṃ || oṃ niyamācca oṃ || oṃ adhikopadeśāttu bādarāyaṇasyaivaṃ taddarśanāt‌ oṃ || oṃ tulyaṃ tu darśanam‌ oṃ ||
pūrvapādoktopāsanāsādhyajñānamāhātmyamatrocyate iti pūrvapādenāsya saṃgatiḥ | atra jñānaṃ kiṃ mokṣamātre heturuta mokṣetarasya svargādipuruṣārthaheturapīti cintā | tadarthaṃ tatra mānaṃ nāstyutāstīti | pūrvapakṣastu | na jñānaṃ mokṣetarapuruṣārthasyāpi hetuḥ | mumukṣostratrecchābhāvāt‌ | saubharyāderivecchāyāḥ bhāve'pi taddhetutve mānābhāvāt‌ | taddhetutvaparaśrutyādeḥ kamarṇaḥ tathātvapratipādakaśrutyādivirodhāt‌ | "karmaiva dehaṃ daivikaṃ mānuṣaṃ vāpyanvārabhenna parastatra hetuḥ' ityādi śrutau karmaṇa eva mokṣetaraphalahetutvasya "yadeva vidyayā' iti śrutau jñānyanuṣṭhite karmajanyaphale'tiśayādhāyakatvena jñāne kamarśeṣatvasya ca śravaṇāt‌ | ata eva jñānakarmaṇoḥ svargādau tulyakakṣatayā hetutvaśaṃkānavakāśaḥ | tathā "naiva daipadam‌' iti smṛtyā jñānināmapi devānāṃ "yajñena yajñamayajanta devāḥ' iti karmānuṣṭhānadarśanena jñānasyaiva sarvamumarthahetutve tadanupapattyā ca karmaṇa eva hetutvānumānācca | evameva jaiminirācāryeraṃgīkārācca | naca jñānināmācāro muktavallīlayānyathopapanna iti śaṃkyaṃ "jñānī ca karmāṇi sadoditāni kuryāt‌' iti  śrutau jñānino'pi karmavidhānāt‌ | vihitatvasya ca līlātvāyogāt‌ | "kurvanneveha karmāṇi' iti  uttarārdhe evaṃ karmakaraṇe tvayi pāpaṃ karma na lipyate iti nāstīti jñānino'pi karmākaraṇe pratyavāyaśravaṇācca | jñānyācārasya līlātvāyogādataḥ śrutyācāraviruddhatvāt‌ jaiminimatavirodhācca "yaṃ yaṃ lokaṃ manasā' ityādikaṃ  jñānasya sarvapumarthahetutvaparaṃ vākyaṃ svargahetukarmaśeṣe'pi dhanādau "svargaṃ dhanāddehato vai gṛhācca prāpsyaṃti' ityādau prādhānyoktivat‌
karmaśeṣabhūtajñānaprādhānyoktiparaṃ neyam‌ | natu sākṣād‌jñānasya sarvapumarthahetujñānaśeṣatvaparam‌ | niravakāśakalpakābhāvātkarmaṇaḥ kṛtāvakṛtau ca jñānaṃ mokṣārjanakṣamameveti stutinaye vakṣyamāṇatvena jñāne'tiśayakaratvayogena taccheṣatvāyogācceti | siddhāntastu | kamarṇo jñāne'tiśayādhāyakatvena taccheṣatvāyoge'pi "jñānādeva svargo jñānādevāpavargaḥ' iti  śrutau jñānādeva svargādiphalaprāpteḥ karmaṇo jñānajanyaphale'tiśayādhāyakatvena jñānaśeṣatvasya ca śravaṇāt‌ "rājasūyajitān‌ lokānaśvamedhābhivardhitān‌ | prā nuhi tvaṃ mahābhāga' iti bhāratavākyena yudhiṣṭhirādīnāṃ rājasūyayāgādinā phalādhikyadarśanarūpaliṃgācca jñānasyaiva sarvamuparthahetutvasya karmaṇaḥ jñānajanyaphale'tiśayādhāyakatvena jñānaśeṣatvasyāvagamena asya bādarāyaṇamatatvena ca karmaṇaḥ taddhetutvaparavākyādīnāṃ taddhetujñānaśeṣaparatvena neyatayā tadvirodhābhāvāt‌ "yaṃ yaṃ lokam‌' ityādi vākyabalāt‌ bādarāyaṇasaṃmatatvācca jñānaṃ mokṣetarasarvapumarthaheturbhavatyeveti | nacaivaṃ jaimini bādarāyaṇamatayovirrodhaḥ devamānuṣarūpādhikāribhedenāvirodhāditi | phalaṃ tu jñānādāvatiśayena pravṛttiriti || 1 ||
|| oṃ asārvatrikī oṃ || 2 ||
|| oṃ vibhāgaḥ śatavat‌ oṃ || oṃ adhyayanamātravataḥ oṃ ||
pūrvatra jñānātsarvapumarthāptau na jñānināṃ sarveṣāṃ adhikāraḥ kiṃtu uttamānāṃ tatrāpi apratibaddhānāmityuktam‌ | iha jñāne sarveṣāmadhikāro netyucyate iti saṃgatiḥ | sarvapumatharhetutvena pūrvatra prakṛte jñāne sarveṣāmadhikāro'styuta neti cintā | tadarthaṃ puruṣavibhāgo nāṃgīkāryo'thāṃgīkārārha iti | tadarthamarthitvādikamevādhikāre tantramutānyadapīti | tadarthamarthitvādyadhikāraprayojakaṃ nāstyutāstīti | pūrvapakṣastu | "arthī samartho vidvānadhikriyate' iti nyāyasiddhaphalāpekṣitvādyanyasya adhikāraprayojakasyābhāvāt‌ tasya ca nirduḥkhasukharūpamokṣe'rthitvasya śravaṇādisāmarthyasya tadupayuktavaiduṣyasya ca saṃbhavena sarveṣu bhāvāt‌ sarveṣāmarthitvarūpaprayojakasāmye'pi keṣāṃcidevādhikāro nānyeṣāmiti puruṣavibhāgāṃgīkārasya nirbījatayā anucitatayā keṣāṃcidabhāve'nyeṣāmapyabhāvāpātena jñānasya niradhikārikatvaprasaṃgāt‌ asti jñāne sarveṣāmadhikāra iti | siddhāntastu | "paṭhedvedānathārthānadhīyītātha vicārya brahma vindet‌  "avaiṣṇavasya vede'pi hyadhikāro na vidyate'  ityādiśrutismṛtisiddhasya sāṃgasaphalayathāśaktikṛtsnādhyayanavattvarūpaprayojakāntarasyāpi sattvena arthitvādimātrasyātantratā | "na ca mokṣo hi devānām‌' iti smṛtyuktadiśā sarveṣāṃ devatvasāmye'pi parāparabrahmadvayādhikaśatasyaiva somādhikāro nānyeṣāmiti puruṣavibhāgasya yuktatvāt‌ na sarveṣāṃ jñānādhikāra iti | phalaṃ tu karmāpekṣayā jñāne'tiśayasamarthanam‌ || 2 || 
|| oṃ nāviśeṣāt‌ oṃ || 3 ||
pūrvaṃ keṣāṃcideva jñānādhikāra ityuktamatra tu keṣāṃcitsādhye'pi saṃpūrṇādhikāro na sarveṣāmapi samarthanātsaṃgatiḥ | prakṛto jñānādhikāraḥ kiṃ sarveṣāṃ devādīnāmekaprakāro'tha nānāprakāra iti cintā | tadarthaṃ "prajñāntarapṛthaktvadṛṣyiśca' ityatrokta  dṛṣyitāratamyamadhikāranānātvena vinā adhikāriśaktinānātvenāpyupapadyate'tha neti | pūrvapakṣastu | adhikāriṇāmuccāvacaśaktyaiva prāguktajñānatāratamyopapatteḥ kalpakābhāvānna nānārūpe'dhikāra iti | siddhāntastu | adhikāriṇāṃ śakteruccāvacatvasya adhikāroccāvacatvanimittatvena tadabhāvena śaktitāratamyasyāpyabhāvāt‌ | upajīvyatvādadhikāratāratamyenaiva prāguktajñānatāratamyasya nirvāhyatvena tadanyathānupapattyā "atha pumarthasādhanāni' iti bhāṣyokta śrutyā cādhikāro nānāprakāra iti devottamānāmeva saṃpūrṇādhikāro nānyeṣāmiti | phalaṃ tu jñānātiśayasamarthanam‌ || 3 ||
|| oṃ stutaye'numitirvā oṃ || 4 ||
|| oṃ kāmakāreṇa caike oṃ || oṃ upamardaṃ ca oṃ || oṃ ūrdhvaretastu ca śabde hi oṃ || oṃ parāmarśaṃ jaiminiracodanā cāpavadati hi oṃ || oṃ anuṣṭheyaṃ bādarāyaṇaḥ sāmyaśruteḥ oṃ || oṃ vidhirvā dhāraṇavat‌ oṃ || oṃ stutimātramupādanāditi cennāpūrvatvāt‌ oṃ || oṃ bhāvaśabdācca oṃ || oṃ pāriplavārthā iti cenna viśeṣitatvāt‌ oṃ || oṃ tathā caikavākyopabandhāt‌ oṃ || oṃ ata evā cāgnīndhanādyanapekṣā oṃ || oṃ sarvāpekṣā ca yajñādiśruteraśvavat‌ oṃ || oṃ śamadamādyupetaḥ syāttathā'pi tu tadvidhestadaṃgatayā teṣāmavaśyānuṣṭheyatvāt‌ oṃ || oṃ sarvānnānumatiśca prāṇātyaye taddarśanāt‌ oṃ || oṃ abādhācca oṃ || oṃ api smaryate oṃ || oṃ śabdaścāto'kāmacāre oṃ || oṃ vihitatvāccāśramakarmāpi oṃ || oṃ sahakāritvena ca oṃ ||
evaṃ pūrvatrādhikāribhede siddhe atra trividhādhikāribhedena viruddhārthakatrividhavākyānāmavirodho varṇyata iti saṃgatiḥ | atra prakṛtajñānavataḥ kiṃ sadācāradurācārābhyāṃ viśeṣo nāstyutāstīti cintā | tadarthaṃ "sa brāhmaṇaḥ kena syādyena syāttenedṛśa eva' iti  vākyaṃ kiṃ jñānimātrasya yatheṣyācaravidhiparamuta niyamena sarvasadācārānuṣṭhānādiparamiti | tatra jñāninaḥ satpravṛttyādinā viśeṣasattve jñānasya muktihetutvaniyamabhaṃgādirūpaṃ jñānakarmasamuccayavādarūpaṃ ca bādhakamastyuta neti cintā | tadarthaṃ jñānino'satpravṛttiḥ mokṣasvarūpapratibandhotpattau hetuḥ uta mokṣagatasukhādhikyapratibandhotpattau heturiti | tadarthaṃ asatpravṛttyādinā sukhādhikyapratibandhādirūpaviśeṣasattve "yadi ha vā apyevaṃvinnikhilaṃ bhakṣayītaivameva sa bhavati' iti śrutau jñāninaḥ sarvānnabhakṣaṇe'pi aviśeṣoktirayuktota yukteti | pūvarpakṣastu | sadasatpravṛttibhyāṃ nāsti jñānino viśeṣaḥ "sa brāhmaṇaḥ' iti śrutau jñānimātraṃ prati yatheṣyācārasya vidhiliṃṅaṃtasyācchabdena vidhānāt‌ sadasatpravṛttibhyāṃ jñānino viśeṣavidherapyabhāve sarvavidhyatidūratvarūpeśalakṣaṇasyāpi jñānini prasaṅgāt‌ | na cedaṃ vākyaṃ jñānimātrasya na yatheṣyācāravidhiparaṃ kiṃtu jñāniviśeṣasyeti yuktam‌ | sāmānyavākyasya viśeṣaparatvakalpakābhāvāt‌ | evaṃ ca na kevalaṃ yatheṣyācāre vidhyanyathānupapattyā'viśeṣo jñāniḥ kalpyate kiṃtu "tenedṛśa eva' iti svecchācaraṇāviśeṣaśravaṇācca | nanu na jñānimātrasya svecchācāravidhiparatvamasya vākyasyopapadyate | "prātarutthāyātha sandhyāmupāsīta' ityādi śrutyanurodhena niṣiddhaṃ kāryamiti vidhyabhāvarūpayā "brāhmaṇo na hantavyaḥ' ityādiniṣedharūpayā yaktyā ca vidhyādividhibandhavarjitatayā niyamena sarvasadācārānuṣṭhānaparatvamasya vākyasya paramāptajaiminyācāryairyadvarṇitaṃ tathā "yastvātmaratireva syādātmatṛptaśca mānavaḥ | ātmanyeva ca santuṣyastasya kāryaṃ na vidyate ||'  iti smṛtyā ātmaratiḥ paramātmadarśananimittasukhamāptaḥ ātmanyeva ca santuṣyaḥ paramātmanimittasantoṣavattvādevahetoḥ ātmatṛptaḥ paramātmaprasādena viṣayeṣvalaṃbuddhimāptaḥ yaḥ tasya kāryaṃ nāstītyuktyanurodhena anācaraṇaprayuktāsāmyaniṣedhakedṛśa eveti vākyaśeṣabalena śrutyādyuktyā ācāramadhye keṣāṃciccaraṇaṃ keṣāṃcidacaraṇamityevaṃrūpakāmācāraparatvasya yadbādarāyaṇācāryairvarṇitaṃ tadubhayārthaparatve ca sati kathaṃ vidhirūpaikārthatvaparatvamupetya jñānino yatheṣyācārepyaviśeṣa ucyate iti cenmaivam‌ | tathātve vākyasya pāriplavārthatvāpattyā prāmāṇyābhāvaprasaṅgāt‌ | kiṃca māstu sādhakābhāvāt‌ jñānino viśeṣābhāvaḥ viśeṣe sādhakabhāvādbhaviṣyati | tathāhi | yadi jñāninaḥ sadasatpravṛttibhyāṃ viśeṣaḥ syāttarhi asatpravṛttyā kuṇṭhitaśaktikasya jñānasya mokṣahetutvabhaṃgaprasaṃgena "aniyamassarveṣām‌' ityuktamokṣaniyamabhaṃgaḥ syāt‌ | satpravṛttisamuccitasya jñānasya muktihetutāpattyā "vidyaiva tu nirdhāraṇāt‌'  ityuktivirodhassyāt‌ | jñānino'satpravṛttyā mokṣāpratibandhe jijñāsorapi tathā jñānāpratibandhāpātena vairāgyapādānarthakyaprasaṃgāt‌ | naca satpravṛttyādeḥ na muktisvarūpe vyāpāraḥ api tu muktāvānandātiśayābhivyaktitatpratibandhayoḥ vyāpāra iti yuktam‌ | tathātve jñāninaḥ sarvānnabhakṣarānumatiśrutivyākopaḥ tatra sarvānnabhakṣaṇe'pi aviśeṣasyaivokteḥ | naca tasya viṣayāntaraparatve kalpakamastīti | siddhāntastu | astyeva jñāninaḥ sadasatpravṛttibhyāṃ viśeṣaḥ kena syāditi vākyasya jñānimātraparatayā pratītasyāpi jñānistutimātraparatve sarvavidhyatidūratvarūpeśalakṣaṇasya anīśe'tiptyādhyāpattirūpabādhakena vidhiparatve'pi jaiminibādarayāṇābhyāmuktarītyā sarvasadācārānuṣṭhānavidhiparatvam‌ | sarvamadhye keṣāṃciddharmāṇāmananuṣṭhanāmityevaparatvaṃ taduktarītyā svecchācāravidhiparatvamityarthatrayaparatvasyāpi "vidhiniyatā manuṣyāḥ, aniyatā hi devāḥ, brahmaiva svecchāniyataḥ' iti gaupavanaśrutau "svecchayaiva pravṛttistu brahmaṇo vidhicoditaḥ' ityādi  smṛtau ca mānuṣadevāviriṃcarūpādhikāribhedena vyavasthoktyā virodhābhāvena jñānimātrasya svecchācārasya vākyasya prāmāṇyābhāvāt‌ | jñānino'satpravṛttyā mokṣāpratibandhasya
"kāmacārāḥ kāmabhakṣāḥ' iti  śrutyā jñānino nivartyatayā prāptasyārabdhakarmaṇo'pi kiṃcidupabhuktasya jñānenopamardasya "ātmānamabhipaśyopamṛdya puṇyaṃ ca pāpaṃ ca' iti  śrutisiddhatayā kaimutyanyāyena ca siddhatvena aniyama ityuktaniyamabhaṃgarūpasya "ya imaṃ paramaṃ guhyamūrdhvaretassu bhāṣayet‌' iti  ūrdhvaretastvādiguṇayukteṣvevopadeśavidhānena jñāne'satpravṛttyā pratibandhasya pramitatvena atiprasaṃgarūpasya kevalasya jñānasyaiva mokṣasvarūpaniṣpādakatve'pi "paśyannapīmamātmānaṃ kuryātkarmāvicārayan‌ | yadātmanassuniyatamānandotkarṣamā nuyāt‌' ityādi  vākyabalena mokṣe sukhātiśayārthe sahakāritvena satpravṛttyasatpravṛttivarjanayoḥ apekṣāyāmapi jñānakarmasamuccayavādāpattirūpasya ca bādhakasyābhāvāt‌ sarvānnānumatiśruteśca bhāṣyādyuktaliṃgena prāṇatyāgaviṣayatvena sāvakāśatvāt‌ | kiṃ ca jñānino niṣiddhasyākaraṇe bādhakābhāvāt‌ svecchācāra ucyate, karaṇaṣa pramāṇābhāvādvā, niṣedhakapramāṇābhāvādvā, karaṇe bādhakābhāvādvā | nādyadvitīyau | asiddheḥ | na tṛtīyaḥ | "evaṃ paśyanna kāmacaritaṃ caret‌' iti  "atītānāgatajñānī trailokyoddharaṇakṣamaḥ | etādṛśo'pi nācāraṃ śrautaṃ smārtaṃ parityajet‌' iti ca niṣedhakaśrutismṛtibhāvāt‌ | na caturthaḥ | "ānando hrasate'kāryāt‌' iti smṛtyā ānandahrāsarūpabādhakasya bhāvāt‌ | tasmādastyeva jñāninaḥ sadasatpravṛttibhyāṃ maktivānandahrāsarūpo viśeṣa iti | phalaṃ tu asadācāre'pi muktisaṃpādanarūpajñānamahimasamarthanam‌ || 4 ||
|| oṃ sarvathā'pi tu ta evobhayaliṃgāt‌ oṃ || 5 ||
|| oṃ anabhibhavaṃ ca daśaryati oṃ || oṃ antarā cāpi tu taddṛṣyeḥ oṃ || oṃ api smaryate oṃ || oṃ viśeṣānugrahaṃ ca oṃ || oṃ atastvitarajjyāyo liṃgācca oṃ || oṃ tadbhūtasya tu tadbhāvo jaiminerapi niyamātadrūpābhāvebhyaḥ oṃ ||
pūrvatra viśeṣitatvādityatra  prakṛtaiḥ mandamadhyottamarūpādhikāribhireva jñānaṃ prāpya nānyairityatrocyata iti saṃgatiḥ | atra jñānaṃ kimayogyairapyāpyaṃ uta prakṛtayogyaireveti cintā | tadarthaṃ yogyairevāpyamityatra kalpakaṃ nāstyutāstīti | tathā yogyaprāpyatvapakṣe'pi kimayogyatāyāḥ mahāprayatnenābhibhavena teṣāmapi yogyatotpattirastyuta neti | tadarthamayogyatā kiṃ teṣāmasvabhāvaḥ uta svabhāva iti | tadarthaṃ tasyāḥ svabhāvatvena anabhibhave jñāpakaṃ nāstyutāstīti | pūrvapakṣastu | yogyānāmevetyatra pramāṇābhāvāt‌ vedāyogyasya prayatnena vedaprāptidarśanenāyogyānāmapi yatnena jñānaprāptirastyeva | jñānasya yogyamātraprāpyatvapakṣe'pi ayogyānāmayogyatābhibhavena vipratvadarśanāt‌ | na kṣatriyatvamasvābhāvikaṃ ayogyatvaṃ svābhāvikaṃ na prayatnenāpyanivartyamiti śaṃkyam‌ | svābhāvikatve mānābhāvāt‌ | svābhāvikatve yavakalamabījāderiva svabhāvānusāriphalatvaprasaṃgācceti | siddhāntastu | deśakālagurūpasattyādimahotsāhe'pi ayogyānāṃ na jñānaprāptiḥ | kiṃ tu yogyānāmeva | indravirocanayoścaturmukhopadeśe samāne'pi na samyak‌ jñānaprāptiḥ pratyuta viparītajñānaprāptirityasya chānyogye'ṣyame spaṣyatvenendrāderjñāprāptyādiliṅgena tathājñānahetuprasādasya muktiyogyeṣveva "ṛṇve vīra ugram‌' ityādi  siddhatvena tadanyathānupapattyā ca tathāvagamāt‌ | na cāyogyānāmapi mahāprayatnenāyogyatābhibhavena yogyatotpattirastviti yuktam‌ | ayogyatāyāḥ svabhāvatvāt‌ | naca jñāpakābhāvaḥ | "daivīmeva sampattiṃ devāḥ' iti śrutau "asurā āsureṇaiva svabhāvena' iti  smṛtau ca anabhibhavasya svabhāvatvasya ca spaṣyaṃ śravaṇāt‌ | tathā "nāsurā daivīṃ na devā āsurīm‌' ityādi niyamaśrutyā "nāsurāṇāṃ daivaṃ rūpam‌' ityatadrūpavattvaśrutyā "taṃ bhūtiriti devā upāsāṃcakrire' iti  devāsurayoranyonyaviparītopāsanena samyak‌phalaviparītaphalaśrutyā ca devāsuramānuṣāṇāṃ devatvādisvabhāvatvapratipādakajaiminyācāryamatena ca "kanīyasā eva devāḥ jyāyasā asurāḥ' iti  śrutyā "na jānāmi' iti  janasamūhapraveśaniṣedhānyathānupapattirūpaliṃgena cāyogyatādeḥ svabhāvatvānabhibhavayoḥ tattatsvabhāvānusāriphalasattvāvagamāditi | phalaṃ tu mokṣasya yogyatāmātraprāpyatvasamarthanam‌ || 5 ||
|| oṃ na cādhikārikamapi patanānumānāttadayogāt‌ oṃ || 6 ||
|| oṃ upapūrvamapītyeke bhāvaśamanavattaduktam‌ oṃ || oṃ bahistūbhayathāpi smṛterācārācca oṃ ||
pūrvaṃ jñānasyāyogyaprāpyatvamuktamatra tu yogyeṣvapi devādipadākāṃkṣāśūnyaireva prāpyate na sarvairityucyate iti vā prāguktadevatvādeḥ svābhāvikatvamayuktam‌ | prayatnasādhyatāśravaṇena devatvasyāsvābhāvikatvāditi śaṃkanādvānaṃtarasaṃgatiḥ | pūrvatra devānāṃ prakṛtatvāt‌ saṃgataṃ devapadaṃ devetaraiḥ prāptuṃ yogyamuta devaireveti cintā | tadarthaṃ tatpadaprāptyarthaṃ icchāprayatnakṛtau bādhakaṃ nāstyutāstīti | pūrvapakṣastu | "svadharmaniṣṭhaḥ śatajanmabhiḥ pumān‌ viriṃcatāmeti'  "vayā viṣṇoreṣasya prabhṛthe havirbhiḥ videhi rudro rudriyaṃ mahitvam‌' ityādi vākyabalena viriṃcatvādeḥ yatnasādhyatāvagamena cetanatvādivat‌ devatvādeḥ svābhāvikatvābhāvena yatnaiḥ sarvaiḥ prāptuṃ śakyatvāccetanatvasya svābhāvikatvasya sarvasaṃmatatvāt‌ devatvādiprāptau icchāprayatnakṛtau bādhakābhāvāt‌ | anyathā jñānabhaktyādīcchaprayatnāvapi na syātāmiti | devādanyairdevādipadaṃ prāpyameva kiṃ bahunā bhagavadaiśvaryamapi yatnena prāpyamiti | siddhāntastu | devatāpadaṃ bhagavadaiśvaryaṃ vā na prāpyaṃ tadicchāprayatnayorevāyogāt‌ | brahmādipadecchāvān‌ devādibhiḥ pātyate aśakyatatpadecchutvāt‌ rājapadecchuvat‌ | devādipadaprayatnavān‌ patati ayogyārohaprayatnavatvāt‌ ayogyavṛkṣārohaprayatnayuktavadityanumānena anarthāvagamāt‌ | naca cetanatvavaddehatvādikaṃ na svābhāvikaṃ iti ayogyatvādiviśeṣaṇamasiddham‌ | "devāsuranaratvādyā jīvānāṃ tu nisargataḥ' iti vacanena svabhāvatvāvagamāt‌ "anyatra śubhamākāṃkṣannapatedavirodhataḥ' iti vacanena jñānādyākāṃkṣāyāmanarthābhāvasyaivāvagame'tiprasaṃgābhāvāditi | phalaṃ tu devatāpadavat‌ jñānamapi yogyaprāpyameveti prāguktasamarthanam‌ || 6 ||
|| oṃ svāminaḥ phalaśruterityātreyaḥ oṃ || 7 ||
|| oṃ ārtvijyamityauḍulomistasmai hi parikriyate oṃ || oṃ sahakāryantaravidhiḥ pakṣeṇa tṛtīyaṃ tadvato vidhyādivat‌ oṃ ||
pūrvatra jñānasyetthambhāva uktaḥ atra tu phalaniṣpādanaprakāraḥ ucyate iti saṃgatiḥ | prakṛtaṃ jñānaṃ tattvābhimānidevānāmeva phalaniṣpādakamuta prajānāmapīti cintā | tadarthaṃ kriyājanyaphalatve prayojakakartṛtvameva prayojakamuta kartṛtvamātramapīti | kartṛtvamapītyatra heturnāsti bādhakaṃ cāsti uta viparītamiti | tadarthaṃ kartṛtvamapi prayojakamiti pakṣe'pi kiṃ prayojakakatarṛsamānaphalatvamāvaśyakamuta neti | pūrvapakṣastu | kartṛtvasyāpi prayojakatve saṃkocakamānābhāvena svāmiphalasamaphalamevāvaśyaṃ vācyam‌ | tacca "svāmī hi phalamaśnute nāsvāmī kamar kurvāṇaḥ' iti  śrutyā ātreyācāryamatena ca viruddham‌ | loke takṣādeḥ prayojakādhīnakarturapi kriyājanyaphalādṛṣyeśca śrutyādivirodharūpabādhakābhāvāt‌ sādhakābhāvācca na kartṛtvamapi prayojakam‌ | kiṃtu prayojakakartṛtvarūpasvāmitvamapi | sarvendriyādiprerakāṇāṃ devānāmeva prajākṛtaśravaṇādijanyajñānaphalaṃ na prajānāmiti | siddhāntastu | tattvābhimānidevānāṃ prajākṛtaśravaṇādau prayojakakartṛtvena tatphalasya saṃpūrṇasya "svāmī hi' iti śrutyā "tato mahattaraṃ proktaṃ devānām‌' iti  smṛtyā''treyamatena ca sattvāvagame'pi devānāmiti śrutivihite jñānadānādikarmaṇi prajānāṃ jñānotpattyanugrahāyaiva sahakāritvenopādānāt‌ | satre vaidehādinā gṛhapatinā sahakāritvenopāttānāmṛtttijāmagṛhapatīnāmapi phaladarśanācca asti prajānāmapi kartṛtvanimittaṃ jñānajanyaphalamiti | phalaṃ tu aniyama ityuktamuktiniyamasamarthanam‌ || 7 ||
|| oṃ kṛtsnabhāvāttu gṛhiṇopasaṃhāraḥ oṃ || 8 ||
|| oṃ maunavaditareṣāmapyupadeśāt‌ oṃ ||
pūrvatra devānāṃ saṃpūrṇaphalavattvamuktamatra tu saṃpūrṇavarṇāśramadharmavatvamucyata iti saṃgatiḥ | atrāśramabhedābhinneṣu jñānimanuṣyeṣu kiṃ muktau gṛhastha uttama uta yatiriti cintā | tadarthaṃ sarvakarmānuṣṭhānayogyatvameva muktāvuttamatve prayojakamuta cirakālasādhanānuṣṭhānameveti | tadarthaṃ muktau yaterādhikye "ācāryakulādvedamadhītya' ityādigṛhasthamokṣopasaṃhāraparavākyavirodho'styuta neti | tadarthaṃ tatkiṃ mānuṣaviṣayamuta devaviṣayamiti | tadarthaṃ
devaviṣayatvakalpakaṃ nāsti bādhakaṃ cāsti uta viparatītamita | pūrvapakṣastu | āśramāntarasthānāṃ sarvasatkarmasu yogyatābhāvāt‌ gṛhasthānāmeva tadyogyatvāttasyaivottamatve prayojakatvād‌gṛhastha evottamo na yatiḥ | anyathā chaṃdogopaniṣadyaṣyamādhyāyānte "ācāryakulādvedamadhītya' ityādinā gṛhasthasyaiva "brahmalokamabhisaṃpadyate na ca punarāvartate' iti  mokṣoktipūrvakamupasaṃhriyamāṇatvena gṛhasthasyaiva muktau anuttamatve tātparyāvagamāt‌ tadvirodhaḥ syāt‌ | nacedaṃ vākyaṃ devaparaṃ kalpakābhāvāt‌ | naca teṣāṃ kṛtsnagṛhasthadharmavattvena mukhyagṛhitvameva kalpakam‌ | tathātve atvarayā siddhimadyatibhyaḥ tvarayā siddhimad‌gṛhiṇo nīcā iti vādinaḥ siddhāntino mate mānuṣayatibhyo devānāṃ muktāvādhikyābhāvāpātāditi | siddhāntastu | gṛhasthasya sarvasatkarmādhikārasattve'pi tāvadācaraṇaniyamābhāvena tatsaṃpādyamokṣasya "tvarayaiva gṛhiṇassādhayanti muktimatvarayaiva yatayaḥ' iti  śrutyā ca acitakālīnasādhanasādhyatvāt‌ yatimokṣasya gṛhimokṣādapi cirakālasādhyatvenādhikyāt‌ yatireva muktāvadhikaḥ na gṛhī | na caivaṃ chaṃdogavākyavirodhaḥ | "kṛtsnā hyete gṛhiṇaḥ' ityādi  śrutau devānāmeva kṛtsnagṛhasthadharmavattvena mukhyagṛhitvoktyā chāṃdogyavākye'pi mukhyagṛhasthadevaparatvasya mukhyāmukhyanyāyaprāptatvāt‌ | nacaivaṃ mānuṣayatibhyo devānāṃ muktāvādhikyābhāvaprasaṃgo bādhakaḥ | "kṛtsnā hyete yatayaḥ' iti  śrutau kṛtsnayatidharmatvasyāpyuktatvāt‌ tathā "devā eva brahmacāriṇaḥ' iti  śrutau sarvāśramadharmavattvaśravaṇācca yatito'pyādhikyasiddheriti | phalaṃ tu jñānādhikāriṇāmādhikyoktyā jñānamahimasamarthanam‌ | ṭīkāyāṃ tu gṛhastha uttamo yatirveti saṃśayānupadarśanaṃ tu pūrvottarapakṣadiśā śiṣyairevohituṃ śakyamiti saṃdehāntaroktyabhiprāyo bhāvadīpe vyaktaḥ || 8 ||
|| oṃ anāviṣkurvannanvayāt‌ oṃ || 9 ||
jñānādhikāriṇāmādhikyaṃ cedanakeṣāṃ jñānādhikāriṇāṃ jñānasaṃpādane phalādhikyādupadeśo'pyāviṣkāreṇa syāditi śaṃkanātsaṃgatiḥ | aparokṣajñānasaṃgataparokṣajñānahetūpadeśaḥ kimāviṣkāreṇa kāryamuta neti cintā | tadarthamāviṣkāre sādhakamasti bādhakaṃ ca nāsti uta viparītamiti | pūrvapakṣastu | sabhācatvarādau sthitvopadeśe bahūnāṃ tattvajñānayogyānāṃ jñānodayasaṃbhavāt‌ tena ca phalādhikyena sādhakabhāvāt‌ bādhakābhāvācca āviṣkāreṇaivopadeśaḥ kārya iti | siddhāntastu | āviṣkāreṇopadeśe yogyānāmivāyogyānāṃ upadiṣyāthargrahaṇaprasaṃgāt‌ | ayogyopadeśasya ca "mānastenebhyaḥ' ityādi  śrutyā niṣiddhatvenānarthahetutvena yogyeṣveva jñānodayahetutvena phalātiśayahetutvena copapannatvādanāviṣkāreṇaivopadeśaḥ kārya iti | phalaṃ tu jñānātiśayasamarthanam‌ || 9 ||
|| oṃ aihikamaprastutapratibandhe taddarśanāt‌ oṃ || 10 ||
pūrvatra parokṣajñānasyātigopanākhyetthaṃbhāvoktirayuktā | tasya dṛṣyirūpaphalavyabhicāreṇa dṛṣyayahetutvāditi śaṃkānirāsātsaṃgatiḥ | atra pūrvatra prakṛtaparokṣajñānasādhyamaparokṣajñānaṃ kiṃ sādhanasaṃpūrtijanmanyeva bhavatīti niyamaḥ uta tatra janmāntarevetyaniyama iti cintā | tadarthaṃ tasminjanmanyanudayo tasya kadāpyanudaya evāpadyate atha neti | tadarthamaniyamakalpakaṃ nāstyutāstīti | pūrvapakṣastu | śravaṇādisādhanasaṃpūrtijanmani aparokṣajñānānudaye yāgādisādhanasaṃpūrtijanmanyeva svargasukhānudaye taddehāvacchedena tādṛśasukhabhogāyogarūpakāraṇasyeva iha prabalakāraṇābhāvāt‌ tasya tajjanmanyanudaye janmāntare tadudaya ityatra kalpakābhāvāt‌ kadāpyanudayasyaivāpātāt‌ tasminjanmanyevāparokṣajñānodaya iti | siddhāntastu | sādhanasaṃpūrtijanmani yāgaphalasvargānudaye taddehāvacchedena bhogāyogasyeva ihāpi taddehe jñānānudaye prārabdhakarmapratibandharūpakāraṇasya sattvāt‌ tajjanmanyanudaye janmāntare tadudayastadabhāve tajjanmanītyaniyama eva | naca kalpakābhāvaḥ "śrutvātmānaṃ matipūrvaṃ hyupāsya ihaiva dṛṣyiṃ paramasya vindet‌' iti śrutau tathāvagāmāditi | phalaṃ tu śravaṇāderjñānahetutvasamarthanam‌ || 10 ||
|| oṃ evaṃ muktiphalāniyamastadavasthāvadhṛtestadavasthāvadhṛteḥ oṃ || 11 ||
yathā pūrvatra jñānamaihikamevetyaniyamaḥ evaṃ mokṣaphalamapi taddehapātānantarameveti na niyama iti sādhanātsaṃgatiḥ | atra
prakṛtajñānasādhyo mokṣaḥ yaccharīre jñānamutpannaṃ taddehapātānantarameva bhavatīti niyama utāniyama ityevaṃ pūrvavadeva cintāparamparā pūrvapakṣasiddhāntāśca jñeyāḥ | viśeṣastu uttaratra jñānasādhanapūrtimajjanmani jñānodayo janmāntare vā tadudaya ityaniyame prārabdhapratibandharūpakāraṇavat‌ iha tādṛśakāraṇābhāve kalpakaṃ netyeva viśeṣaśaṃkāyāṃ "brahmasaṃstho'mṛtameti' iti brahmasaṃsthasya mokṣāvadhāraṇānyathānupapattiḥ "vidvānamṛtamā noti' iti śrutiścāniyame kāraṇībhūtapratibandhābhāvatadbhāvakalpaketi samādhānam‌ | phalaṃ tu aniyama ityuktasamarthanam‌ || 11 ||
|| iti śrīrāghavendrayativiracitāyāṃ nyāyamuktāvalyāṃ tṛtīyādhyāyasya caturthaḥ pādaḥ samāptaḥ ||
|| atha caturtho'dhyāyaḥ ||
|| pūrvatra "evaṃ muktiphalāniyamaḥ' iti phalapadena muktireva jñānasya pradhānaṃ phalaṃ anyattu ānuṣaṃgikamiti sūcitaṃ pradhānaṃ phalamatrādhyāye prapaṃcyate | tatra karmakṣayotkrāntimārgabhogabhedena catuvirdhasya mokṣasya kramabhāvitvāttatsarvaṃ kramāt‌ pādacatuṣyaye'pi vadiṣyan‌ ādau karmakṣayākhyaphalamāhādyapāde | tatra saptabhirnayaiḥ atyaṃtāvaśyaṃbhāvi sādhanaṃ vicāryate |
|| oṃ āvṛttirasakṛdupadeśāt‌ oṃ || 1 ||
|| oṃ liṃgācca oṃ ||
atraihikamityatra prakṛtaṃ śravaṇādikaṃ sakṛdanuṣṭheyamuta āvartanīyamiti cintā | tadarthaṃ śravaṇādividheragniṣyomādividhivat‌ sakṛdanuṣṭhāna eva paryavasānamuta avaghātavidhivadāvartanameveti | tadarthaṃ jñānaṃ hi śravaṇādeḥ sakṛdanuṣṭhānena bhavati uta neti | tadarthaṃ jñānasya suśakatvaṃ guṇaḥ uta duḥśakatvameva guṇaḥ iti | tadarthaṃ duḥśakatvasya guṇatvakalpakaṃ nāstyutāstīti | pūrvapakṣastu | śravaṇādisādhanaṃ agniṣyomādivatsakṛdevānuṣṭheyam‌ | tāvataiva jñānodayasaṃbhavena sakṛdanuṣṭhāna eva śravaṇādividheḥ paryavasānāt‌ | naca vrīhīnavahantītyādividheḥ sakṛdanuṣṭhānena tuṣavimokarūpaphalāsiddheḥ avaghātāvartane paryavasānavat‌ iha kiṃ na syāditi vācyam‌ | tathātve jñānasya sakṛtsādhanānuṣṭhānalabhyatvarūpasuśakatvaguṇābhāvaprasaṃgāt‌ | naca jñānasya duḥśakatvameva guṇaḥ kalpakābhāvāditi | siddhāntastu | avagādivacchravaṇādikamāvartanīyam‌ | avaghātasya sakṛdanuṣṭhānena tuṣavimokasyeveha śravaṇādeḥ sakṛdanuṣṭhānena jñānodayasyādarśanena tadvidheravaghātādividhivadāvartana eva paryavasānam‌ | naca jñānasya śravaṇādyāvartanasādhyatvopagame suśakatvaguṇabhaṃgaḥ | mahāphalahetujñāne duḥśakatvasyaiva guṇatvāt‌ | naca jñānasya duḥśakatvaṃ guṇa ityupetya śravaṇādyāvartanasādhyatvopagame kalpakābhāvāt‌ | uddālakena śvetaketuṃ prati "sa evo'ṇimā' ityāditattvasya navakṛtvaḥ chānyogye ṣaṣṭhe upadiṣyatvena tadanyathānupapatteḥ śravaṇāvṛttikalpakatvāt‌ | anyathā sakṛdevopadeśakaraṇāpātāt‌ | tathā taittirīye bhṛguvallayāṃ "tasmā etatprovāca annaṃ prāṇaḥ cakṣuḥ śrotraṃ mano vācam‌' iti śrutyā annādirūpaviṣaya eva bhṛgoḥ "adhīhi bhagavo brahma' iti punaḥ śravaṇamananadhyānāni kurviti bhāvena "tadvijijñāsasva' iti varuṇasyoktiḥ | śrutasya punarālocanenāpekṣitatvamityevaṃ rūpaliṃgadarśanasya ca "nityaśaḥ śravaṇaṃ ca' iti  smṛteḥ śravaṇamananadhyānāvṛttikalpakatvāditi | phalaṃ tu aihikamityādāvabhimatasya śravaṇāderjñānahetutvasamarthanam‌ || 1 ||
|| oṃ ātmeti tūpagacchanti grāhayanti ca oṃ || 2 ||
pūrvaṃ yajjñānārthaṃ śravaṇādyāvartyamityuktaṃ tasyātmatvasya svāmitvaśabditasyātra dhyānamāvaśyakamiti sādhanātsaṃgatiḥ | atra prakṛtātmatvopāsanopadeśau na nityaśaḥ kāryāvuta kāryau iti cintā | tadarthamātmatvarūpasvāmitvasyātiprasiddhatvaṃ tadupāsanādipratibandhakamuta neti | tadarthaṃ tasyāpratibandhakatvakalpakaṃ nāstyutāstīti | pūrvapakṣastu | ātmatvadhyānopadeśau na nityaśaḥ kāryau tasya svāmitvarūpasya nityaprasiddhatvāt‌ | suprasiddhasya nityaṃ sarvamanasi viparivartanena dhyānopadeśavaiyathyarāt‌
| aprasiddhatvasyaiva upadeṣyavyatvādau prayojakatvāt‌ suprasiddhasyāpi avaśyaṃ dhyānopadeśau kāryāvityatra kalpakābhāvāditi | siddhāntastu | "ātmetyupāsanaṃ kāryaṃ sarvathaiva mumukṣubhiḥ' ityādi  vacanenānvayavyatirekābhyāṃ viṣṇurātmetyupāsanopadeśayoḥ mokṣahetutvāvagamena mokṣārthopāsanādeḥ prasiddhayaprasiddhibhyāṃ kartavyatvādyasiddheḥ nityaṃ sarvathātmatvopāsanopadeśau kāryāveveti | phalaṃ tu vedānāṃ svāmitve mahātātparyasya yuktatvasamarthanam‌ || 2 ||
|| oṃ na pratīke na hi saḥ oṃ || 3 ||
pūrvoktasvāmitvopāsanākṣepeṇeha pūrvapakṣodayātsaṃgatiḥ | saptame "nāma brahmetyupāste' "vācaṃ brahmetyupāste' ityādirūpeṇa  śrutanāmavāgādiśabditoṣāsvāhādikaṃ brahmābhedenopāsyaṃ uta tatrasthatveneti cintā | tadarthaṃ nāmabrahmetyādiśrutiḥ aikyābhiprāyota nā nibrahmetyevaṃrūpeṇa tatrasthatvābhiprāyeti | tadarthaṃ mithyopāsanamapi pumarthaheturuta satyopāsanamiti | pūrvapakṣastu | nāmavāgādikaṃ brahmatayopāsyaṃ "nāma brahma' iti sāmānādhikaraṇaśrutyā tayoraikyopāsanasya śrutatvāt‌ | naca "pṛthagupadeśāt‌' ityatra  jīvabrahmabhedasya siddhatayā nāmabrahmaikyaṃ mithyaiveti cenna | pūvartra prasiddhayaprasiddhayoraprayojakatvena pumarthahetutvenaivātmatvopāsanavat‌ upāsyavastunaḥ satyamithyātvayoraprayojakatvena "yāvannā no gataṃ tatrāsya yathā kāmacāro bhavati' ityādivākyaśeṣaśrutaphalavattvena tathopāsanasambhavāditi | siddhāntastu | "acetanāsatyāyogyānyanupāsyānyaphalatvaviparyayābhyām‌' iti saṃkarṣaṇasūtreṇa anuvyākhyānoktavacanaiśca mithyopāsanāyāḥ aphalatvaviparītaphalatvayoruktyā mithyopāsteḥ pumarthāhetutayā tathopāsterakartavyatvāt‌ | "nāmādiprāṇaparyantam‌' ityādi smṛtibalācca nā ni brahmetyeva tatrasthatvenaivopāsanaṃ kāryaṃ na tvaikyeneti | phalaṃ tu prāguktātmopāsanasamarthanam‌ || 3 ||
|| oṃ brahmadṛṣyirutkarṣāt‌ oṃ || 4 ||
pūrvatra nāmabrahmetiprakṛtabrahmatvopāsteratrāvaśyakatvasādhanātsaṅgatiḥ | atra prakṛtaṃ brahmatvaṃ kiṃ sadā sarvathopāsyaṃ na bhavati uta bhavatīti cintā | tadarthaṃ tasya tathopāsyatve heturnāstyutāstīti | pūrvapakṣastu | na brahmatvaśabditasyopāsanaṃ kāryaṃ sadā sarvatheti | siddhāntastu | brahmatve savarguṇāntarbhāvena tasya bhagavadutkṛṣyatvarūpatvena itaraguṇebhyo jyāyastvena tadupāsterbhagavatprītiviśeṣahetutvena ātmatvopāsteriva etadupāsterabhāve mokṣāsiddheśca mokṣahetubhagavatprītiviśeṣajanakatvātsarvadā sarvathaiva brahmatvamupāsyam‌ | tacca ātmabrahmetyātmatvaviśeṣaṇatayaiveti na caturguṇopāstiniyamabhaṃga iti dhyeyam‌ | phalaṃ tu bhū no jyāyastvamityuktasamarthanamiti || 4 ||
|| oṃ ādityādimatayaścāṃga upapatteḥ oṃ || 5 ||
pūrvatra yadviśeṣaṇatayā brahmatvaṃ dhyeyamityuktaṃ tadātmatvameva aṃgāśritatvavanna dhyeyamiti śaṃkodayātsaṃgatiḥ | atra devānāṃ svasvotpattyaṃgāśritatvenopāsanaṃ devaiḥ kāryamiti yaduktaṃ "aṃgeṣu yathāśrayabhāvaḥ'  ityatra tatkiṃ devānāṃ sadā sarvathā na kāyumuta kāryamiti cintā | tadarthaṃ tathā kāryatve viśeṣaheturnāstyutāstīti | pūrvapakṣastu | guṇāntareṣu satsvapi viśiṣya svasvāśrayāṃgattvarūpaguṇasyaiva nityamupāsane brahmatvopāsana iva viśeṣahetvabhāvāt‌ sūryādidevānāṃ jagatprakāśanādyarthaṃ vividhisthāneṣu saṃcaraṇena nityaṃ bhagavadaṃgeṣvavasthityabhāvena tathopāstermithyopāstitayā saṃcaraṇena nityaṃ bhagavadaṃgeṣvavasthityabhāvena tathopāstermithyopāstitayā anatharphalatvācca na tathopāsanaṃ kāryamiti | siddhāntastu | "svotpattyagaṃ ca devānāṃ viṣṇościntyam‌' iti  smṛtyā devānāṃ muktau bhagavadaṃgapraveśasyāvaśyakatayā tasya ca sadā tādṛśaguṇopāsanena vinā siddhayabhāvāt‌ | bhagavadagapraveśarūpakāraṇasattvāt‌ nityaṃ rūpāntareṇa bhagavadaṃśeṣu sthitatvena mithyopāsanā'prasakteśca svāśrayacakṣuṣkatayā sūryeṇa, candrāśrayamanaskatayā candreṇa, brahmāśritamūrdhatayā brahmaṇā, ityevaṃrūpeṇa devaiḥ sadā sarvatra kāryamevopāsanamiti | phalaṃ tu ātmatvopāsanasamarthanameva satyapyāśrayāśrayibhāve svasvāmisambandhasyāpi anupāsyatvāpātāditi śaṃkānirāsāt‌ || 5 ||
|| oṃ āsīnaḥ saṃbhavāt‌ oṃ || 6 ||
|| oṃ dhyānācca oṃ || oṃ acalatvaṃ cāpekṣya oṃ || oṃ smaranti ca oṃ || oṃ yatraikāgratā tatrāviśeṣāt‌ oṃ ||
pūrvaṃ saṃmugdhatayoktopāsanasya dhyānasmaraṇabhedoktipūrvamitikartavyatākathanātsaṃgatiḥ | "upaviśyāsane yuṃjvāt‌' iti  ṣaṣṭhe gītāyāṃ dhyānāṃgatvena uktamāsanādikaṃ kiṃ nāvaśyakamutāvaśyakamiti cintā | tadarthaṃ āsanena vinopāsanopayuktaḥ harau manoniveśaḥ yukta uta na yukta iti | pūrvapakṣastu | bhagavaddṛṣyihetūpāsanasya harau manoniveśamātreṇopapatteḥ āsanaṃ nāvaśyakam‌ | na cāsanena vinā harau manoniveśa eva na yukta iti vācyam‌ | pratyāhārādināpi tatsaṃbhavāt‌ | anyathā "smartavyassatataṃ viṣṇuḥ' ityādividhīnāmanupapattiprasaṃgāt‌ | yadi cāsanādyupadeśānyathānupapattyā tadāvaśyakatve ca "parvatāgre nadī tīre' "brāhme muhūrte utthāya'  ityādyuktyanyathānupapattyā deśādiniyamopyāvaśyakaḥ syāditi | siddhāntastu | manaso vikṣepābhāvena harau niveśasya pratyāhārādinā saṃbhave'pi "acalaṃ ceccharīraṃ syānmanasaścāpyacālanam‌' iti  smṛtyā śarīrācalatvenāpi manaso vikṣepābhāvasyāvagamāt‌ | śarīrācalatvasya ca āsana eva saṃbhavāt‌ | "samaṃ kāyaśirogrīvam‌' ityādi  smṛtibalācca upāsanopayuktatayā samyagbhagavati manoniveśanārthaṃ āvaśyakamevāsanam‌ | naca nityopāsanasyāsanāvaśyakatvasya ca viruddhatvādāsanasyāvaśyakatve "smartavyassatatam‌' iti smṛtyā upāsanavidhivirodhaḥ | smaraṇadhyānarūpeṇopāsterdvaividhyena smaraṇopāsanasya nityaṃ kāryatvena tatrāsananiyame'pi kādācitkadhyānarūpeṇopāsane tadāvaśyakatve virodhābhāvāt‌ | ata eva nāsanādyupadeśavaiyarthyam‌ | nacaivaṃ deśaviśeṣāderapi āvaśyakatvaprasaṃgaḥ | āsanādivat‌ dhyānopayogitāyā vā śravaṇādivat‌ jñānopayogitāyā vā abhāvāt‌ | "tameva deśaṃ seveta' ityādi  smṛteśca yatraikāgratā dhyānasya sa eva deśādirgrāhyo na parvatāgrādiniyama iti | phalaṃ tu āsanādyupadeśasārthakatvam‌ || 6 ||
|| oṃ āprāyaṇāttatrāpi hi dṛṣyam‌ oṃ || 7 ||
pūrvatra viśeṣabhāvādāsanamāvaśyakaṃ deśaviśeṣādi tu viśeṣaṇābhāvānnāvaśyakamityuktam‌ | tadvadihāpi "dhyānācca' iti prakṛtaṃ dhyānaṃ jñānaparyantameva na tu tadanantaramapi muktiparyantaṃ muktau tatkṛtaviśeṣābhāvāditi śaṃkodayātsaṃgatiḥ | atra dhyānācceti prakṛtaṃ dhyānaṃ kiṃ jñānaparyantamevāvaśyakamuta muktiparyantamapīti cintā | tadarthaṃ jñānārthaṃ vihitasya muktipayarntamanuṣṭhāne heturnāstyutāstīti | tadarthaṃ muktāvānandātiśayo dhyānādanyenāpi bhavatyuta dhyānenaiveti | pūrvapakṣastu | muktāvānandavṛddheḥ dhyānādanyena śravaṇādisādhanāntareṇāpi saṃbhavāt‌ jñānārthaṃ vihitasya muktiparyantamanuṣṭhāne hetvabhāvānna muktiparyantaṃ dhyānamanuṣṭheyamiti | siddhāntastu | "yogarūḍhasya tasyaiva śamaḥ kāraṇamucyate' iti  gītāyāṃ yogārūḍhasyāparokṣajñāninaḥ śamaḥ dhyānaṃ paramasukhakāraṇamityuktyā prādhānyena dhyānasyānandavṛddhihetutvāvagamāt‌ | "prāyaṇāntamoṃkāramabhidhyāyīta' iti  śruteḥ | "muktā api hyenamupāsate' iti  śrutyuktakaimutyanyāyācca kāryameva muktiparyantaṃ dhyānamiti | phalaṃ tu yāvadadhikāramityādau  uktatāratamyasamarthanam‌ | anyathā muktau jñānināṃ dhyānaphalābhāvaprasaṃgena tāratamyābhāvāpātāditi || 7 ||
|| oṃ tadadhigama uttarapūvarāghayoraśleṣavināśau tadvayapadeśāt‌ oṃ || 8 ||
|| oṃ itarasyāpyevamasaṃśleṣaḥ pāte tu oṃ || oṃ anārabdhakārye eva tu pūrve tadavadheḥ oṃ || oṃ agnihotrādi tu tatkāryāyaiva taddarśanāt‌ oṃ || oṃ ato'nyadapītyekeṣāmubhayoḥ oṃ || oṃ yadeva vidyayeti hi oṃ || oṃ bhogena tvitare kṣapayitvā'tha sampatsyate oṃ ||
evaṃ prāsaṃgikaṃ samāpya karmakṣayākhyaṃ mokṣasvarūpamevātrocyata ityavasarasaṃgatiḥ | "evaṃ muktiphala' iti 
pūvaroktajñānānmuktirayukteti cintā | tadarthaṃ muktipratibandhakānāṃ jñānapūrvāparabhāvikarmaṇāṃ jñānānnāśādikamayuktamuta yuktamiti cintā | tadarthaṃ nāśādyaṅgīkṛtau bādhakamastyuta neti | pūrvapakṣastu | "evaṃ muktiphalāniyamaḥ' ityatra karmakṛtapratibandhābhāve jñānānmuktirbhavatyevetyuktamayuktam‌ | jñānātpūrvaṃ paścāccānuṣṭhitakarmaṇāṃ pratibandhakībhūtānāṃ nivṛttyayogāt‌ | tatkiṃ pūrvakarmaṇāṃ bhogena nivṛttiruta agninā tūlasyeva jñānena nāśaḥ uttareṣāmalepaḥ | ādye kiṃ tasminneva dehe teṣāṃ bhoga uta dehāntare | ante'pi kiṃ krameṇota ekasminneva janmani kāyavyūhena yugapadeva bhogaḥ | nādyaḥ | bahudehaphalakarmaṇāṃ bhāvāt‌ | na dvitīyaḥ | tathātve bahukālāntaritajñānasya jīrṇabījavanmokṣahetutvābhāvāpatteḥ | na tṛtīyaḥ | aniyatadeśakālavipākatvāt‌ tādṛśānāmapi yogaprabhāvenaikadeśakālayoreva bhoga ityaṅgīkāre ca yogasāmarthyādbhogābhāvaprasaṅgāt‌ | tadbhogakāleṣu punaḥ karmāntarasaṃbhavenāsamāpteśca | naca na karoti jñānī "na hi kaścitkṣaṇamapi jātu tiṣṭhatyakarmakṛt‌' iti  gītoktivirodhāt‌ | na caturthaḥ | "nābhuktaṃ kṣīyate karma kalpakoṭiśatairapi' iti smṛtivirodhāt‌ | jñānenaiva karmakṣaye jñānotpattyuttarakālameva mokṣāpātāt‌ | kiṃ ca jñāninaḥ prācīnapuṇyakarmaṇo'pi jñānena kṣaye "sarvāpekṣā ca yajñādiśruteḥ' ityatra  jñānopayogoktivirodhaḥ | uttarasyāśleṣe ca stuninayokta  ānandātiśayahetutvavirodhaḥ syāditi | anenaiva nyāyena "aniṣyādikāriṇām‌' iti vairāgyapādīya ekādaśādhikaraṇe haridviṣāṃ tamaḥprāptyuktirayuktā | dveṣapūrtitaḥ prāgūrdhvaṃ satāṃ karmaṇāṃ bhogena vā dveṣapūrtyā vā nāśasyāśleṣasya vā ayogāditi | siddhāntastu | bhogena karmaṇāṃ kṣayayoge'pi jñānodayānantaraṃ karmānuṣṭhānabhāve'pi jñānānmokṣo yukta eva | jñānena pūrvāparakarmanāśāśleṣābhyupagamānnoktadoṣaḥ | jñānena prācīnānāṃ aprārabdhāśeṣapāpakarmaṇāṃ puṇyamadhyepi aprārabdhānabhīṣyakāmyapuṇyakarmaṇāmeva nāśopagamena tadanyapuṇyapāpayorbhāvena "nābhuktam‌' itismṛtestadviṣayatayā tadvirodhasya jñānotpattyuttarakṣaṇa eva mokṣasya prācīnapuṇyajñānopayogoktyavirodhasya copapatteḥ | tathottarasyāpi pāpasyāśucitvādyanāpādakatvarūpāśleṣasya akāmyapuṇyasya ca mokṣe sukhātiśayahetutvasya copagamena stutinayoktavirodhasyābhāvāt‌ | prārabdhapuṇyapāpānāṃ aprārabdhakāmyapūrvottarābhīṣyapuṇyakarmaṇāṃ ca bhogena kṣapaṇasambhavācca | ato bādhakābhāvāt‌ jñānātkarmanāśādikaṃ yuktameveti jñānānmokṣo yukta eva | evaṃ dveṣiṇo'pi dveṣapūrtitaḥ prākkṛtāśeṣāprārabdhapuṇyānāṃ nāśasya udīcīnāṃ aśeṣapuṇyānāmaśleṣasya prārabdhapuṇyapāpānāṃ bhogena kṣapaṇasyopagamāt‌ dveṣiṇastamaḥprāptyuktirapyupapanneti | phalaṃ tu prāguktaprameyasamarthanaṃ vyaktam‌ || 8 ||
|| iti śrīmadrāghavendrayatikṛtāyāṃ nyāyamuktāvalyāṃ caturthādhyāyasya prathamaḥ pādaḥ ||
|| atha dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ ||
jñānino'taḥpadoktamokṣaphalāya jñānabhogābhyāṃ karmakṣaya uktaḥ pūrvapāde | atra tu kamarkṣayasādhyadehakṣayakrama ucyate iti pūrvānantaryamasya pādasya dhyeyam‌ |
|| oṃ vāṅmanasi darśanācchabdācca oṃ || 1 ||
|| oṃ ata eva ca sarvāṇyanu oṃ ||
|| oṃ jñānināṃ dehakṣayo dvividhaḥ | devānāṃ svottameṣu layo'nyeṣāṃ brahmanāḍyotkramaṇeneti | tatra devānāmadhikāryuttamatvādādau teṣāṃ "avibhāgo vacanāt‌'  ityataḥ prāktanasaptabhiradhikaraṇaiḥ dehalayobhidhīyate | tatra vāgdevyā bhagavatprāptilakṣaṇamokṣāya manodeve layo'śakyanirṇayo'tha śakyanirṇaya iti cintā | tadatharṃ manovedasya uttamatvaṃ nirṇetumaśakyamuta śakyamiti | tadarthaṃ "vākpūrvarūpaṃ mana uttararūpam‌' "manaḥ pūrvarūpaṃ vāguttararūpam‌'  iti vyāmiśroktiḥ vāṅmanasayoranyajanmoktyā samaprādhānyāvagamena manasa uttamatvanirṇayāya "viparyayeṇa tu kramo'ta upapadyate ca'  ityatra svasṛṣyatayā svottamadeva eva layasya vācyatvāt‌ vāco manolayaḥ aśakyanirṇaya iti | siddhāntastu | vāco manovaśyatvasya sarvapratyakṣasiddhatvāt‌ "manasā vāgre saṃkalpayatyatha vācā vyāharati'  iti vākpravṛtteḥ
manaḥpravṛttipūrvakatvayuktyupetatayā viśeṣaṇācca manasa uttamatvanirṇayasambhavāt‌ vāgdevyā manodeve layo'styeveti | naca vyāmiśroktivirodhaḥ | tasyāḥ saṃhitāyāḥ pūrvottaravarṇadevatātve guṇapradhānabhāve ca māṃḍūkeyatajjyeṣṭhaputrayorvivādaparatvāt‌ | vyaktametadaitareyabhāṣye | phalaṃ tu pūrvapakṣe vāgdevyāḥ mokṣābhāvenāniyamaḥ sarveṣāmityuktyākṣepaḥ | siddhānte tatsamādhiḥ | atra vāgdevī vāruṇī sauparṇī ca | manodevaḥ śeṣaḥ suparṇaśca jñeyāḥ | etena "agnau sarve devā vilīyante agnirindre' ityādiśrutibalātsarvadevānāmapi svasvottamadeveṣu layaḥ siddho bhavati | tatra vyāmiśroktyādirūpabādhakābhāvāditi | atra ṭīkāyāṃ manasi līyate na veti saṃdehavākye ityanirṇayo'tha līyata eveti nirṇaya iti śeṣo vā nobhayathāpi sambhavatīti pūrvapakṣapravṛttiriti vā tasyābhiprāyo dhyeyaḥ | tena na pūrvapakṣānānuguṇyamitiśaṃkā kāryā || 1 ||
|| oṃ tanmanaḥ prāṇa uttarāt‌ oṃ || 2 ||
atrādhikaraṇatraye vāṅmanasi līyata ityādiśrautakramātkramo dhyeyaḥ | atra manodevasya prāṇadevādutpattirnāstyutāstīti | pūrvapakṣastu | prāṇadevasya caturmukhasya sarvatra pravṛttihetutvamiva manodevasyāpi śeṣādeḥ sarvatra pravṛttikāraṇatvena tattadanubhavasiddhānvayavyatirekābhyāṃ siddhatayā ceṣyakatvasāmye manodevasya prāṇādevādanūnabalavattvānna manodevaḥ prāṇe līyate | ato nāsti mokṣaḥ | "viparyayeṇa' ityatroktadiśā  adhikabale eva nyūnabalo līyate iti vaktavyatvāt‌ | naca manasaḥ prāṇajatvāt‌ tatra layaḥ śaṃkyaḥ | "tatprāk‌śruteśca' iti  sūtre bhagavadutpattyokteriti | siddhāntastu | "vāṅmanasi sampadyante' ityuktyā "manaḥ prāṇe' iti manodevasya prāṇadeve saṃpattiśabditalayaśravaṇāt‌ manodevo'pi prāṇadeve caturmukhe vacanāntarabalādvāṇīdvārā līyata "mana udakramāt‌' ityādyaitareyaśrutau  brahmadehānmanodevasyotkramaṇapraveśayorapi taddehapātotthānayorabhāvasya prāṇadevotkramaṇapraveśayostadbhāvasya śravaṇena manodevādapi prāṇadevasyādhikyāvagamena ceṣyakatvasāmyābhāvādbhagavadutapannatvasya viyatpadīyanyāyena prāṇadevadvāropapattyā "vāyorvāva rudra udeti' iti  śrutyā ca manasaḥ prāṇajatvācca asti manaso mokṣa iti | phalaṃ tu pūrvavat‌ || 2 ||
|| oṃ so'dhyakṣo tadupagamādibhyaḥ oṃ || 3 ||
atra śrautakramādvā pūrvanayadvayaphalākṣepādvā saṃgatiḥ | atra prāṇapadokto viriṃcaḥ bhagavatprāptirūpamokṣāya harau na līyate'tha līyata iti cintā | tadarthaṃ tasyeśādhīnatvaniścayo nāstyutāstīti | tadarthaṃ prāṇasvātantyaśrutibāhulyaṃ anadyamānatve satyattṛtvamāhātmyaṃ ca niravakāśamuta sāvakāśamiti | pūrvapakṣastu | "na prāṇaḥ kiṃcidāśritaḥ' iti, "yasminnidaṃ sarvamadhyā not‌' iti, "prāṇasyaitadvaśe sarvaṃ tridive yatpratiṣṭhitam‌' iti, "vāyunā vai gautamasūtreṇa'  ityādiśrutibāhulyena prāṇasya ananyādhīnatvāvagamena īśādhīnatvaniścayānna tatra viriṃcasya layaḥ | naca "ananyeśvaratā prāṇe tadanyeśvaravarjanam‌' ityuktadiśā viṣṇvanyeśvaravarjanaparatvena śrutīnāṃ sāvakāśatvaṃ śaṃkyam‌ | bahutvaprayuktaprābalyopetānāṃ śrutīnāṃ ananyetyādidurbalavacanena gatyantarakalpanāyogāt‌ | anadyamāna iti prāṇasya layābhāvokteśceti | siddhāntastu | astyeva prāṇapadoktaviriṃcasya harau layaḥ | prāṇasyāsvātante śrutibāhulyasyeśādhīnatve'pi "prāṇaḥ parasyāṃ devatāyām‌' iti, "prāṇaḥ paramupagacchati' iti, "prāṇaḥ paramanuprāṇiti' ityādiśrutibāhulyasya sattvāt‌ | naca sāmyeneśādhīnatvānirṇayaḥ | svātantyaśrutīnāṃ "sarveprāṇamupagacchanti'iti, "prāṇaṃ devā anuprāṇanti' "ananyeśvaratā prāṇetadanyeśvaravarjanam‌' ityādi vacanabalena rudrādisarvopajīvyatvabhagavadanyeśvaravarjanaparatvena sāvakāśatvāt‌ | anadyamānatvasyeśādanyairanadyamānatvenopapatteriti | prāṇastejasītiśrutau tejasi śrītattve prāṇasya layaśravaṇaṃ tu "prāṇasya tejasi layo mārgamātram‌' iti smṛtyā tatprāptimātraparaṃ na tatra layaparam‌ | viriṃcasya sākṣātparamātmanilayayogyatvāt‌ prāṇasyeva tatra laya iti jñeyam‌ | "cakṣurādivattu tatsahaśiṣyayādibhyaḥ'  ityatra prāṇasyeśavaśatvoktāvapi iha svātante śrutibāhulyaviśeṣāśaṃketi na gatārthatā | phalaṃ tu pūrvapakṣe viriṃcasya layābhāve mokṣābhāvena tadanupraviṣyānāmaśeṣajñānināmapi mokṣābhāvena pūrvoktanayadvayākṣepadvārā aniyama ityuktākṣepātsiddhānte tatsamādhiriti || 3 ||
|| oṃ bhūteṣu tacchateḥ oṃ || 5 ||
pūrvoktākṣepeṇātra śaṃkanātsaṃgatiḥ | "vāṅmanasi' ityādiśrutau viśiṣyoktebhyo'nye devāḥ bhagavatprāptirūpamokṣāya kimekasminbhūtamāniti līyante'tha bhūtāntaramāniṣvapīti cintā | tadarthaṃ "agnau sarve devā vilīyante' iti śrutiḥ pratītārthanirṇāyakā bhavati na veti | tadarthaṃ bhūtāntaramāniṣu laye pramāṇaṃ nāsti prāguktanyāyaśca anirṇāyaka utāsti pramāṇaṃ prāguktanyāyaśca nirṇāyaka iti | pūrvapakṣastu | "agnau sarve devāḥ' iti śruteḥ agnideve eva sarve devā vilīyante'nyatrāpi līyanta ityatra pramāṇābhāvācca yo yanniyamaḥ sa tatra līyata iti prāguktanyāyasya niyāmakānāṃ bahutvenānirṇāyakatvāt‌ prāguktasvasvaniyāmake laya ityetadayuktamiti | siddhāntastu | "bhūteṣu devā vilīyante' iti bhāṣyoktaśrutyā paṃcasvapi bhūtamāniṣu yathā svasvaniyāmake devā vilīyante niyāmakānāṃ bahutve'pi "agnau sarve devā vilīyante'gnirindre indra umāyāṃ umā rudre' ityādiśrutyuktadiśā nirṇayasambhavena prāguktanyāyasyānirṇāyakatvābhāvāditi | phalaṃ tu prāguktākṣepasamādhī || 4 ||
|| oṃ naikasmindarśayato hi oṃ || 5 ||
