atrāpi pūrvākṣepeṇa śaṃkanātsaṃgatiḥ | pūrvaprakṛtadevāḥ bhagavatprāptilakṣaṇamokṣasya kiṃ agnāveva līyante'tha paṃcasvapīti cintā | "bhūteṣu devā vilīyante' iti prāguktaśrutiḥ sāvakāśātha niravakāśeti | tadarthaṃ "agnau sarve devāḥ' iti śrutyanyathānupapattiḥ niravakāśota sāvakāśā | tadarthaṃ paṃcasvapītyatra viśeṣapramāṇābhāvena agnāvitiśrutyanyatānupapatteḥ gatyantarakalpakābhāvāt‌ niravakāśatayā bhūteṣviti bahuvacanāntaśruteḥ agnau devānāṃ laye'pi agniḥ svajanakavāyau, vāyośca svajanakākāśe, layena paraṃparālayābhiprāyeṇa sāvakāśatvakalpanādagnāveva sarvadevānāṃ laya iti paṃcasvapi laya iti prāguktamayuktamiti | siddhāntastu | "pṛthivyāṃ ṛbhavo vilīyante' iti, "ṛbhavaḥ pṛthivyām‌' iti ca, bhāṣyoktaviśeṣapramāṇābhyāṃ pratyekaṃ paṃcasvapi bhūtamāniṣu ṛbhuprabhṛtidevānāṃ layaśravaṇādagnau sarve devā iti śruteḥ "agnāvagnayo vāyāvindraḥ' ityādi śrutau nirdiṣyāgniputrāṃ prāyeṇādhamānāṃ sarveṣāṃ bhūtāntarāpraveśināṃ layaviṣayatvena sāvakāśatayā bhūteṣvitiśruteḥ paraṃparayā layābhiprāyarūpāmukhyārthatākalpakatvāyogāt‌ niravakāśabhūteṣu iti prāguktaśrutyā paṃcasvapi bhūtamāniṣu devānāṃ prāguktapaṃcasvapi laya ityetadyuktamiti | phalaṃ vyaktam‌ || 5 ||
|| oṃ samanā cāsṛtyupakramādamṛtatvaṃ cānupoṣya oṃ || 6 ||
|| oṃ tadapīteḥ saṃsāravyapadeśāt‌ oṃ || oṃ sūkṣmaṃ pramāṇataśca tayopalabdheḥ oṃ || oṃ nopamardenātaḥ oṃ || oṃ asyaiva copapatterūṣmā oṃ || oṃ pratiṣedhāditi cenna śārīrāt‌ oṃ || oṃ spaṣyo hyekeṣām‌ oṃ || oṃ smaryate ca oṃ ||
tadadhīnatvayuktiviśeṣaśrutibhyāṃ prāguktasiddhāntanyāyenātra śaṃkanādāpavādakī saṃgatiḥ | prāṇāduttamatvena buddhisthatayā prakṛtā, śrautakrameṇa vā prakṛtā, citprakṛtiḥ bhagavati līyate na veti cintā | tadarthaṃ prāguktayuktiḥ, "tejaḥ parasyām‌' itiviśeṣaśrutiśceti dvayamapi niravakāśamuta sāvakāśamiti | tadarthaṃ kāmādityatroktaṃ  asaṃsāritvaṃ prakṛterayuktamuta yuktamiti | tadarthaṃ muktatvaṃ tasyāḥ kimāgantukamuta svābhāvikamiti | svābhāvikatve bhagavatā sarvasāmyamāpatatyuta neti | pūrvapakṣastu | bhagavadadhīnatvayukti "teja' iti śrutibhyāṃ sāpi harau līyata eva | anyathā vāgādīnāṃ manaḥprabhṛtiṣu tābhyāmuktalayo'pi na syāt‌ | naca asaṃsāritvāllayābhāvaḥ tasyaivānupapatteḥ | naca muktatvāttadyuktaṃ tasyāgantukatvāt‌ | svābhāvikatve bhagavatā tasyāḥ sarvasāmyāpātāt‌ | nityaṃ saṃsārarāhitye guṇāntaraiḥ sāmyasyāpyāpātāditi | siddhāntastu | "dvau vāva sṛtyanupakramau prakṛtiśca paramaśca' iti bhāṣyokta  śrutau paramapuruṣatvasarvagatatvanityatvayuktibhyāṃ kadāpi saṃsārābhāvasya taddhetukanityamuktvasyoktyā bhagavadupāsanādisādhanāsādhyatayā muktatvasya svābhāvikatvāt‌ tādṛśamuktimatyāśca devyāḥ hareriva dehatyāgarūpalayāyogāt‌ na prakṛtirlīyate | tadadhīnatvādvāgādivatsvottame harau līyata ityabhipretānumānaṃ tu saṃsāritvopādhigrastāt‌ | "tejaḥ' iti śrutirapi
"hareratyantasāmīpyaṃ layo lakṣmāḥ prakīrtitaḥ' iti vacanādatisāmīpyārthatayopapannā | prakṛterīśena sarvasāmyāpātasya ca "tadapīteḥ' "sūkṣmam‌' "asyaiva ca' iti sūtratrayoktaiḥ mocakatvāmocakatva, paramasūkṣmatvasūkṣmatva, jñānānandādiguṇaparimāṇādhikya, nyūnatva, ūṣmāvattvānūṣmāvattvahetubhirnirāsāt‌ | sarvasāmyābhāve brahmāderiva nityamuktatvādināpi sāmyaṃ na syāditi codyasya ca "nopamardena' iti sūtre bhagavadviśeṣaguṇānupamardenaiva deśakālavyāptyādikiṃcitsāmyasyaiva pramāṇasiddhatvoktyā nirāsānna "asamo vā eṣa paraḥ' iti śrutau hareḥ sāmyaniṣedhasya "pratiṣedhāt‌' ityādisūtratrayoktadiśā jīvaviṣayatvāditi | phalaṃ tu pūrvapakṣe devyāḥ saṃsāritvāt‌ bhagavati layasadbhāvādadhikāritvena adhikāriṇāṃ "iyadāmananāt‌' ityatroktapramāṇāvadhitvākṣepaḥ siddhānte tatsamādhānam‌ || 6 ||
|| oṃ tāni pare tathā hyāha oṃ || 7 ||
pūrvatra "vilīnaḥ parame hyamṛtatvameti' ityuktau astu amṛtatvaṃ "so'dhyakṣe'  ityuktasya prāṇasya, na "bhūteṣu devā vilīyante' ityuktadevānāmiti śaṃkanātsaṃgatiḥ | "bhūteṣu devā vilīyante' ityuktadevāḥ bhagavati na līyante'tha līyanta iti cintā | tadarthaṃ kimete prāṇapadoktacaturmukhapratibiṃbāḥ uta bhagavatpratibiṃbāḥ apīti | tadadhīnatve sati tatsadṛśatvarūpapratibiṃbo hi svaniyāmake biṃbe līyate | "pratibiṃbo hareḥ prāṇaḥ prāṇasyānye kalāḥ kramāt‌' iti vacanena prāṇasyaikasyaiva bhagavatpratibiṃbatvāt‌ tasyaikasyaiva tatra praveśarūpamokṣo nānyeṣāṃ jñānināmityaniyamaḥ sarveṣāmityuktamayuktamiti prāpte kramātprāṇabiṃbatve'pi devānāṃ "sarve devāḥ prāṇamāviśya deve muktā layaṃ parame yānti' iti  śrutyā prāṇadvārā paramātmani praveśopapatteḥ | bhagavatpratibiṃbaviriṃcapraviṣyānāṃ, viriṃcasyeśvarapraveśe, paramātmapraveśasyāparihāryatvāt‌ | "ābhāsa eva ca' iti  sūtroktadiśā sāmānyato'śeṣajīvānāṃ uktarūpapratibiṃbatvācca asti tatra praveśarūpamokṣaḥ iti aniyama ityuktaṃ yuktamiti | phalaṃ tu vyaktam‌ || 7 ||
|| oṃ avibhāgo vacanāt‌ oṃ || 8 ||
pūrvākṣepeṇātra śaṃkanātsaṃgatiḥ | pūrvādhikaraṇe mokṣavattvenoktāḥ devāḥ kiṃ muktau svatantrāḥ uteśādhīnā iti cintā | tadarthamanyādhīnatve saṃsāraduḥkhādiprasaṃgaḥ śrautasatyakāmatvādyabhāvaprasaṃgaśca astyuta neti | muktānāṃ parādhīnatve saṃsāraduḥkhādiprasaṃgāt‌ "ete devā etamātmānamanuviśya satyāḥ satyakāmāḥ' ityādi  śrutyuktasatyakāmatvādyayogācca na muktāḥ parādhīnā iti prāguktamīśādhīnatvādirūpaṃ prati biṃbatvamayuktamiti prāpte "kāmena me kāma āgamāt‌' iti  "muktānāṃ satyakāmatvam‌' iti  śrutismṛtibhyāṃ śrautasatyakāmatvāderīśecchānusāritvādeva duḥkhādiprasakterapyabhāvātkāmitālābhaprayuktatvāt‌ duḥkhādeḥ bhagavadicchānusāreṇa kāmayamānānāṃ muktānāṃ kāmitālābhābhāvādīśādhīnā eva muktāvapi jñānina iti prāguktaṃ yuktamiti siddhāntaḥ || 8 ||
|| oṃ tadokograjvalanaṃ tatprakāśitadvāro vidyāsāmarthyāt‌ taccheṣagatyanusmṛtiyogācca hārdānugṛhītaḥ śatādhikayā oṃ || 9 ||
|| oṃ raśmyanusārī oṃ || oṃ niśi neti cenna sambandhāt‌ oṃ || oṃ yāvaddehabhāvitvāddarśayati oṃ || oṃ ataścāyane'pi hi dakṣiṇe oṃ ||
pūrvatrādhikāryuttamakrameṇādau devānāṃ svasvottameṣu dehalayānmokṣaḥ sāpavādaḥ saptabhiḥ nayairuktaḥ | atra tu naye manuṣyāṇāmutkramaṇena bhagavatprāptirūpamokṣa ucyata iti pūrvapeṭikayāsya saṃgatirdhyeyā | atra jñānināṃ dehādutkrāntiḥ ajñamṛtivadeva atha jñānaśaktyā tato vilakṣaṇeti cintā | tadarthaṃ jñānaṃ kiṃ tanmṛtervilakṣaṇatāsaṃpādanāśaktamuta śaktamiti | tadarthaṃ prārabdhakarmaśeṣo jñānaśaktipratibandhako bhavatyuta neti | tadarthaṃ kiṃ jñānaṃ svodayātphaladānasamarthaṃ kiṃ vā karmāvasāna eveti | tadarthaṃ svodayātphaladānaśaktatve'tiprasaṃgo'styuta neti | pūrvapakṣastu | svodayamārabhyaiva jñānasyājñamṛtervailakṣaṇyarūpaphaladānaśaktatve
jñasyādimṛterapi ajñamṛtito vailakṣaṇyasaṃpādakatvāpātena prārabdhāvasāna eva phaladānaśaktaṃ na tu tasminsatīti vācyatvāt‌ "ādeham‌' iti  vacanena dehapātaparyantavidyamānaprārabdhapratibaddhaśaktikaṃ jñānaṃ nājñamṛtito jñānimṛtervailakṣaṇyasaṃpādakam‌ | naca mṛtikāle prārabdhaṃ neti yuktam‌ ajñamṛteriva jñamṛterapi prārabdhanibandhanatvāt‌ | "svodayātphaladaṃ jñānam‌' iti tu  phalāntaraparaṃ neyamiti | siddhāntastu | syādevaṃ yati jñānamātreṇa vailakṣaṇyasaṃpādakaṃ jñānimṛterityaṃgīkurmaḥ tadā syādevātiprasaṃgaḥ | nacaivaṃ kiṃ tu svahṛdayamārabhya bhagavatprāptiparyantaṃ vidyamānamārgasmṛtisahitaṃ vidyāprasannahṛdgatabhagavadanugrahasahitameva | tathā tad‌vyayaṃca karmāvasāna eva bhavati nānyadeti nātiprasaṃgo'taḥ tadubhayasahakṛtaṃ jñānamajñānimṛtito jñānimṛtervailakṣaṇyasaṃpādakaṃ bhavatyeveti jñānapādoktajñānasāmarthyaṃ yuktameveti phalaṃ dhyeyam‌ | vailakṣaṇyaprakāraśca hṛdgatabhagavatprakāśena prakāśitadvārayā brahmanāḍyā yāvaddehabhāvisūryaraśmibhirantarvyāptayā jñānino nirgamanaṃ nājñasyeti tatprakāśitadvāra ityādi sūtrabhāṣyādyuktadiśā jñeyaḥ | atra yadyapi jñāninaḥ astyutkramaṇamiti sāmānyataḥ siddhau kimajñamṛtito vilakṣaṇamuta neti viśeṣacintā yuktā | tathā'pi viyatpāde janmoktyaiva layaṃ siddhavatkṛtya sṛṣyikrameṇota vaiparītyeneticintāvadihāpi devānāmanyonyapraveśena dehalayoktau tadanyeṣāṃ dehādutkramaṇamiti labdhameveti siddhavatkṛtyeyaṃ viśeṣacinteti na doṣaḥ || 9 ||
|| oṃ yoginaḥ prati smaryete smārte cete oṃ || 10 ||
pūrvākṣepeṇa śaṃkanātsaṃgatiḥ | pūrvatra pradhānāṃgabhāvena prakṛtaṃ vidyā gatismaraṇaṃ cetyetadubhayaṃ gatyarthaṃ nāvaśyakamutāvaśyakamiti cintā | tadarthaṃ "agnirjyotirahaḥ śuklaḥ' ityādi  smṛtiḥ lokānusaraṇasahāyā satī gateḥ kālamṛtyadhīnatvaparā | "yaṃ yaṃ vāpi' iti  smṛtiḥ liṃgadarśanaṃ ca lokānusārādanyathā neyamuta "agniḥ' ityādismṛtiḥ vidyādyadhīnatvaparā yaṃ yaṃ iti vacanamanyathā neyamiti | pūrvapakṣastu | "agnirjyotirahaḥśuklaḥ' iti smṛtau "dhūmo rātriḥ' iti  smṛtau ca brahmaprāpticandraprāptigatyoḥ kramādaharādirātyādikālamṛtyadhīnatāvagamālloke phalānusmṛtiṃ vināpi phalaprāptidarśanācca na gatiprāptyarthaṃ gatismṛtiḥ vidyā cāpekṣitā kiṃtvaharādikālasmṛtireva | naca "yaṃ yaṃ vāpi smaran‌' iti smṛtau smṛtiviṣayasyaiva prāpyatvoktestadvirodhitayā "ācāryastu te gatiṃ vaktā' iti  śrutyarthagatyuktirūpaliṃgavirodhaścaivaṃ sati prā notīti śaṃkyam‌ | dehatyāgakṣaṇe duḥkhena smaraṇācchāstrādinā pūrvaṃ jānanniti jñānamātrāthartvena smarannityasya neyatvāt‌ | etena liṃgadarśanamapi pratyuktaṃ jñānārthatayā tadupadeśopapatteḥ | anyathā lokānusāravirodhāditi | siddhāntastu | ādyasmṛtau "brahmavidaḥ' iti "dhūmaḥ' iti smṛtau yogīti ca viśeṣaṇācca tābhyāṃ jñānakarmarūpopāyasādhyatvapratītyā kālamṛtyadhīnatvāpratīteḥ vidyādikamavaśyamapekṣitameva | anyathā smṛtyostadviśeṣaṇavaiyarthyāpātāt‌ | aharāderabhimāninayanyāyena devatātvāt‌ kālatvābhāvācca "yaṃ yaṃ vāpi' iti smṛtau tyāgakṣaṇe smṛtyayogaḥ | mahātmanāṃ dehe sadā heyatvabuddhibhāvena tadā duḥkhābhāvāt‌ | pāmarāṇāmapi kāla eva duḥkhabhāvena tatkṣaṇe duḥkhābhāvācca | naca lokānanusāritvadoṣaḥ | loke phalaprāptau karṣaṇādikriyānuvṛtterdarśanenehāpi smṛtirūpakriyāvṛttyaṃgīkārāt‌ | loke kriyā karṣaṇādirūpā | atra tu mānasīti avāntaravailakṣaṇyamātram‌ | na tāvatā tadananusāritā śaṃkanīyā atiprasaṃgāt‌ | etena liṃgadarśanamapi nānyathā neyamiti | phalaṃ tu jñānasya caturvidhamokṣahetutvākṣepasamādhī || 10 ||
|| iti śrīrāghavendrayatikṛtāyāṃ nyāyamuktāvalyāṃ caturthādhyāyasya dvitīyaḥ pādaḥ ||
|| atha tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ ||
pūrvatra brahmanāḍyotkrāntānāṃ brahmaprāptau yadgatismaraṇaṃ vidyāṃtayoktaṃ sā gatistadgamyaṃ ca atrocyata iti pūrvapādānaṃtaryasya dhyeyam‌ | naca gatigamyayoḥ dvayorapyuktau pādabhedaprasaṃgaḥ śaṃkyaḥ | caramagamyena sahitasyaiva mārgatvena mārgarūpaikārthatve'pi mārgaikadeśacaramagamyasya pṛthagvivakṣayā gamyaṃ cocyata ityukteḥ |
|| oṃ arcirādinā tatprathiteḥ oṃ || 1 ||
pūrvatra yadgatismaraṇamāvaśyakamityuktaṃ sā gatiḥ kiṃ arcirvāyūbhayaprāthamikā acirḥprāthamikā veti cintā | tadarthaṃ śrutidvayaṃ niravakāśamuta sāvakāśamiti | tadarthamubhayaprāthamyaṃ gateraviruddhamuta viruddhamiti | tadarthaṃ sā kiṃ dvirūpā utaikarūpeti gateradhikāribhedenākṣiptayordvayorapi prāthamyavirodhena niravakāśota sāvakāśeti | "te arciṣāmabhisaṃbhavanti'  "sa vāyumāgacchati' iti  śrutidvayena arcirvāyūbhayaprāthamikā gatiriti prāpte siddhāntaḥ | "dvāveva mārgau prathitāvarcirādivipaścitām‌ | dhūmādiḥ karmiṇām‌' iti smṛtāveka eva māgarḥ | sa cārcirādika eva na tu vāyvādika iti prathitapadena prasiddhatvoktyā "te'rciṣamiti śruteḥ prābalyāttadanurodhena arcirnāmikadevatāprāthamikā ekaiva brahmagatirnānekā | sa vāyu mā gacchatītiśrutistu prabalaśrutyanurodhena arciranantaraprāptyarthatayopapannā | śiṣyaṃ ṭīkāyām‌ | phalaṃ tu adhikāribhedena mārgadvayasya vyavasthitatve'pi ahamatrādhikārītyanirṇayādgatismṛtyanupapattistatsamādhiśceti || 1 ||
|| oṃ vāyuśabdādaviṣaviśeṣābhyām‌ oṃ || 2 ||
pūrvatra brahmaprāptimārge prathamaprāpyadevatādinirṇayaḥ kṛtaḥ | atra dvitīyaprāpyadevatāniṇaryaḥ kriyata iti saṃgatiḥ | pūrvavaiṣamyeṇa śaṃkanādvā saṃgatiḥ | pūrvaprakṛtārcirādimārge vāyudevatāhardevatetyubhayaṃ dvitīyaprāpyamuta vāyudaivatameveti cintā | tadarthaṃ "sa vāyum‌' iti  "arciṣo'hariḥ' iti  śrutidvayaṃ dvayordvitīyaprāpyatvaprāpakaṃ bhavatyuta neti | tadarthaṃ kiṃ dvau mārgāvutaika eveti | pūrvapakṣastu | pūrvatra dvāveva mārgāvitiviśeṣavacanena arciṣa eva prāthamyāvagame'pi iha vāyoreva dvitīyaprāpyatve viśeṣavacanābhāvāt‌ "arciṣo'haḥ' iti "sa vāyum‌' iti śrutidvayasyāpi prāmāṇyāt‌ mārgabhedena dvayorapi dvitīyaprāpyatve virodhābhāvācchatidvayabalāt‌ aharvāyū dvāvapi dvitīyaprāpyāviti | siddhāntastu | "sa vāyumāgacchati' iti sāmānyavacanena vāyośca prāptaprāthamyasya "dvāveva mārgau prathitau' ityuktaprasiddhivirodhena tyāge'pi dvitīyaprāpyatvasya nyāyaprāpyatvāt‌ "sa ito gato dvitīyāṃ gatiṃ vāyumāgacchati' iti viśeṣaśruteśca vāyureva dvitīyaprāpyaḥ ahaḥ tadanantaraprāpyamiti | mārgadvayena dvayordvitīyaprāpyatvaṃ tu dvāvevetivacanoktaprasiddhivirodhādeva bādhitamiti | phalaṃ tu pūrvatreveti || 2 ||
|| oṃ taṭito'dhi varuṇaḥ sambandhāt‌ oṃ || 3 ||
"te'rciṣamabhisambhavanti arciṣo'rahna āpūryamāṇapakṣamāpūryamāṇapakṣādyān‌ ṣaḍudaṅ‌ eti māsāṃstān‌ | māsebhyaḥ saṃvatsaram‌' ityādi chandogaśrutyādyukta  brahmaprāptimāge yatra vivādaḥ tadādyaṃ dvitīyaṃ ca prāpyaṃ nirṇītam‌ | anyatra saṃvatsaraparyantaṃ kramaprāpyeṣu vivādābhāvānnirṇaya eva | saṃvatsarādanantaraṃ prajāpatilokaṃ gantuṃ kiṃ taṭidvaruṇau dvāvapi mārgabhedena prāpyāvuta mārgaikyena taṭillokaṃ prāpya veti cintā | tadarthaṃ "saṃvatsarādvaruṇalokam‌' iti "saṃvatsarāttaṭitam‌' iti ca śrutidvayaṃ tadubhayaprāptiprāpakaṃ na veti | netyatra pramāṇaṃ nāstyutāstīti | pūrvatra dvitīyāṃ gatiṃ vāyumiti viśeṣavacanena vāyordvitīyaprāpyatvanirṇayepīha tādṛśapramāṇābhāvāt‌ śrutidvayaprāmāṇyādadhikāribhedādhīnamārgabhedena dvayorapi prāpyvopapatteḥ dvāvapi lokau saṃvatsaramānilokānantaraṃ prāpyāviti saṃgatipūrvapakṣau | siddhāntastu | "taṭito varuṇaṃ gacchati taṭitā hyūhyate varuṇalokaḥ' iti viśeṣaśrutyā taṭidanantarameva sayuktikaṃ varuṇalokasya prāpyatvokteḥ mārgadvayasya ca dvāvevotyatroktabrahmaprāptimārgaikyaprasiddhivirodhayuktatayā dvayorekasminmārgai yugapatprāpyatvavirodhena śrutidvayasya dvayoḥ paurvāparyeṇaiva prāptiprāpakatvārthakatvasyāvaśyaṃbhāvāt‌ pūrvaṃ taṭillokaṃ prāpya paścādvaruṇalokaṃ prā notīti | phalaṃ tu mārgānirṇayāttadanusmṛtyanupapattitatsamādhī || 3 ||
|| oṃ ativāhikastalliṃgāt‌ oṃ || 4 ||
|| oṃ ubhayavyāmohāttatsiddheḥ oṃ ||
pūrvatra bhinnavākyagatabhinnapadārthayostaṭidvaruṇayoḥ paurvāparyoktau atraikavāyuśabdārthayerātivāhikamukhyayoḥ paurvāparyamucyata iti saṃgatiḥ | dvitīyāṃ gatiṃ vāyumāgacchatītiśrutau vāyuḥ kimātivāhiko'tha mukhyavāyuriti cintā | tadarthaṃ tasya ātivāhikatve mukhyasya prāpyatvaṃ na prā notyuta prā notīti | tadarthaṃ "dyau rvāva vidyuttatpatiṃ vāyumupagamya tenaiva brahma gacchati' itiśrutavāyuśabde mukhyo vā''tivāhiko veti sandehena prāktane'pi sandehādinirṇayaḥ atha mukhya iti nirṇaya iti | pūrvatra taṭito varuṇaṃ gacchatīti sayuktikavacanabalena paurvāparyanirṇaye'pi iha pūrvaḥ ātivāhikaḥ caramo mukhyaḥ iti vā pūrvo mukhyaścarama ātivāhika iti vā sandehe nirṇayābhāvāt‌ pūrvasyātivāhikatvajñāpakābhāvāt‌ somāgnīndradhruvānāmapi mārge kramātprāpyatvoktyā tāratamyakramasyātrāvivakṣitatvena mukhyasyāpi dvitīyaprāpyatvasambhavāt‌ mukhyārthatvācca mukhyavāyureva dvitīyaprāpya vāyuśabdārtho nātivāhikaḥ | tathātve mukhyasya prāpyatvābhāvaprasaṃgāditi pūrvavaiṣamyeṇāpi śaṃkanātsaṃgatiśca | siddhāntastu | pūrvaprāpyatva, vidyucchabditavāṇīpatitvarūpaliṃgābhyāṃ pūrvaṃ ātivāhikaścaramo mukhya iti nirṇayasambhavāt‌ | mukhyārthasyāpi pūrvaprāpyatvaliṃgena "utkrāntastu śarīrātsvāt‌' ityādi bhāṣyokta  vacanena ca tyāgasambhavāt‌ pūrvaprāpya ātivāhika eveti | phalaṃ tu "iyadāmananāt‌'  ityatrokataprāṇādhikāryuttamatvasyāyogastatsamādhiśceti || 4 ||
|| oṃ vaidyutenaiva tatastacchateḥ oṃ || 5 ||
pūrvavaiṣamyeṇa śaṃkanātsaṃgatiḥ | prakṛtabrahmaprāptimārge mukhyavāyoranantaraṃ, brahmaṇo'rvāk‌ prāpyo'sti na veti cintā | tadarthaṃ mukhyabrahmaṇorantarāle prāpyāṃgīkāre bādhakaṃ nāstyutāstīti | pūrvatra divaspatitvabādhakena caramavāyorātivāhikatvāyoge'pi iha mukhyabrahmāntarāle kasyacitprāpyasyāṃgīkāre bādhakābhāvānmukhyavāyuḥ svasmādantaramapi brahmetaraprāyāntarayuktaḥ adhikāritvātprācīnādhikārivadityanumānācca | naca iyadāmananādityatroktādhikāyaruttamatvena bādhakaṃ tadbādhitaṃ cānumānamiti śaṃkyam‌ | "somaṃ vaiśvānaraṃ cendraṃ dhruvam‌' iti pūrvatra bhāṣyokta smṛtāvindrādavarasya dhruvasyendrānantaraṃ prāptyuktyottamatvakramānurodhena prāptikramābhāvāt‌ vāyoranantaramapi tadanyaḥ kaścitprāpyo'stviti prāpte siddhāntaḥ | adhikāryuttamatvasya prāpyāntarābhyupagamābādhakatve'pi "tatpuruṣo mānavaḥ' "sa etān‌ brahma gamayati'  "vidyutpatirvāyureva nayedbrahma na cāparaḥ'  ityādiśrutismṛtyavagatabrahmaprāpakatvaṃ vāyoḥ śrutaṃ antarāle prāpyāntarakalpanaṃ bādhata iti sa evāntimaprāpyaḥ | anyasyāntimaprāpyatve tasyaiva brahmaprāpakatvāpatteḥ rājādau tathā darśanāditi | phalaṃ tu vāyoratiśayena prādhānyāyogaḥ tatsamādhiśceti || 5 ||
|| oṃ kāryaṃ bādarirasya gatyupapatteḥ oṃ || 6 ||
|| oṃ kāryātyaye tadadhyakṣeṇa sahātaḥ paramabhidhānāt‌ oṃ || oṃ smṛteśca oṃ || oṃ paraṃ jaiminirmukhyatvāt‌ oṃ || oṃ darśanācca oṃ || oṃ na ca kārye patipattyabhisandhiḥ oṃ || oṃ apratīkālaṃbanānnayatīti bādarāyaṇa ubhayathā ca doṣāttatkratuśca oṃ || oṃ viśeṣaṃ ca darśayati oṃ ||
brahmaprāptimārge caramaprāptinirūpaṇādvā | "sa enān‌ brahma' iti pūrvatrodāhṛtaśrutigatabrahmapadārthanirūpaṇādvā pūrvasaṃgatiḥ | atra sa enān‌ brahmeti śrutaṃ brahma kiṃ caturmukha evātha brahmaiva utobhayamapīti cintā | tadarthamubhayamiti pakṣe ekaikapakṣoktasādhakabādhake samīkartuṃ na śakyete kiṃ vā śakyete iti | tathobhayaprāptipakṣe yugapadubhayaprāpterayogāttatra krame pramāṇaṃ nāstyutāstīti | pūrvapakṣastu | caturmukha evedaṃ śrutyuktaṃ brahma | yadiha vāva paramabhipaśyati prā noti prā noti brahmāṇaṃ caturmukham‌' iti  śrutyā "brahmaṇā saha te sarve samprāpte pratisaṃcare | parasyānte parātmānaḥ praviśanti paraṃ padam‌ ||' iti smṛtyā "tathā'pi prakṛterbandho brahmaṇā saha bhidyate' ityādinā caturmukhamuktipayarntaṃ bandhasya santatatvena tatorvāk‌ caturmukhasyaiva śravaṇādisādhanairaparokṣīkṛtatvāttasyaiva prāpteryuktatvādityupapattyā ca caturmukhaprāpterevāvagamena bādhakena mukhyārthasyāpi parabrahmaṇastyāgena amukhyārthaviriṃcagrahaṇopapatteḥ | naca "brahmavidā noti param‌' iti  parabrahmaprāptiśrutivirodhaḥ | tasya
sāmānyavākyasya prāguktaviśeṣaśrutyādinā caturmukhaprāptyanantaraṃ acireṇa pralayakāle tena saha paramā notīti sāmīpyārthatayā sāvakāśatvasambhavādityādyaḥ | sa enān‌ brahmetyatra brahmapadārthaḥ parabrahmaiva brahmaśabdasya tatra mukhyatvāt‌ | tasyaiva śravaṇādisādhanairevāparokṣīkṛtatvāccaturmukhe ca śravaṇādisādhanārjitapratipatteḥ caturmukhaṃ prā nuvānītīcchāyāścābhāvāditi dvitīyaḥ | ubhayasvīkārapakṣasya coktahetūnāṃ samīkaraṇāyogāt‌ yugapatprāptyayogaśceti | kramaprāptipakṣasya ca mānābhāvenāyogāditi | siddhāntastu caturmukha eva parabrahmaivetyanyavyāvṛttyā tattanmātrārthakatve viśeṣahetvabhāvenoktahetūnāṃ tattadarthagrahaṇamātrasyaivopapādakatvāt‌ ubhayamapi brahmaśabdārthatvena atra prāptiśrutau vivakṣitam‌ | yattu caturmukhagrahaṇe pratipattyabhisandhyabhāvarūpaṃ bādhakamuktaṃ yacca parabrahmagrahaṇena "gatyupapatteḥ' ityatra bandhasya santatatvarūpabādhakamuktaṃ tatrādyaṃ pratīkālambanānāṃ paraprāptyanaṃgīkāreṇa teṣāṃ pratipattyāderabhāvānna bādhakam‌ | dvitīyaṃ "apratīkāśrayā ye hi te yānti parameva tu' iti  pramāṇabalena amuktasthānagataparabrahmaprāptyaṃgīkāreṇa copapannam‌ | caturmukhapraptimatāṃ ca pratīkālambanānāṃ astyeva kārye pratipattyādīti teṣāmapi tanna bādhakamityadhikāribhedena pakṣadvayoktahetūnāṃ ubhayārthatvāṃgīkāre samīkartuṃ śakyatvāt‌ | "apratīkāśrayā ye hi te yānti parameva tu | svadehe brahmadṛṣyayaiva gacchedbrahma salokatām‌ ||' ityukteḥ yāgyatākrameṇaiva pralaye pūrvaṃ ca tayoḥ prāptiriti | phalaṃ tu gamyānirṇayanirṇayābhyāṃ gatyanusmṛtitatsamādhī || 6 ||
|| iti śrīmadrāghavendrayatikṛtāyāṃ nyāyamuktāvalyāṃ caturthādhyāyasya tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ ||
|| atha caturthaḥ pādaḥ ||
karmakṣayādi caturvidhaphale trividhamuktvā atrāvasaraprāpto bhoga ucyate |
|| oṃ sampadyāvihāya svena śabdāt‌ oṃ || 1 ||
"paraṃ jaiminiḥ' ityatra  brahmaprāptimattvena prakṛto muktaḥ kiṃ brahmātikramya bhogān‌ bhu ‌kte'thānatikramyeti cintā | tadarthaṃ "etaṃ setuṃ tīrtvāghassannanandho bhavati' iti śrutiratikramaparotānatikramapareti | anatikramārthakatve kalpakaṃ nāstyutāstīti | pūrvapakṣastu | "etaṃ setum‌' iti śruteranyārthaparatve kalpakābhāvāt‌ setupadoktabrahma tīrtvātikramyeti brahmātikramānantarameva svarūpāvirbhāvarūpamuktyuktyā brahmaprāptasyāmuktatvāvagamāt‌ muktasyaiva bhogabhoktṛtvāt‌ brahmātikramya muktaḥ san‌ bhogān‌ bhu ‌kte iti | siddhāntastu | "paraṃjyotirupasampadya' iti  śrutau brahmapāptasyaiva svarūpāvirbhāvarūpamuktatvasyokteḥ "imāṃ ghoramaśivāṃ nadīṃ tīrtvaitaṃ setumāpya' iti spaṣyaśrutyanurodhena etaṃ setuṃ gantuṃ anyattīrtvā ityeva tasyāḥ śruterarthasya vācyatvāt‌ "ātmānamabhisampadyaitenātmanā yathākāmaṃ sarvān‌ kāmānanubhavati' iti śruteśca jñānī brahma prāpya tadavihāyaiva bhogān‌ bhu ‌kte iti | phalaṃ tu brahmaṇaścaramaprāpyatvākṣepasamādhī || 1 ||
|| oṃ muktaḥ pratijñānāt‌ oṃ || 2 ||
pūrvākṣepeṇātra śaṃkanātsaṃgatiḥ | "paraṃjyotirupasampadya svena rūpeṇābhiniṣpadyate' "sa tatra paryeti' iti pūrvasūtropātte śrutiśeṣe sa ityukteḥ kimamukta uta mukta iti cintā | tadarthaṃ sa iti tacchabdaḥ kimavyavahitapūrvaparāmarśako'tha prākaraṇikaparāmarśaka iti | tadarthamavyavahitaparāmarśe paryetītyādinoktakriyāvattvaṃ bādhakamuta neti | pūrvapakṣastu | pūrvatra paraṃjyotisampannasyāvirbhūtasvarūpasya muktasyaiva "sa tatra paryeti' iti vākyasyeśānatikrameṇa bhoge mānatvamabhipretya sūtre svenetiśrutyuktirayuktā | svenetipūrvavākye nirguṇopāsakasya svarūpāvirbhāvokteḥ | sa tatretyatra punaḥ daharavidyopāsakasyāmuktasyaiveha kalpakatvāt‌ | na hi kriyāvattvaṃ kṛtakṛtyasya muktasyopapadyate | amuktasya tu anyadetādṛśabhogāyoge'pi brahmaprāptasya tādṛśabhogasambhavāt‌ | naca dvitīye "vihāropadeśāt‌' ityatra  muktasya sa tatra paryetīti vihārokterna kartṛtvaṃ kālpanikamityuktestadvirodha iti śaṃkyaṃ tasyāpīhākṣepāditi | siddhāntastu |
"aharaharenam‌' itibhāṣyokta  śrutau brahmaprāptānāṃ suptānāmamuktatayā divyabhogābhāvamuktvā muktasyaiva tatprāptyā tādṛśabhogokteḥ | kriyāvattvasya kṛtimattvasya mukte'pi siddhatvena tasya prāguktaparāmarśakatve'pi bādhakābhāvāt‌ sa iti tacchabdārtho mukta eveti | phalaṃ tu muktasya bhogākṣepasamādhiśceti || 2 ||
|| oṃ ātmā prakaraṇāt‌ oṃ || 3 ||
pūrvatra sveneti śrutiśeṣasthatacchabdārtha uktaḥ | atra pūrvavākyasthajyotiḥśabdārtha ucyata iti saṃgatiḥ | paraṃjyotirityuktajyotiḥśabdārthaḥ kimādityo'tha brahmeti cintā | tadarthaṃ "sa tejasi sūrye sampannaḥ' iti  samākhyā balavatyuta prakaraṇamiti | tadarthaṃ samākhyā niravakāśota sāvakāśeti | jyotiḥśabdasya "sūryamaganma jyotiḥ' ityādau āditya iva "jyotiścaraṇaḥ' ityādau  uktadiśā bhagavati prayoge'pi "sa tejasi sūrye sampannaḥ' iti  samākhyayā aikārthyalābhāt‌ | jyotiratra āditya eva na viṣṇuḥ prakaraṇasya ca vaiṣṇavatve'pi prasaṃgādanyoktisambhavāt‌ niravakāśasamākhyayā bādhācceti prāpte siddhāntaḥ | "ya ātmāpahatapāpmā' ityādinā  brahmāsādhāraṇaśrutiliṃgābhyāṃ brahmopakramya paraṃjyotirityadhītatvenāsya vākyasya brahmaprakaraṇasthatvāt‌ prakaraṇasya ca samākhyāto balavattvāt‌ samākhyāyāśca prārabdhakarmamokṣaparatvena brahmaprāptimārge antarādityaprāptiparatvena copapattyā sāvakāśatvāt‌ "paraṃjyotiḥ paraṃbrahma' iti  smṛtyā paraṃjyotiḥśabdasya paramātmanyeva mukhyatvācca brahmaiva paraṃjyotiḥśabdārthaḥ iti | phalaṃ tu muktasya brahmasamīpa eva bhoga iti prāguktasyākṣepasamādhiśceti || 3 ||
|| oṃ avibhāgena dṛṣyatvāt‌ oṃ || 4 ||
evaṃ tribhirnayairmuktau brahma prāpya bhogān‌ bhu ‌kte iti siddhe sati sa bhogaḥ kiṃrūpa iti viśeṣacintanātsaṃgatiḥ | mukteṣu sāyujyabhājāṃ bhogaḥ kiṃ bhagavadabhuktasyātha bhagavadbhuktaśeṣasyeti cintā | tadarthaṃ bhuktaśeṣayoge mukteḥ paramapumarthatvaṃ hīyata uta neti | pūrvapakṣastu | mukteḥ paramamumarthatvena bhagavadabhuktasyāpi bhogyasya saulabhyāt‌ bhagavadabhuktāneva bhu ‌kte | bhagavadbhuktaśeṣaniyame ca keṣāṃcitsaṃjātabhogecchānāṃ bhogābhāvaprasaṃgena mukteḥ pumathartvahāniḥ | īśvarasya nityapūrṇānandatvena kadācidabhogasyāpi saṃbhavāditi | siddhāntastu | mukteḥ pumarthatvena bhagavadabhuktabhogānāṃ sulabhatve'pi "yānevāhaṃ śruṇomi' iti  śrutau bhagavatā bhujyamānabhogabhoktṛtvaśravaṇānmuktānāṃ bhagavadbhujyamānabhogyabhoktṛtvamevopeyam‌ | nacaivaṃ bhagavadbhuktāśeṣabhoktṛtvasya yāneveti śrutau pratīteḥ bhagavatsāmyāpattiḥ śaṃkyā | "muktāḥ prāpya paraṃ viṣṇum‌' iti  viśeṣasmṛtyā "tāneva' iti sāmānyaśruteḥ saṃkocāt‌ | nacoktarītyā kadācidbhogābhāvaprasaktyā mukterapumarthatvaṃ śaṃkyam‌ | "pūrṇānandaḥ pūrṇabhuk‌ pūrṇakatarā' iti śrutau pūrṇānandatve'pi pūrṇabhogaśravaṇena tasyābhogāprasakteriti | phalaṃ tu "attā' ityādau  uktasya hareḥ sarvabhoktṛtvasyākṣepaḥ tatsamādhiśca || 4 ||
|| oṃ brāhmeṇa jaiminirupanyāsādibhyaḥ oṃ || 5 ||
|| oṃ citimātreṇa tadātmakatvādityauḍulomiḥ oṃ || oṃ evamapyupanyāsātpūrvabhāvādavirodhaṃ bādarāyaṇaḥ oṃ ||
pūrvākṣepeṇa śaṃkanātsaṃgatiḥ | prakṛto bhogaḥ kiṃ muktānāṃ na yuktaḥ uta yukta iti cintā | tadarthaṃ teṣāṃ bhogahetuśarīraṃ nāstyutāstīti | tathā bhautikadehābhāve'pi dehāntareṇa na yukta uta yukta iti | pūrvatrokto muktānāṃ brahmānatikrameṇa bhogo vā bhagavadbhuktabhogo vā na yuktaḥ | teṣāṃ saṃsārapāraṃgatatvena bhogahetudehābhāvāt‌ | tatsattve muktatvavyāghātāt‌ | brāhmadehādinā bhogastu na yuktaḥ | pralayādau tadbhāve'pi bhogābhāvāditi prāpte siddhāntaḥ | bhautikādisarvasvadehābhāve'pi "sa vā eṣa brahmaniṣṭhaḥ' iti  "ādatte harihastena' iti bhāṣyoktaśrutismṛtibhyāṃ paramātmānaṃ praviṣyasya taddehenaiva dehivadbhogāvagamāt‌ | "gacchāmi viṣṇupādābhyām‌' iti  smṛtyuktyā viṣṇvavayavaireva gamanādi karomīti
pūrvasmaraṇabalena muktau taddehaprāptimattvayuktyā ca brāhmadehena vā "sa vā eṣa etasmānmartyādvimuktaścinmātrī bhavati' ityādi  śrutyā "martyadehaṃ parityajya' ityādi  smṛtyā bhagavadanugṛhītaiḥ svarūpabhūtacinmātradehāvayavairvā muktaḥ bhogānbhuṃjate karmāṇi ca kurvanti ceti | phalaṃ tu mukteḥ pumarthatvākṣepasamādhī || 5 ||
|| oṃ saṃkalpādeva ca tacchateḥ oṃ || 6 ||
pūrvatra bhāge'laukikopakaraṇānapekṣoktā | atra tu laukikasādhanāpekṣā netyucyata iti saṃgatiḥ | prakṛtamuktabhogaḥ kiṃ tattadupāyasādhyasādhanasādhyaḥ uta saṃkalpamātrasādhya iti cintā | tadarthaṃ bhagavadokasthatvayuktiḥ lokānusārasahāyā satī sādhanasādhyatvasādhikā bhavatyuta neti cintā | bhagavadokastho muktabhogastattadupāyasādhyasādhanasādhyaḥ okasthatvāt‌ bhūmyādyokasthabhogavaditi yuktyā loke bhogasya tattadupāyasādhyasādhanasādhyatvadṛṣyayā lokānusārācca muktabhogo'pi tādṛśa eveti prāpte siddhāntaḥ | "sa yadi pitṛlokakāmo bhavati saṃkalpādevāsya pitaraḥ samuttiṣṭhanti' iti  śruteḥ saṃkalpamātrasādhyo muktabhogo na tattadupāyasādhyasādhanasādhyaḥ | prāguktayuktistu śrutibādhitā satī na sādhikā | lokavirodhastu nirdoṣabhagadokasi guṇa eveti | phalaṃ tu saṃsārasamānadharmatāpattyā mukteḥ pumarthatvākṣepastatsamādhiśceti |
|| oṃ ata eva cānanyādhipatiḥ oṃ || 7 ||
atra prāguktahetvatideśādantarasaṃgatiḥ | prakṛto muktaḥ kiṃ kl‌ptapatibhyo'nyapatiniyataḥ atha kl‌ptapatiniyata eveti cintā | tadarthaṃ muktāvanapekṣitamapi bhavatyuta neti | loke rājagṛhapraviṣyasya anapekṣitasvāvarapratihārādiniyatatvamiva muktasyāpi paragṛhagatatvāt‌ svāvaraniyatatvaṃ syādanyathā lokavirodhāditi prāpte siddhāntaḥ | loke satyasaṃkalpatvābhāvena anapekṣitasvāvaraniyatatvasaṃbhave'pi muktasya satyasaṃkalpatvādeva nānapekṣitaprāptiryukteti kl‌ptapatiniyatā eva muktāḥ | nahi muktānāmavaraniyatatvamapekṣitam‌ | tathāca smṛtiḥ "paramodhipatisteṣām‌' ityādiketi | phalaṃ tu muktasya saṃsārasamānadharmavattvatadabhāvau || 7 ||
|| oṃ abhāvaṃ bādarirāha hyevaṃ oṃ || 8 ||
|| oṃ bhāvaṃ jaimininirvikalpā nānāt‌ oṃ || oṃ dvādaśāhavadubhayavidhaṃ bādarāyaṇo'taḥ oṃ || oṃ tanvabhāve sandhyavadupapatteḥ oṃ || oṃ bhāve jāgradvat‌ oṃ || oṃ pradīpavadāveśastathā hi darśayati oṃ || oṃ svāpyayasampattyoranyatarāpekṣamāviṣkṛtaṃ hi oṃ ||
saṃkalpādevetyatrokatasaṃkalpamātrādhīnabhogākṣepeṇātra śaṃkanātsaṃgatiḥ | "atra eva' ityasya  prāguktahetvatideśakatayā prāsaṃgikatvenāvyavadhāyakatvāt‌ prakṛto muktānāṃ bhogaḥ na yujyate uta yujyate iti cintā | tadarthaṃ dehabhāvābhāvayoḥ bhoga upapādayitumaśakya uta śakya iti | tathā muktānāṃ dehabhāvābhāvau virodhādayuktāvuta yuktāviti | pūrvapakṣastu | muktānāṃ dehasya bhāve duḥkhādiprasaṃgena nirduḥkhabhogo'nupapādanaḥ | abhāve ca bhogāsambhavaḥ | naca brāhmeṇetyatroktadiśā brāhmadehena bhogo yukta iti vācyam‌ | sāyujyabhājāṃ tatsaṃbhave'pi anyeṣāṃ tadabhāvāt‌ | naca ciccharīreṇa teṣāṃ bhogopapattiḥ | suptyādau sato'pi tasya bhogāyatanatvādarśanāt‌ | ato dehabhāvenābhāvena vā bhogasya durupapādanatvānna yujyate bhogaḥ | virodhādayuktau ca dehabhāvābhāvapakṣāviti | siddhāntastu | yujyate muktānāṃ bhogaḥ | pakṣadvaye'pi bhogasya sūpapādanatvāt‌ | bāhyadehabhāve tasya jaḍatve'pi śuddhasattvātmakatejorūpatayā duḥkhādyanāpādakatvena tatra sukhānubhavamātrasyopapatteḥ | pradīpo yathā tailādyeva bhuṃkte na tu tadgatakārṣṇyādi tadvat‌ | "tīrṇo hi tadā sarvān‌ śokān‌' iti  śruteḥ | dehābhāvapakṣe'pi yathā sva nāvasthāyāṃ bāhyadehābhimānābhāve'pi bhogaḥ tathā muktau bāhyadehābhāve'pi ciccharīreṇa bhogopapatteḥ anabhimānasyābhāvasāmyāt‌ | suptau manasa uparatatvādabhogaḥ dehabhāvapakṣau ca "cintā vācitā' veti
 "aśarīro vāva tadā bhavati' iti ca  śrutisiddhatvāt‌ | dvādaśāhasya yajamānecchayā ekānekakartṛkatvābhyāṃ ahīnatvasatratvavat‌ muktānāṃ svecchayā tejorūpabāhyadehabhāvābhāvayoravirodhācca yuktāviti | tathāca smṛtiḥ "svecchayā vā śarīrāṇi tejorūpāṇi kānicit‌ | svīkṛtya jāgaritavadbhuktvā tyāgaḥ kadācaneti | phalaṃ tu nirduḥkhabhogābhāvānmukteḥ pumarthatvākṣepastatsamādhiśceti || 9 ||
|| oṃ jagadvayāpāravarjam‌ oṃ || 9 ||
|| oṃ prakaraṇādasannihitatvācca oṃ || oṃ pratyakṣopadeśāditi cennādhikārikamaṇḍalasthokteḥ oṃ || oṃ vikāravarti ca tathā hi darśayati oṃ ||
evamanupapattinirāsena muktānāṃ bhoge siddhe'tra maryādocyate iti saṃgatiḥ | muktāḥ kiṃ jagadvayāpārān‌ kurvanti na veti cintā | tadarthaṃ "sarvān‌ kāmānāptveti śrutau  sarvaśabdo'saṃkucitavṛttiruta saṃkucitavṛttiriti | tadatharṃ tatsaṃsocakaṃ nāstyutāstīti | tadarthaṃ "sarve'smai devā viriṃcaviṣayā veti | sarvesmai devā iti śruterjīvamātraviṣayatvāt‌ sarvān‌ kāmānitiśruteḥ "śarīrabhedādūrdhvaḥ utkramya'  ityādijīvaprakaraṇagatatve'pi jīvānāṃ tādṛśaśaktikalpanena vā śaktyabhāve'pi satyakāmatvādeva sarvaiśvayarsambhavena sarvavākyasaṃkocakābhāvāt‌ kurvantyeva muktāḥ jagatsṛṣyayādivyāpāramiti prāpte siddhāntaḥ | "māhācamasya' ityupakrameṇa "sarve'smai' iti śruteḥ viriṃcaviṣayatayā sarvamuktaviṣayatvābhāvena jīvānāṃ sṛṣyayādau śaktikalpanābhāvena tatra sāmarthyābhāvāt‌ satyakāmatvasya "svādhikānandasamprāptau sṛṣyayādivyāpṛtiṣvapi | muktānāṃ naiva kāmaḥ syāt‌' iti  smṛtyā sṛṣyayādyanyakāmānavattvenopapatteḥ | jagadvayāpāravarjamanyān‌ kāmān‌ bhuṃkte mukta iti | atra muktaviriṃcasyāśeṣamuktaniyamanavyāpāro'nyadevānāṃ svāvaramuktaniyamanavyāpāraścāstīti dhyeyam‌ | phalaṃ tu lakṣaṇasūtrākṣepasamādhī || 9 ||
|| oṃ sthitimāha darśayataścaivaṃ pratyakṣānumāne oṃ || 10 ||
|| oṃ bhogamātrasāmyaliṃgācca oṃ ||
pūrvatra kāmapadoktaviṣayabhogeyattoktā atra tu tadabhivyaṃgyasvarūpasukhasya yathāsthitirūpeyattocyata iti saṃgatiḥ | muktaḥ kimānandavṛddhihrāsavān‌ uta yathāsthitimāniti cintā | tadarthaṃ teṣāmupāsanādikaṃ kiṃ vṛddhayādiheturbhavatyuta neti | pūrvapakṣastu | "etatsāma gāyannāste'  ityādau muktānāmupāsanāśravaṇāt‌ tasya ca vaiyarthyānyathānupapattyā phalatvāvaśyaṃbhāvāt‌ muktānāṃ vaśitvarūpaiśvaryabalena kāmitāśeṣabhogaviśeṣasattvācca vṛddhimān‌ ānandastaduparame ca hrāsavāniti | siddhāntastu | ekaprakāreṇaiva sthitimān‌ muktānandaḥ | "sa eṣaḥ' iti bhāṣyokta  śrutau vṛddhayādyabhāvamuktvā "darśannevātmānam‌' ityupāsanāvaco'pi āpattivipattiśabditavṛddhihrāsaniṣedhāt‌ mahatāṃ pratyakṣeṇa mukto na vṛddhayādimān‌ kāraṇaśūnyatvādityanumānena ca vṛddhayādyabhāvasya siddhatvāt‌ | upāsanayāśca "harerupāsanā cātra sadaiva sukharūpiṇi' iti  smṛtyā svayaṃ phalarūpatvena phalāntarasādhanatvābhāvāt‌ | vaśitvena kāmitāśeṣabhogaprāptāvapi ayogye kāmanāvirahāt‌ yogyasya ca prāptatvāt‌ bhogaviśeṣe'pi taduparamādapi na sukhavṛddhihrāsahetutayāpi sa bhogaviśeṣaḥ "avṛddhihrāsarūpatvaṃ muktānāṃ prāyikaṃ bhavet‌ | kādācitkaviśeṣasya naiva teṣāṃ niṣiddhayate' iti  uktadiśā svarūpasukhasya vaicityahetutayā vopapanna iti | phalaṃ tu saṃsārasamānadharmatāpattyā mukterapumarthatvākṣepasamādhī || 10 ||
|| oṃ anāvṛttiśśabdādanāvṛttiśśabdāt‌ oṃ || 11 ||
pūrvatra muktānandasya vṛddhayādi netyuktam‌ | atra tu tadanubhavasyānto nāstītyucyata iti saṃgatiḥ | prakṛto muktaḥ kiṃ
janmamṛtyādiprāptirūpapunarāvṛttimānuta neti cintā | tadarthaṃ muktasya samastakālavāsitvaṃ punarāvṛttāvabādhakamiti | tadarthaṃ samastasyāpi kālasya samāptirastyuta neti | pūrvapakṣastu | svargādisthasyeva muktasyāpyasti punarāvṛttiḥ | muktasya samastakālavāsitve'pi samastasyāpi kālasya kadācitsamāptisambhavāt‌ | naca kālasyānantyāt‌ samāpterasambhavaḥ | bhāratayuddhe "caturdaśīṃ paṃcadarśīṃ bhūtapūrvāṃ tu ṣoḍaśīm‌ | imāṃ tu nābhijānāmi amāvāsyāṃ trayodaśī' iti  trayodaśyāḥ asambhāvitapañcadaśītvadarśanāditi | siddhāntastu | kālasya samastasyāpi samāpteḥ vyāhatatvena anantakālāvasāyitvāt‌ svagirṇo manvantarādyavasāyitayā punarāvṛttāvapi na muktasya punarāvṛttiḥ "naca punarāvartate' iti śruteśca | "sarvān‌ kāmānā noti' iti śrutau sarvakāmāpteḥ śravaṇena śrutāvapunarāvṛtteḥ kāmitatvācca na punarāvṛttiriti nityanirduḥkhānandānubhavavanta eva muktā iti | phalaṃ tu mukteḥ pumarthatvākṣepasamādhī || 11 ||
kalyāṇaguṇapūrṇāya doṣadūrāya viṣṇave |
namaḥ śrīprāṇanāthāya bhaktābhīṣyapradāyine ||
|| iti śrīmatparamahaṃsaparivrājakācārya śrīmatsudhīndragurupādaśiṣyarāghavendrayatikṛtāyāṃ nyāyamuktāvalyāṃ caturthādhyāyasya caturthaḥ pādaḥ ||
